### [74] [DriveMA: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models with verifiable Meta-Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31271)
*Weicheng Zheng,Yixin Huang,Qiao Sun,Derun Li,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveMA提出了一种基于可验证元动作的驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型框架，通过将未来自车运动总结为语言域意图，并结合轨迹标注管道、规则投影验证、动作监督训练以及回合级信用分配强化学习，显著提升了高层决策与低层轨迹规划的语义对齐，在Waymo数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶VLA模型存在语言与动作之间的语义鸿沟（language-action gap），使得语言难以有效指导端到端规划，亟需一种可验证的接口来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出元动作（meta-actions）作为语言域意图表示，通过轨迹标注管道从专家轨迹构建，利用规则投影进行可验证性检查；设计动作监督训练和基于回合级信用分配的强化学习框架，实现高层决策与低层轨迹规划的显式对齐。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset视觉端到端驾驶任务上，2B模型Rater Feedback Score达8.060，4B模型提升至8.079，均达到SOTA；在NAVSIM闭环规划上也取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 即使简单的元动作接口，只要具备可验证性并针对语言-动作对齐进行优化，就能实现最优的驾驶规划性能。

Abstract: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models (Driving VLAs) aim to use language to improve end-to-end planning, but the language-action gap limits this promise. We propose DriveMA, a Driving VLA framework built on verifiable meta-actions, which summarize future ego motion into compact language-domain intentions and can be constructed from expert trajectories with a trajectory-grounded annotation pipeline and can be verified against generated trajectories through rule-based projection. DriveMA exploits this verifiability with action-centric supervised training and a data-efficient turn-level credit assignment reinforcement learning framework, explicitly aligning high-level decisions with low-level trajectory planning through dense rewards and precise credit assignment. DriveMA sets a new state of the art on the Waymo Open Dataset Vision-based E2E Driving, achieving a Rater Feedback Score of 8.060 with a 2B model and further improving it to 8.079 with a 4B model; it also obtains competitive closed-loop planning performance on NAVSIM. These results show that even a simple meta-action interface can achieve state-of-the-art planning when made verifiable and optimized for language-action alignment. Code, data, and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [75] [SAM for Robust Mitochondria Instance Segmentation in Fluorescence Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31284)
*Suyog Jadhav,Dilip K. Prasad,Krishna Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过合成荧光显微镜数据微调SAM模型，解决了线粒体实例分割中标注数据稀缺的问题，在真实数据上取得了优于基线模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光显微镜下线粒体形态分析对理解细胞健康至关重要，但现有基础模型（如SAM）因域偏移（衍射限制、低对比度、重叠结构）难以直接应用，且高质量人工标注的实例分割数据集极度匮乏。

Method: 通过模拟真实线粒体数据并仿真荧光显微镜光学特性，生成大规模合成标注数据集，对SAM模型进行微调。

Result: 在真实人工标注的FM图像上，合成数据微调后的模型在精度和平均Dice分数上均优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了仿真辅助训练在荧光显微镜实例分割中的巨大潜力，为数据稀缺问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The morphological analysis of mitochondria in fluorescence microscopy (FM) is crucial for understanding cellular health, energy production, and metabolic regulation. While foundation models like the Segment Anything Model (SAM) have revolutionized natural image segmentation, their direct application to FM is hindered by a significant domain shift characterized by diffraction-limited resolution, low contrast, and complex overlapping organelle networks. Furthermore, the development of robust models is bottlenecked by a severe lack of high-quality, manually annotated instance segmentation datasets for mitochondria. In this paper, we propose a scalable solution to this data scarcity by finetuning SAM exclusively on synthetically generated FM data. We simulate realistic mitochondria data and emulate the optical properties of fluorescence microscopes to create a large-scale annotated dataset. We evaluate our fine-tuned model on a curated dataset of real, manually annotated FM images. Qualitative and quantitative analyses demonstrate that our synthetically fine-tuned model improves precision and average dice score over strong baselines. This work establishes the potential of simulation-assisted training for FM instance segmentation.

</details>


### [76] [Authentication of Copy Detection Patterns via Cross-Camera Dual-Synthetic Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31292)
*Ivan Oleksiyuk,Roman Chaban,Slava Voloshynovskiy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注册的跨相机双合成参考框架，利用深度学习将数字模板与注册图像结合生成高质量参考图像，显著提升CDP鉴伪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在验证相机域合成参考图像时忽略了打印过程中的随机性，导致防伪鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出 enrolment-based cross-camera dual-synthetic referencing 框架：先通过受控注册相机捕获每个打印的CDP，再使用深度学习翻译器联合利用数字模板和注册图像生成验证图像的高质量参考。

Result: 在异构移动相机上实现了改进的鉴伪性能，对基于机器学习的复制攻击具有鲁棒性，并能从小区域CDP和低端设备上实现可靠验证。

Conclusion: 双参考比基于模板的参考信息量更大（信息论证明），该框架有效提升了CDP鉴伪的实用性与安全性。

Abstract: Copy Detection Patterns (CDPs) are structures printed on physical objects to enable cost-effective authentication. Verification is achieved by comparing a captured image with the digital template from which the CDP was printed. In practice, printer stochasticity and camera distortions hinder this comparison, limiting robustness against counterfeiting. Prior work addressed camera effects by synthesising reference images in the verification camera domain, but it ignored printing variability. We introduce an enrolment-based cross-camera dual-synthetic referencing framework. Each printed CDP is first captured by a controlled enrolment camera, and a deep-learning-based translator jointly exploits the digital template and the enrolled capture to generate a high-quality reference for the verification image. We provide an information-theoretic justification showing that the dual reference is more informative than template-based references. Experiments on heterogeneous mobile cameras demonstrate improved authentication performance, robustness to machine-learning-based copy attacks, and reliable verification from small CDP regions and on low-end devices.

</details>


### [77] [TokTalk: Expressive Real-time Facial Animation from Audio-LLM Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31294)
*Qingcheng Zhao,Yifang Pan,Karan Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokTalk是一个直接从音频令牌流式输出3D面部动画的系统，利用Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching模型在音频令牌与面部运动之间建立映射，实现低延迟、高质量且富有表现力的实时面部动画生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式虚拟角色在面部表情和对话流畅度上仍显机械，原因在于传统流水线（语音识别→文本生成→语音合成→面部动画）存在多个串行阶段，导致延迟和表现力不足。作者观察到Audio-LLM产生的音频令牌已包含足够信息来重建面部表现。

Method: 1) 构建了音频令牌到3D面部运动的数据集；2) 使用Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching模型进行训练；3) 采用轻量级适配策略，可无缝连接任意基于令牌的Audio-LLM；4) 通过分块处理实现延迟与面部质量之间的参数化权衡。

Result: 实时性能与先前方案在延迟上相当，在质量、表现力和3D面部控制方面显著优于对比方法（通过感知研究验证）。消融实验证实了分块处理在延迟与质量权衡上的有效性。

Conclusion: TokTalk展示了在多种音视频面部应用中的灵活性，包括聊天机器人虚拟角色、语音驱动的用户虚拟角色以及动画导演界面，为端到端的面部动画生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Audio-LLMs like GPT-4o have ushered in an era of conversational interaction with language models. Conversational avatars however, still seem robotic in facial expression and conversational flow, in part due to sequential stages of speech recognition, text generation, turn-based text response, speech synthesis, and audio driven facial animation. Based on our insight that audio-tokens produced by current Audio-LLMs carry sufficient information to reconstruct a plausible facial performance, we present TokTalk, a system that directly outputs expressive facial animation in real-time from streaming audio-tokens. We construct a novel audio-token to 3D facial motion dataset, on which TokTalk is trained using a Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching model. A lightweight adaptation strategy allows our trained model to seamlessly connect to any token-based Audio-LLM at minimal computational overhead. Our chunk-based processing further enables parametric trade-off between latency and facial quality, shown through ablation studies. We further show that the real-time performance of TokTalk is comparable in latency to prior art solutions, and significantly favorable (via a perceptual study) in terms of quality, expressivity and control of the 3D facial performance. We showcase TokTalk's flexibility using a chatbot Avatar, a voice-driven user Avatar, and an animation Director's interface, as diverse audio-visual face applications.

</details>


### [78] [Learning from Fine-Grained Visual Discrepancies: Mitigating Multimodal Hallucinations via In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31312)
*Haolin Deng,Xin Zou,Zhiwei Jin,Chen Chen,Haonan Lu,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IC-VCO的多模态幻觉缓解方法，通过上下文视觉对比优化和视觉对比蒸馏，结合对比样本编辑策略，在五个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉偏好DPO方法存在目标函数理论不一致（分区函数不匹配）以及依赖粗粒度负样本导致捷径学习的问题，多模态幻觉难以有效缓解。

Method: 1) IC-VCO：将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中，实现数学上严谨的目标函数；2) VCDist：辅助可靠性门控正则化器，促进多图像对比训练与单图像推理之间的一致性；3) 对比样本编辑策略：通过精确语义扰动生成硬负样本。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，IC-VCO取得了最佳整体性能，样本编辑策略也证明了有效性。

Conclusion: IC-VCO通过理论一致的目标函数和硬负样本生成策略，有效缓解了多模态幻觉问题。

Abstract: Multimodal hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Standard textual Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often fails to mitigate it due to a lack of explicit visual supervision. While existing works introduce visual preference DPO by contrasting original images against negative ones, they suffer from a theoretically inconsistent objective caused by partition function mismatches and rely on coarse-grained negatives that could enable shortcut learning. In this work, we propose In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO). By placing contrastive images within a shared multi-image context, IC-VCO ensures a mathematically rigorous objective. We further introduce Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist), an auxiliary reliability-gated regularizer that encourages consistency between multi-image contrastive training and single-image inference. Finally, we propose a contrastive sample editing strategy that generates hard negatives via precise semantic perturbations. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate IC-VCO's best overall performance and the effectiveness of our sample editing strategy. Code and data are available at https://github.com/OPPO-Mente-Lab/IC-VCO.

</details>


### [79] [DecMem: Towards Minute-Long Consistent World Generation with Decoupled Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31336)
*Zhenhao Yang,Xiaoshi Wu,Zhengyao Lv,Xiaoyu Shi,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Kwan-Yee K. Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DecMem，一种解耦记忆架构，通过稀疏全局记忆和锚定局部记忆实现分钟级可控长视频生成，在细粒度时空一致性上显著超越当前SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在长程推理下难以维持细粒度时空一致性，而显式3D记忆和粗粒度隐式建模存在局限。

Method: 提出DecMem解耦记忆架构，包含Sparse Global Memory（稀疏全局记忆）实现高效细粒度全局历史访问，以及Anchored Local Memory（锚定局部记忆）实现稳定高质量外推。

Result: DecMem在长程外推任务上显著超越当前SOTA方法，实现了分钟级可控长视频生成，具有高保真度和一致性。

Conclusion: 通过解决朴素可学习记忆架构中的计算低效和注意力分散问题，DecMem为可控世界模型提供了可扩展的长期记忆解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have promoted rapid progress in controllable world models. However, maintaining fine-grained spatio-temporal consistency under long-horizon reasoning remains a key challenge. In this work, we move beyond explicit 3D memory and coarse frame-level implicit modeling, and propose a fine-grained, learnable, and scalable memory for consistent world generation. We first identify two fundamental limitations of naïve learnable memory architectures in long-horizon extrapolation, namely computational inefficiency and attention dispersion. Through a systematic analysis of attention dispersion, we propose DecMem, a decoupled memory architecture that employs Sparse Global Memory for efficient fine-grained access to global history and Anchored Local Memory for stable and high-quality extrapolation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecMem significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. By ensuring precise and efficient long-term memory and achieving superior extrapolation capabilities, DecMem enables minute-level controllable long video generation with high fidelity and consistency.

</details>


### [80] [Triangle Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31419)
*Nicholas Fry,Eric Dexheimer,Kirill Mazur,Paul H. J. Kelly,Andrew J. Davison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个使用可微分三角形（Triangle Soup）作为3D地图表示的密集RGB-D SLAM系统，通过在线可微分渲染进行跟踪与建图，并支持实时网格生成与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅在新视图合成方面表现出色，但三角形仍是传统渲染管线、游戏引擎和下游任务（如仿真、碰撞检测、编辑）的标准图元。作者希望将三角形图元引入SLAM系统，同时实现高质量跟踪建图与显式几何能力。

Method: 利用可微分三角形汤（Delaunay三角剖分优化得到），通过在线可微分渲染进行跟踪与建图；地图可随时通过受限Delaunay三角剖分转换为连通网格，支持网格变形和碰撞检测。

Result: 在Replica和TUM-RGBD数据集上，该方法在3D几何质量上优于基线，相机跟踪精度与现有方法持平，并实现了在线网格场景编辑。

Conclusion: 可微分三角形是SLAM系统中兼顾渲染质量与显式几何表示的理想选择，为在线网格编辑、碰撞检测等应用提供了新可能。

Abstract: We present a dense RGB-D SLAM system using differentiable triangles as the 3D map representation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as the leading method for novel-view synthesis, triangles remain the standard primitive for traditional rendering hardware, game engines, and downstream tasks requiring explicit geometry such as simulation, collision, and editing. Recent offline methods have demonstrated that an unstructured 'triangle soup' can be optimised into a photorealistic mesh via Delaunay triangulation across a set of posed images. Building upon this insight, we present the first dense SLAM system to employ Triangle Splatting to perform both tracking and mapping through online differentiable rendering of a triangle soup. The map can be converted into a connected mesh on-the-fly via restricted Delaunay triangulation, enabling new online capabilities such as mesh deformation and collision checking. On Replica and TUM-RGBD, our system outperforms baselines on 3D geometry, matches the camera-tracking accuracy, and enables online mesh-based scene editing.

</details>


### [81] [YARD: Y-Architecture Register Decoding for Efficient Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31429)
*Ting Chen,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Yu Hu,Guan Huang,Mai Chen,Langsheng Lei,Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YARD是一种无需训练的Y型架构寄存器解码框架，通过在中间层分支并用寄存器标记替代局部视觉标记，有效缓解LVLM中的幻觉问题，同时大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比解码方法中，完全丢弃视觉标记过于极端会引发语言幻觉，而破坏输入图像则控制粗粒度且推理延迟高。

Method: 构建Y型架构，共享浅层计算后在中间解码层分支；退化分支用寄存器标记替代局部视觉标记，保留全局语义但缺乏细粒度局部证据。

Result: 在生成式和判别式幻觉基准上，YARD在多个LVLM上均达到最先进的幻觉缓解效果，同时显著降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: YARD通过巧妙的架构设计，在无需训练的情况下实现了高效且有效的幻觉缓解。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) seeks to mitigate hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of a standard model and a visually degraded model. However, existing training-free CD methods suffer from sub-optimal degraded branches: completely dropping visual tokens is too extreme and induces language hallucinations, while corrupting input images offers coarse control over visual evidence and suffers from high inference latency due to requiring two full forward passes. To address these dilemmas, we propose YARD, a training-free Y-Architecture Register Decoding framework. Motivated by the observation that reliable text-to-vision grounding predominantly emerges in the middle decoder layers, YARD constructs the degraded branch internally by sharing shallow-layer computations and branching exactly at this critical stage. For the degraded branch, YARD replaces patch-level visual tokens with register tokens, which preserve global image semantics but lack fine-grained local evidence. This image-aware yet locally under-grounded design provides a faithful contrastive signal without extreme modality mismatch, while the Y-architecture strictly avoids a costly second forward pass. Extensive experiments on generative and discriminative hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that YARD consistently achieves state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation across multiple LVLMs, alongside a significant reduction in inference latency.

</details>


### [82] [Astra: a generalizable report generation foundation model for 3D computed tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31437)
*Zhuhao Wang,Fang Chen,Chaohui Yu,Zihan Li,Yuchao Zheng,Jing Wang,Xuan Yang,Jia Guo,Zhenlu Yang,Xingju Zheng,Yihua Sun,Haojie Han,Xiaoxiao Qin,Zhan Feng,Wenbo Xiao,Chao Zhu,Yuehua Li,Shipeng Zhang,Hao Luo,Yunsong Peng,Fan Wang,Hongen Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Astra，一种基于90,678对胸腹部CT-报告训练的通用CT报告生成基础模型。通过报告风格统一和强化学习诊断一致性优化，Astra在多个外部队列中实现了SOTA性能（细粒度诊断指标平均提升44.1%），并将临床报告起草速度提升29.6%，报告完整性提升11.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT报告解读耗时且高度依赖专家经验，现有方法缺乏支持多区域报告且对外部真实世界队列鲁棒的通用生成模型；不同队列间报告风格和诊断术语的不一致性导致联合训练易受噪声文本监督影响，限制了模型泛化能力。

Method: 1) 构建包含90,678对胸腹部CT-报告的大规模数据集CTRgDB（涵盖353,671个异常、8个器官系统）；2) 通过报告风格统一（harmonization）消除跨队列不一致性；3) 采用强化学习进一步优化诊断一致性。

Result: 在CTRgDB及6个外部队列上达到SOTA，细粒度诊断指标平均提升44.1%（P<0.001）；临床应用中胸部报告起草速度提升29.6%，腹部报告完整性提升11.3%（P<0.001）。

Conclusion: Astra可作为广泛可及的临床辅助工具，并作为CT AI开发的基础设施，通过高质量报告合成提升下游诊断性能和视觉-语言预训练效果。

Abstract: CT interpretation requires radiologists to review hundreds of volumetric slices per examination, making reporting time-consuming and highly expertise-dependent. Automated CT report generation offers a promising route to improving clinical efficiency, yet the field still lacks a generalizable CT report generation foundation model that supports multi-region reporting and remains robust across external real-world cohorts. Intrinsic inconsistencies in reporting style and diagnostic terminology across cohorts make naive joint training prone to noisy textual supervision, thereby limiting model generalizability. Here we present Astra, a generalizable CT report generation foundation model trained on 90,678 thoracoabdominal CT-report pairs (CTRgDB) with 353,671 abnormalities spanning eight organ systems. By harmonizing report style and further refining diagnostic consistency via reinforcement learning, Astra achieves style-consistent and diagnostically accurate report generation across diverse anatomical regions and institutions. Evaluating on CTRgDB and six external cohorts, Astra achieves state-of-the-art performance with a 44.1% average improvement in fine-grained diagnostic metrics (P<0.001). In real-world clinical workflows, Astra assistance accelerates chest report drafting by 29.6% and improves abdominal report completeness by 11.3% (P<0.001). Furthermore, Astra also demonstrates broad utility as a foundation for CT AI development, improving downstream diagnostic performance and scaling vision-language pretrain through high-quality report synthesis. Overall, Astra serves as a broadly accessible clinical assistant and a pivotal infrastructure for the next generation of AI-powered healthcare.

</details>


### [83] [VisionPulse: Dynamic Visual Sparsity for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31457)
*Hengbo Xu,Shengjie Jin,Yanbiao Ma,Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisionPulse框架，在推理过程中按步骤动态剪枝视觉token，仅保留5%的关键token，推理轨迹缩短11.2%，精度几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在预填充阶段静态剪枝视觉token，但视觉证据在推理过程中是逐步依赖的，冗余的视觉上下文会引导模型关注无关区域，延长推理轨迹。

Method: 计算轻量级的视觉注意力质量来估计每步的保留预算，利用其与有效视觉token使用的正相关性，仅保留最关键token，实现逐步视觉token剪枝。

Result: 每步仅保留5%的视觉token，推理轨迹缩短11.2%，精度几乎保持不变。

Conclusion: VisionPulse通过动态剪枝冗余视觉token，在不损失精度的情况下显著降低推理开销并缩短推理轨迹。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large multimodal models (LMMs), inference-time overhead has become a key bottleneck for real-world deployment. Existing methods typically prune visual tokens at prefill, assuming the required visual evidence remains static during reasoning. However, we empirically show that visual evidence is strongly step-dependent: only a sparse subset of visual tokens is critical at each decoding step, and the critical set evolves across reasoning. Furthermore, we identify a coupled bottleneck where redundant visual context can steer the model toward query-irrelevant regions, lengthening the reasoning trace. Guided by these insights, we propose VisionPulse, a step-wise visual token pruning framework during reasoning. VisionPulse computes a lightweight visual attention mass to estimate the step-wise retention budget by exploiting its strong positive correlation with LMMs' effective visual token usage and retain only the most critical tokens under this budget. By enforcing visual sparsity during reasoning, VisionPulse filters redundant visual context while preserving relevant visual evidence, shortening reasoning traces naturally. Extensive experiments show that VisionPulse only retains 5% of visual tokens per step with reasoning traces shortened by 11.2%, while keeping accuracy almost unchanged.

</details>


### [84] [VolFill: Single-View Amodal 3D Scene Reconstruction with Volumetric Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31466)
*Tuan Duc Ngo,Chuang Gan,Evangelos Kalogerakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VolFill是一个生成式框架，从单张RGB图像预测完整3D场景结构，利用混合3D VAE压缩稀疏TSDF网格，结合潜空间扩散Transformer去噪重建完整场景，在SCRREAM和NRGB-D数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张RGB图像重建完整场景几何仍然困难，尤其是推断视觉证据不完整的隐藏结构，现有方法受限于逐像素对齐回归、逐光线约束或非结构化点云查询，缺乏对完整场景结构的鲁棒推理能力。

Method: 提出VolFill框架：1) 混合3D VAE将稀疏截断无符号距离函数(TSDF)网格压缩到紧凑潜空间；2) 潜空间扩散Transformer去噪恢复完整场景；3) 以几何基础模型为条件，利用丰富空间先验进行鲁棒推理；4) 提供结构化表示支持直接表面提取和大规模占用查询。

Result: 在SCRREAM和NRGB-D数据集上的大量实验表明，VolFill显著优于当前基线方法。

Conclusion: VolFill为整体空间理解提供了鲁棒基础，克服了传统像素对齐回归的局限性，是一种有效的生成式场景补全方法。

Abstract: Reconstructing the complete geometry of a scene from a single RGB image remains challenging - especially when inferring hidden structures where visual evidence is incomplete. We introduce VolFill, a generative framework that predicts the 3D structure of the complete scene rather than relying on traditional pixel-aligned regression. Our method utilizes a hybrid 3D VAE to compress sparse truncated unsigned distance function grids into a compact latent space, paired with a latent Diffusion Transformer that denoises this representation to recover the complete scene. We condition the generation on geometry foundation models, leveraging rich spatial priors for robust reasoning. Unlike existing methods limited by per-ray constraints or unstructured point-cloud queries, VolFill provides a structured representation that supports direct surface extraction and occupancy queries at scale. Extensive experiments on the SCRREAM and NRGB-D datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms current baselines, providing a robust foundation for holistic spatial understanding.

</details>


### [85] [Enhancing Computer Vision Model Generalization in Warehouse Facilities: A Case Study on Anomaly Detection in Vertical Material Handling Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31487)
*Ruiliang Liu,Tina Dongxu Li,Joshua Migdal,Ken Meszaros,Trevor Dardik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创新方法，仅在实验室环境中完成标准流程（相机安装、图像采集、标注、训练），通过优化相机位置、图像触发策略、模型选择与集成，实现对不同仓库环境的有效泛化，从而简化部署流程，省去重复标注和模型再训练的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仓库计算机视觉模型部署需要针对每个新环境重复进行相机安装、图像采集、标注、训练和部署，资源消耗巨大且流程繁琐，亟需一种简化且可泛化的方法。

Method: 仅在实验室环境中执行标准流程，结合最优相机放置、策略性图像触发、精心模型选择与模型集成，使模型能从实验室环境泛化到多种实际仓库环境。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能有效从实验室条件泛化到多样化仓库环境，将部署简化为仅需相机安装、图像采集和模型部署，显著节省标注和再训练的资源与时间。

Conclusion: 该研究为仓库自动化实现提供了一种潜在的变革性方案，但本研究仅为实验性研究，并非生产部署。

Abstract: Deploying computer vision models in Warehouse Facilities traditionally requires extensive resources for camera mounting, image collection, annotation, training, and deployment - a process often needing repetition in each new environment due to camera mounting constraints and environmental variability. This paper explores an innovative approach to streamline this process by conducting the standard procedure solely in a laboratory setting, focusing on vertical material handling systems and anomaly detection in forks of the systems. Through extensive experimentation, we have found that combining optimal camera placement, strategic image triggering, careful model selection and model ensemble enables effective generalization from laboratory conditions to diverse warehouse facilities environments, potentially transforming warehouse automation implementation by simplifying warehouse facilities deployment to just camera mounting, image collection, and model deployment, thereby saving significant resources and time typically spent on image annotation and model retraining. This is an experimental research study and not a production deployment.

</details>


### [86] [How can embedding models bind concepts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31503)
*Arnas Uselis,Darina Koishigarina,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多目标场景中概念绑定（颜色与形状的对应）问题，发现CLIP模型存在绑定失败的现象。虽然CLIP的嵌入可分解为对象表示，但其绑定函数复杂度高，导致无法学习泛化的共享绑定机制。通过控制实验表明，在充足数据覆盖下，训练从零开始的Transformer模型可以学习低复杂度的乘法交互绑定函数，实现系统性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松完成多目标场景中的概念绑定（如判断哪个颜色属于哪个形状），但视觉-语言嵌入模型（如CLIP）在绑定任务上表现不佳。尽管CLIP在跨模态检索中类似词袋模型，但从其图像和文本嵌入中可分别恢复对象信息，这种矛盾现象值得深入探究。

Method: 通过绑定函数这一形式化框架，将概念映射到场景嵌入；分析CLIP嵌入的可加性分解特性；在受控的Transformer模型上进行从零开始的训练实验，研究数据覆盖度对绑定泛化的影响。

Result: CLIP的场景嵌入可加性分解为对象表示，解释了单模态探针能恢复对象信息的原因；但CLIP的绑定函数复杂度高，阻碍了图文编码器学习共享的绑定机制；在从零训练的Transformer中，充足数据覆盖下可学习到低复杂度的绑定函数（以概念间乘法交互为特征），实现系统性泛化。

Conclusion: CLIP在概念绑定上的局限性并非根本性的，通过充足的数据训练可以获得绑定泛化能力，关键在于学习低复杂度的、具有乘法交互特征的绑定函数。

Abstract: Humans easily determine which color belongs to which shape in multi-object scenes, an ability known as concept binding. Vision-language embedding models such as CLIP struggle with binding: they recognize individual concepts but fail to represent which concepts form which objects. Although CLIP behaves like a bag-of-concepts model in cross-modal retrieval, object information is recoverable from its image and text embeddings separately. We study this tension through the binding function, which maps concepts to scene embeddings. We find that scene embeddings decompose additively into object representations, explaining why uni-modal probes can recover object information. However, CLIP's binding function is high-complexity, which likely prevents the image and text encoders from learning a shared binding mechanism that generalizes to unseen concept combinations. We then ask whether this limitation is fundamental. We show that it is not. In controlled transformer models trained from scratch, binding generalization emerges with sufficient data coverage. These models learn low-complexity binding functions characterized by multiplicative interactions between concepts, enabling systematic generalization. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/oshapio/binding-concepts-complexity.

</details>


### [87] [Internalizing Temporal Consistency in Video Object-Centric Learning without Explicit Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31508)
*Rongzhen Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Juho Kannala,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出xSSC方法，通过时序通道分解（CCD）和跨时间重建（CTR）两个准零开销机制，将时序一致性从显式损失函数转化为隐式模型设计，替代了视频目标中心学习中的Slot-Slot对比学习损失，提升了训练效率并在视频目标发现与识别任务上取得新最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频OCL方法依赖Slot-Slot对比损失（SSC）来保持帧间对象一致性，但SSC需要帧间一对一的物体对应关系并引入额外损失，不符合奥卡姆剃刀原则。作者希望将时序一致性作为隐式模型设计而非显式损失函数。

Method: 提出xSSC框架，包含两个机制：(1) Chrono-Channel Decomposition (CCD) - 将slot表示沿通道维度分解为静态子空间和动态子空间；(2) Cross-Temporal Reconstruction (CTR) - 通过融合当前帧slot的静态通道与目标帧slot的动态通道，随机重建当前或前一帧的目标特征，仅使用标准OCL解码器即可实现时序一致性学习。

Result: 将xSSC集成到主流基线模型中，不仅提高了训练效率，还在视频目标发现与识别任务上取得了新的最优结果。PCA和梯度分析证实了物体时不变语义和时变运动学信息被分别编码到提出的子空间中。

Conclusion: xSSC通过隐式建模替代显式对比损失，更符合奥卡姆剃刀原则，在减少计算开销的同时提升了视频OCL性能，为视频目标中心学习提供了更简洁有效的范式。

Abstract: Video Object-Centric Learning (OCL) aims to represent objects as \textit{slot} vectors and maintain their consistency across frames. Slot-Slot Contrastive (SSC) loss has become the cornerstone for state-of-the-art (SOTA) video OCL methods. While highly effective, SSC relies on one-to-one object correspondence across frames and introduces an extra loss. Following Occam's Razor, we propose a paradigm shift: temporal consistency is better enforced as an implicit model design rather than an explicit loss. To elegantly exclude SSC (\textbf{xSSC}), we introduce two quasi-zero-overhead synergistic mechanisms: (\textit{i}) Chrono-Channel Decomposition (CCD) structurally disentangles slot representations along the channel dimension into \textit{static} and \textit{dynamic} sub-spaces, serving as an empirically unified information bottleneck; (\textit{ii}) Cross-Temporal Reconstruction (CTR) stochastically reconstructs target features of either the current or previous time step by fusing current slots' static channels and target slots' dynamic channels, using a single standard OCL decoder with minor training adaptation. Thereby, the slot sets inherently learn temporal consistency by minimizing the standard reconstruction error alone. Extensive experiments show that integrating xSSC into leading baselines not only improves training efficiency but also establishes new SOTAs on video object discovery and recognition tasks. Furthermore, our PCA and gradient analyses confirm that objects' time-invariant semantics and time-variant kinematics are encoded into the proposed sub-spaces. Our source code, model checkpoints and training logs are provided on https://github.com/Genera1Z/xSSC.

</details>


### [88] [Feature-Optimized Vision for Adaptive 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31534)
*Eric Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应特征优化的视觉前端，用于3D场景重建。该方法根据纹理、可重复性、区分性、三角测量角度和空间覆盖度为候选特征评分，并在固定重建管线中按视图分配特征预算。在合成多视图数据上，自适应策略相比随机、纯纹理和均匀网格基线，获得了最佳质量完整度和最低重建RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法使用固定特征阈值和均匀特征预算，容易在重复纹理、低视差区域或不稳定点上浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出自适应特征优化前端，对候选特征按纹理、可重复性、区分性、期望三角测量角度和空间覆盖度进行评分，然后为每个视图分配特征预算以最大化有效轨迹。

Result: 在合成多视图原型上，自适应策略相比随机、纯纹理和均匀网格基线，获得了最佳质量完整度和最低重建RMSE，同时保持了广泛的图像覆盖。

Conclusion: 该方法是模块化的前端策略，不是现代学习匹配或神经重建系统的替代品，但可以使经典和学习的3D管线更审慎地选择计算投入的视觉证据。

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene reconstruction depends on local image evidence that is both visually discriminative and geometrically useful. Fixed feature thresholds and uniform feature budgets are easy to deploy, but they can waste computation on repeated texture, low-parallax regions, or unstable points. This paper proposes an adaptive feature-optimized vision front end for 3D reconstruction. The method scores candidate features by texture, repeatability, distinctiveness, expected triangulation angle, and spatial coverage, then allocates a per-view feature budget to maximize useful tracks under a fixed reconstruction pipeline. A small synthetic multi-view prototype evaluates four selection policies across corridor, facade, object-table, and cluttered scenes. Compared with random, texture-only, and uniform-grid baselines, the adaptive policy obtains the best quality-aware completeness and the lowest aggregate reconstruction RMSE while preserving broad image coverage. The result is not a replacement for modern learned matching or neural reconstruction systems; it is a modular front-end policy that can make classical and learned 3D pipelines more deliberate about which visual evidence they spend compute on.

</details>


### [89] [Automated Prediction of Postoperative Pancreatic Fistula Using Preoperative Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31539)
*Ashok Choudhary,Chris Varghese,Leo Y. Li-Han,Frank G. Lee,Ellen L. Larson,Elizabeth B. Habermann,Cornelius A. Thiels,Hojjat Salehinejad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的深度学习管道，利用术前CT扫描自动进行胰腺分割和分类，用于术后胰瘘（POPF）的风险评估和分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后胰瘘是胰腺切除术后严重并发症，增加发病率、住院时间和医疗成本，现有方法缺乏自动化的术前风险评估工具。

Method: 构建了从胰腺自动分割到分类的端到端深度学习管道，评估了CNN3D、R(2+1)D ResNet-18和ResNet-MC3-18等多种3D架构。

Result: 多种3D架构均展现了有前景的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为胰腺CT分类提供了临床有价值的工具和方法学基准，可支持胰腺手术的术前决策。

Abstract: Postoperative pancreatic fistula (POPF) is a serious complication after pancreatic resection, increasing morbidity, hospital stay, and healthcare costs. We present an automatic, end-to-end deep learning pipeline-from pancreatic segmentation to classification-for preoperative POPF risk estimation and stratification using preoperative CT scans. A data set with auto-segmented pancreas volumes and surgical outcomes was used to evaluate multiple architectures, including a custom lightweight 3D CNN baseline (CNN3D), R(2+1)D ResNet-18, and ResNet-MC3-18 models. Evaluation across multiple 3D architectures demonstrated promising predictive performance. This approach offers a clinically valuable tool and a methodological benchmark for pancreas-specific CT classification, supporting improved preoperative decision-making in pancreatic surgery.

</details>


### [90] [Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet,Simon Henniger,Mahzarin R. Banaji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型在性别模糊图像中会默认输出男性职业偏见，并通过提出LALS指标揭示模型内部编码与实际输出之间存在系统性解耦——即使内部编码为女性关联，输出仍倾向男性，且这种不对称过滤机制从中间层到生成层逐步增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注性别明显可见时VLM的偏见表现，但对实践中常见的性别模糊输入（如全副武装的工人、背影）研究不足。本文旨在探究模型在模糊输入下的职业-性别默认关联，以及内部表征与输出行为之间是否存在解耦。

Method: 提出LALS（潜在关联倾斜分数），这是一种零样本度量方法，通过将视觉token激活映射到模型文本嵌入空间，逐token逐层测量概念关联。实验涵盖15个职业、800+性别模糊图像和4种VLM，并结合颜色消融实验进行分析。

Result: 内部表征与输出存在系统性解耦：模型内部编码为女性关联时，输出仍常为男性。层分析揭示了不对称过滤机制——男性信号从输入到生成端到端增强，而女性信号在中间层达到峰值后在被生成前被抑制。颜色消融实验表明服装颜色等文化视觉线索会进一步调节内部关联。

Conclusion: VLM在性别模糊输入下存在隐蔽的性别偏见行为，内部编码与输出行为的不一致表明仅靠输出层面的偏见评估可能低估模型偏见程度，需要更多关注内部表征层面的偏见检测与干预。

Abstract: Alignment teaches vision-language models (VLMs) to avoid expressing demographic biases, and when gender is clearly visible they largely succeed. Far less is known about ambiguous inputs (a worker in full gear, a figure seen from behind) cases common in practice yet rarely studied. We find that minimal prompting pressure exposes occupation-gender defaults when prompting ambiguous input images, with models collapsing to male even for strongly female-stereotyped occupations. But do these outputs reflect what models actually encode internally? We introduce LALS (Latent Association Leaning Score), a zero-shot metric that projects visual-token activations into the model's text-embedding space to measure concept associations per token and layer. Across 15 occupations, over 800 gender-ambiguous images, and four VLMs, internal representations and outputs are systematically decoupled: models often encode a female association internally yet output male. Layer-wise analysis reveals an asymmetric filter -- male signal amplifies end-to-end while female signal peaks mid-network and is suppressed before generation -- and a color ablation shows that culturally loaded visual cues such as clothing color further modulate these internal associations.

</details>


### [91] [EGOSTREAM: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Streaming Episodic Memory in Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31557)
*Rosario Forte,Giuseppe Lando,Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoStream，一个针对第一人称视角流式视频的 episodic memory（情节记忆）诊断基准，包含2250个问题、7个认知维度，并引入“答案有效期窗口”（AVW）概念。通过统一的多模态大模型框架评测多种记忆管理机制发现，所有方法均远低于实时速度，最高准确率仅约45%，揭示了当前架构在流式记忆方面的重大缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频基准缺乏对模型“记住了什么、记忆了多久”的诊断能力，无法有效评估自主智能体在动态真实环境中所需的连续情节记忆能力。

Method: 构建EgoStream基准，包含2250个问题，覆盖细节、空间、时间、事件、社交、因果和前瞻7个认知维度。引入答案有效期窗口（AVW）概念，将问题扩展为8528个基于回忆条件的评估。基于Qwen3-VL骨干，统一框架下比较滑动窗口、注意力汇、KV缓存剪枝、合并与卸载等多种流式记忆机制。

Result: 在统一骨干下，不同记忆机制的总准确率相近，但记忆特征差异显著：token剪枝优于token合并保留细节和时间结构，量化卸载改善了超长期回忆。所有方法均超过1秒/帧，最佳方法准确率仅约45%。

Conclusion: EgoStream为流式情节记忆提供了精细的诊断工具，暴露了当前多模态大模型在长时记忆和实时处理方面的关键差距，为未来架构改进提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Continuous episodic memory is a core capability for autonomous agents operating in dynamic, real-world environments, yet current streaming video benchmarks provide limited tools for diagnosing what models remember and for how long. We introduce \egostream, a diagnostic benchmark for streaming episodic memory evaluation in egocentric vision. \egostream organizes 2,250 curated questions along seven cognitive dimensions: detail, spatial, temporal, event, social, causal, and prospective memory. We introduce the Answer Validity Window (AVW), which specifies the temporal span an answer remains valid as the observed scene evolves. This allows us to expand the questions into 8,528 recall-conditioned evaluations, enabling controlled testing from instant to ultra-long-term recall while separating genuine model forgetting from natural world-state changes. We rigorously establish baseline performance through a unified streaming MLLM framework that compares several state-of-the-art memory-management mechanisms, covering sliding windows, attention sinks, KV-cache pruning, merging, and offloading. Experiments within a unified Qwen3-VL backbone reveal that comparable aggregate accuracies mask starkly different memory profiles. For instance, token pruning preserves fine-grained details and temporal structure significantly better than token merging, while quantized offloading rescues ultra-long-term recall. Ultimately, all mechanisms operate well below real-time (>1s per frame), and top performing methods ceil at about 45\% accuracy, exposing critical gaps in current architectures. \egostream provides the diagnostic testbed needed to close these gaps.

</details>


### [92] [nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31572)
*Zhiyu Huang,Johnson Liu,Rui Song,Zewei Zhou,Ruining Yang,Yun Zhang,Tianhui Cai,Hanyin Zhang,Mingxuan Gao,Valeria Xu,Jiali Chen,Yishan Shen,Yiluan Guo,Tony,Qi,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: nuReasoning是一个面向自动驾驶长尾场景推理的大规模真实世界数据集与基准，包含20,000个20秒长的视频片段，覆盖空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理三种推理类型，并支持推理评估与规划评估。实验表明，在该数据集上微调VLM可显著提升驾驶QA性能，且推理监督可提升VLA的规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准主要针对感知、预测或规划任务，缺乏对长尾驾驶场景中推理能力的充分监督与评估，而推理能力对于处理需要常识、空间关系理解和安全决策的复杂场景至关重要。

Method: 构建了nuReasoning数据集，包含20,000个20秒视频片段（多城市采集），提供多相机图像、LiDAR数据、HD地图、目标标注以及人工验证的三种推理类型标注（空间推理、决策推理、反事实推理），同时支持推理评估和规划评估。

Result: 在该数据集上微调VLM显著提升了驾驶相关的问答质量；将推理监督融入VLA训练后，即使在推理时关闭文本推理输出，规划性能也能得到改善。

Conclusion: nuReasoning为在真实长尾场景中评估和改进鲁棒、可解释的推理驱动型自动驾驶系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [93] [TunerDiT: Training-free Progressive Steering of Diffusion Transformer for Multi-Event Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31590)
*Ruotong Liao,Guowen Huang,Qing Cheng,Guangyao Zhai,Lei Zhang,Xun Xiao,Thomas Seidl,Daniel Cremers,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TunerDiT提出了一种无需额外训练的视频生成渐进式引导方法，通过事件分区掩码和跨事件提示融合，在多事件长视频生成中实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V模型在生成包含多个事件的长时间视频时效果不佳，面临事件边界模糊、时序一致性差等问题。

Method: (1) 事件分区掩码（Event-Partitioned Masking）：强制执行事件边界并允许跨事件过渡带；(2) 跨事件提示融合（Cross-Event Prompt Fusion）：注入相邻事件语义进行后期细化。

Result: 在8项指标上达到SOTA性能，文本对齐度随事件数量增加而提升，展现了扩展可能性。

Conclusion: TunerDiT是一种简单有效的多事件视频生成方法，无需训练即可在视频一致性和事件分离之间实现可调平衡。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation faces challenging questions when generating videos with long horizons containing multiple events. Inspired by the intrinsics of the diffusion process, we probe video diffusion transformers (DiTs) and uncover intrinsic turning points in the DiT denoising trajectory where conditioning text affects generation from global layout to fine-grained details. Building on this finding, we present TunerDiT, a simple yet effective progressive steering method that requires no additional training for multi-event generation. TunerDiT comprises two steering handles: (1) Event-Partitioned Masking that enforces event boundaries while allowing cross-event transition bands; (2) Cross-Event Prompt Fusion that injects neighboring event semantics for late-stage refinement. We contribute a self-curated prompt suite for benchmarking multi-event generation, i.e., Meve. TunerDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 metrics and offers a tunable trade-off between video consistency and event separation, compared with other training-free methods. The improvement in text alignment increases with the event count, indicating a scaling possibility with increasing event count.

</details>


### [94] [Joint Multi-Camera LiDAR Extrinsic Calibration via Learned Pairwise Initialization and Geometric Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31576)
*Aziz Al-Najjar,Marzieh Amini,James R. Green,Felix Kwamena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段联合多相机-激光雷达标定框架，通过先独立预测再联合优化，实现全局一致的外参标定，在KITTI和Walkley数据集上显著提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习-based方法独立处理每个相机-激光雷达对，忽略多相机平台中相机间的刚性几何耦合，导致单相机标定结果系统级不一致。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用CMRNext独立估计每个相机的外参并生成稠密2D-3D对应点；第二阶段通过多帧束调整联合优化，包含重投影误差、单相机先验和相对位姿先验三项，将独立预测转化为全局一致的多相机标定结果。

Result: KITTI上平移误差0.89cm、旋转误差0.038°；Walkley上平移误差从108.6cm降至3.1cm。

Conclusion: 显式建模多相机之间的耦合关系能显著提升标定精度和系统一致性，尤其在单相机预测不可靠时优势更为明显。

Abstract: Most learning-based camera-LiDAR calibration methods treat each camera-LiDAR pair independently, ignoring the rigid geometric coupling in multi-camera platforms. As a result, per-camera estimates may be individually accurate yet inconsistent at the system level. We present a two-stage framework for joint multi-camera LiDAR extrinsic calibration that combines learned pairwise matching with geometric refinement. First, CMRNext is applied independently to each camera to produce initial extrinsic estimates and dense 2D-3D correspondences. These predictions are then jointly refined through a multi-frame bundle adjustment with reprojection, per-camera prior, and relative-pose prior terms. This approach converts pairwise predictions into a globally consistent multi-camera calibration. Experiments on KITTI (in-domain for CMRNext) and Walkley (out-of-domain) datasets show improved per-camera accuracy and inter-camera consistency. On KITTI, the method achieves 0.89 cm translation error and 0.038 rotation error. On Walkley, it reduces translation error from 108.6 cm to 3.1 cm, highlighting the benefit of explicit multi-camera coupling when single-camera predictions are less reliable.

</details>


### [95] [Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31603)
*Jiazheng Xing,Hangjie Yuan,Lingling Cai,Xinyu Liu,Yujie Wei,Fei Du,Hai Ci,Tao Feng,Jiasheng Tang,Weihua Chen,Fan Wang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lumos-Nexus是一个训练高效的统一视频生成框架，采用两阶段设计：训练时仅对齐轻量生成器与理解模块，推理时通过UPFB渐进式将生成任务交给高容量预训练生成器，在提升视觉保真度的同时保持推理质量。同时提出VR-Bench基准评估推理驱动视频生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连接器的视频统一模型在指令引导视频合成上表现良好，但将高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中计算成本过高，限制了视觉质量。因此需要一种训练高效的框架，既能发展强推理驱动生成能力，又能显著提升视觉保真度。

Method: 1) 两阶段训练设计：训练阶段仅将轻量生成器与理解模块对齐，学习接收推理驱动的语义控制；2) 推理阶段引入统一渐进频率桥接(UPFB)，在共享潜在空间中逐步将生成任务移交给高容量预训练生成器，实现由粗到细的精炼；3) 提出VR-Bench基准数据集用于评估推理驱动视频生成能力。

Result: 在VBench上，Lumos-Nexus在视觉真实感和时间连贯性上取得显著提升；在VR-Bench上展现出强大的基于推理的生成性能。

Conclusion: Lumos-Nexus通过训练高效的解耦设计，在不损害推理质量的前提下大幅提升了视频生成保真度，为推理驱动的高保真视频生成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at https://jiazheng-xing.github.io/nexus-lumos-home/.

</details>


### [96] [SurGe: Improved Surface Geometry in Point Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31577)
*Karim Knaebel,Gonzalo Martin Garcia,Christian Schmidt,Ilya Fradlin,Lucas Nunes,Daan de Geus,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出点图法线度量来评估局部表面几何质量，并通过梯度匹配损失和邻域注意力解码器改进前馈3D重建的局部几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法虽能估计全局3D几何，但局部表面几何不准确，且常见评估指标对此不敏感。

Method: 1) 提出点图法线度量评估局部表面方向；2) 设计点梯度匹配损失监督深度归一化3D有限差分；3) 提出邻域注意力解码器(NAD)进行上采样和局部特征混合。

Result: 在8个零样本单目几何基准上，SurGe在全局点图AbsRel上达到最佳平均排名，并持续改进局部点图和法线评估。

Conclusion: 所提度量能更显式地反映局部几何误差，结合两种互补方法可有效提升局部表面几何质量。

Abstract: Recent feedforward 3D reconstruction methods predict point maps and estimate global 3D geometry remarkably well. However, their predictions still exhibit inaccurate local surface geometry, which is clearly visible qualitatively but only weakly reflected in common metrics. To make these errors more explicit in evaluation, we introduce a point map normal metric that evaluates the local surface orientation induced by neighboring 3D predictions. To reduce these errors, we propose two complementary components: a point gradient matching loss that supervises depth-normalized 3D finite differences, and a Neighborhood Attention Decoder (NAD) that progressively upsamples features and uses Neighborhood Attention for local feature mixing. Across eight zero-shot monocular geometry benchmarks, our model, SurGe, achieves the best average rank for global point map AbsRel and consistently improves local point map and point map normal evaluations.

</details>


### [97] [Recognizing Co-Speech Gestures in-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31589)
*Sindhu B Hegde,K R Prajwal,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRW数据集，首个大规模、帧级标注的语义手势基准，包含156,688个视频片段和150个词汇类别，并基于此训练视频模型完成手势分类、识别与时间定位三大任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型难以捕捉与言语语义相关的共语手势，主要受限于缺乏精确标注的训练数据。

Method: 构建GRW数据集（156,688个手动标注视频片段，涵盖150个词汇），并基于该数据集训练视频模型完成三项任务：手势语义分类、手势对应词汇识别、手势时间定位。

Result: 成功建立了GRW大规模手势数据集，并在其上完成了三项基准任务的训练与评估。

Conclusion: GRW数据集有效解决了语义共语手势标注数据匮乏的问题，为多模态手势理解提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: While humans naturally gesture during speech, only a sparse subset of these movements are visually depictive and semantically linked to specific spoken words. Current multimodal models struggle to capture these semantic co-speech gestures, heavily bottlenecked by a lack of precisely annotated training data. To address this, we introduce the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset, the first large-scale benchmark designed to map unconstrained human gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips, GRW spans a highly diverse 150-word taxonomy of physical actions, spatial descriptors, and abstract concepts. We leverage GRW to train video models to (a) classify gestures as semantic or not, (b) recognize the word corresponding to a co-speech gesture, and (c) temporally localize the gesture. We also use GRW to establish benchmarks for these three tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Global Motion with Compact Gaussians for Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31595)
*Mungyeom Kim,Minkyeong Jeon,Honggyu An,Jaewoo Jung,Hyuna Ko,Jisang Han,Hyeonseo Yu,Donghwan Shin,Sunghwan Hong,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C4G是一种基于少量可学习高斯查询令牌的4D重建框架，无需相机姿态即可从单目视频实现动态场景重建和新视角合成，具有更强的运动建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法逐帧逐像素预测3D高斯，存在高斯冗余和视角依赖偏差，难以有效学习场景运动。

Method: 提出C4G框架，使用时间戳条件化的可学习高斯查询令牌，聚合全局时序上下文特征并解码3D高斯，位置由目标时间戳调制；同时引入视频扩散模型增强渲染，并扩展到4D特征场支持点跟踪。

Result: 在显著减少高斯数量的情况下实现强新视角合成性能，无需相机姿态，运动建模更强，对大时间间隔鲁棒。

Conclusion: C4G通过紧凑的可学习查询令牌有效解决了前馈动态场景重建中的高斯冗余和运动建模问题。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

</details>


### [99] [KLIP: localized distribution shift detection via KL-divergence with diffusion priors in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31596)
*Alireza Kheirandish,Jihoon Hong,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于KL散度的OOD检测指标，无需校准数据或分布先验知识，可在逆问题中检测和定位分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的OOD检测方法需要偏移分布的先验知识，无法检测细微/局部分布偏移，且仅适用于完整图像而非逆问题的间接测量数据。

Method: 利用扩散先验与后验分布之间的KL散度作为OOD检测度量，无需校准数据或偏移分布知识，可检测整体图像OOD并定位局部OOD区域。

Result: 能检测细微且语义上有意义的分布偏移（如健康肝脏CT与肿瘤CT的区分），在不同扩散模型、数据集和逆问题上具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提KL散度指标有效解决了现有方法在OOD检测中的局限性，具有无需先验知识和可定位的优势。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown promising performance as data-driven priors for computational imaging, as well as some capacity to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) images. However, existing approaches to OOD detection often require some knowledge of the shifted distribution, fail to detect subtle or localized distribution shifts, and operate on full images, rather than the indirect measurements available in inverse problems. We propose an OOD detection metric based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the diffusion prior and the posterior distribution, that (i) does not require any calibration data or knowledge of the shifted distribution, and (ii) can detect whole images as OOD as well as localize OOD patches within an image. Experimentally, we show that this metric can detect subtle yet semantically meaningful distribution shifts, such as the shift from healthy liver CT scans to those with tumors, and generalizes across different types of diffusion models, datasets, and inverse problems. Our code can be found at https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP.

</details>


### [100] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel,Basavaraj Sunagad,Haoran Wang,David T. Hoffmann,Christian Theobalt,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SOCO是一个新的语义对应基准，包含100个类别、超过100万个对应关键点对及语言描述，用于系统评估视觉基础模型的结构化物体理解能力。实验发现视觉基础模型编码了强语义结构但在跨类别对应迁移上表现不佳，LVLM在文本定位上优于视觉匹配，且对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测下游密集任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型评估缺乏一致的协议和细粒度部件级标注，难以衡量模型对物体结构化部分的理解能力。作者希望通过构建一个大规模、功能语义一致的语义对应基准来系统评估这一能力。

Method: 构建SOCO基准，包含100个类别、超100万对应关键点对，引入对应的分类体系，并提供关键点语言描述以支持LVLM评估。通过涵盖多种语义对应类型的全面实验，评估视觉基础模型和LVLM的部件级理解能力。

Result: (i) 视觉基础骨干编码了强语义结构但跨类别对应迁移差，仅部分捕捉物体部件位置；(ii) LVLM在文本提示部件定位上优于视觉参考跨图像匹配，存在语言定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；(iii) 对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测等下游密集任务的表现。

Conclusion: SOCO可作为评估视觉和多模态基础模型中结构化、部件级表示质量的基准，填补了现有评估体系的空白。

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [101] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre,Jiajun Wu,Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StateKV方法，通过固定容量的重要性驱动循环状态将长视频VLM的预填充复杂度从二次方降至线性，在不微调或修改架构的情况下保持接近全自注意力的准确性，并显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频VLM在长视频和流式场景中，视频编码器依赖时空自注意力机制，导致计算量和延迟随帧数二次增长；而现有效率方法虽提升可扩展性，但常因激进地丢弃帧/令牌或粗略注意力近似而损失准确性。

Method: StateKV是一种推理时方法，通过固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态在帧间传递跨帧上下文，实现线性时间视频预填充；同时配合第二个完整的逐帧缓存用于解码阶段。

Result: 在三个长视频基准、七个模型（涵盖三个模型家族和多种规模）上，StateKV保持接近全自注意力的性能，始终优于主流的滑动窗口/基于近因的流式近似方法；同时显著降低视频预填充的计算成本（FLOPs），在固定计算预算下可通过运行更大模型获得更强的准确性。

Conclusion: StateKV为可扩展的长视频理解提供了一种实用的方向，无需微调或架构更改即可实现高效的线性时间推理。

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Dynamic Interaction-Aware and Causality-Disentangled Framework for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30994)
*Guangyuan Dong,Ziwei Hong,Shenghao Liu,Chenyu Wu,Yuanyuan Fang,Zihao Li,Xudong Zhang,Bingchen Liu,Yuchen Zhang,Haitao Ding,Zhenzhou Zhou,Ziyu Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出动态多模态因果解耦与自适应融合框架(MCAF)，通过因果干预、动态路由和条件扩散去噪，解决多模态情感分析中的静态冲突抑制和语言模态情感偏差问题，在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSA方法存在两个核心挑战：1) 静态冲突抑制机制无法适应样本间的动态变化；2) 语言模态固有的情感偏差会误导其他模态的学习。

Method: 1) 基于信息瓶颈原理的因果干预模块，构建结构因果模型解耦语言特征的情感偏差；2) 动态多模态路由器，在特征、时间、模态三个层面实时评估交互状态并自适应分配权重；3) 轻量级条件扩散去噪模块，对融合表示迭代去噪生成鲁棒的超级模态表示。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI上Acc-2/F1达86.52%/86.51%，在CMU-MOSEI上达86.72%/86.65%，均取得新的SOTA。

Conclusion: MCAF能动态感知多模态交互、解耦语言偏差，并提升模型可解释性。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) effectively leverages rich information from language, visual, and acoustic modalities, existing methods still face two core challenges: 1) static conflict suppression mechanisms fail to adapt to dynamic variations across samples, and 2) the inherent sentimental bias within the language modality, which can misguide learning from other modalities, remains entangled. To this end, we propose a Dynamic Multimodal Causal Disentanglement and Adaptive Fusion Framework (MCAF). Its cornerstone is the Multi-Granularity Causal Dynamic Router and a Conditional Diffusion Denoising Module. First, we introduce a causal intervention module based on the information bottleneck principle, which builds a Structural Causal Model to disentangle sentimental bias from language features, yielding a "de-confounded" language representation as a pure guiding signal. Second, we devise a Dynamic Multimodal Router that evaluates the interaction states (complementary, conflicting, or redundant) among visual, acoustic, and de-confounded language signals in real-time across three levels: feature, temporal, and modality, then adaptively allocates weights and routes information flow for fine-grained regulation. Finally, a lightweight Conditional Diffusion Denoising Module performs iterative denoising on the fused joint representation to explicitly filter out residual irrelevant information, generating a robust hyper-modality representation. Extensive experiments on the CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI benchmarks show that MCAF sets new state-of-the-art on key classification metrics, achieving an Acc-2/F1 of 86.52%/86.51% on MOSI and 86.72%/86.65% on MOSEI, while remaining highly competitive on others. Comprehensive analyses and visualizations further validate its efficacy in dynamically perceiving interactions, disentangling bias, and enhancing interpretability.

</details>


### [103] [A Pilot Study on Curator-Guided Multilingual Art Description for Blind and Low-Vision Audiences with Small Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31080)
*Iosif Tsangko,Andreas Triantafyllopoulos,George Margetis,Ioana Crihana,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用小型本地化视觉语言模型Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct，通过语言特定的LoRA适配器为德语、罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语的盲人和低视力群体生成多语言艺术描述。实验表明语言特定适配器在罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语中表现更稳定，而多语言适配器在德语中仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人和低视力群体在视觉艺术描述方面长期得不到充分服务，尤其在博物馆等隐私和知识产权受限场景中，需要小型本地化模型实现多语言无障碍描述。

Method: 构建面向BLV的平行字幕语料库，在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct骨干模型上，对比语言特定LoRA适配器与单一多语言适配器的效果。评估采用自动词汇和嵌入指标，并结合LLM评审协议，辅以小规模罗马尼亚语BLV试点研究校准。

Result: 语言特定适配器在罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语上展现出更稳定的可控性和视觉接地描述质量；多语言适配器在德语上表现竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 研究为小型本地化VLM在博物馆多语言无障碍部署提供了初步证据，但强调需要更大规模BLV用户研究和更广泛语言覆盖才能得出普遍结论。

Abstract: Blind and low-vision (BLV) audiences remain underserved by visual art descriptions, particularly across languages and in museum settings where privacy and intellectual-property constraints may favour small on-premise vision-language models (VLMs). This pilot study investigates curator-guided multilingual art description with Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct for German, Romanian, and Serbian. We construct a parallel BLV-oriented caption corpus from artwork images and metadata, and compare language-specific LoRA adapters with a single multilingual adapter under a fixed backbone and training budget. Evaluation combines automatic lexical and embedding-based metrics with an LLM-as-Judge protocol calibrated against a small Romanian BLV pilot study. Under our pilot setup, language-specific adapters show more stable controllability and visually grounded description quality for Romanian and Serbian, while multilingual adaptation remains competitive in German. We frame these findings as deployment-oriented evidence for small on-premise VLMs, and highlight the need for larger BLV user studies and broader language coverage before drawing general conclusions about multilingual accessibility.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen 是一个神经符号学管道，用于从文本生成符合物理定律的物理图表，在包含1449个问题的基准测试中显著优于GPT-5-image等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽然能产生视觉上合理的输出，但会系统性地出现力向量幻觉、忽略守恒定律和违反几何约束的问题，缺乏物理准确性。

Method: 采用神经符号学管道：1) LLM提取带类型场景图；2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图(PSLG)，编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑；3) 微调Qwen-VL模型执行视觉反馈循环迭代修正约束违反。

Result: 在力学、光学和电磁学共1449个问题的基准测试中，PhyDrawGen在物理准确性上显著优于GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 3 Pro。

Conclusion: 通过将语义理解与物理约束满足解耦，神经符号学方法能有效生成物理准确的图表，即使在处理非常见物体问题时也表现鲁棒。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [105] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有基于观测预测的世界模型在具身AI中会产生物理上错误的结果，提出应构建“物理可行”的世界模型，即只需识别出回答干预查询所需的最简物理抽象，通过模块化组件和动态编排实现可解释、可验证的物理推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测预测世界模型虽能生成视觉上合理的未来帧，但常违背物理规律，导致推荐不可行动作、错误预测交互结果或认证不安全行为。根本原因是不同物理系统可能外观相同但干预结果不同，模型缺乏对潜在物理结构的辨识能力。

Method: 提出物理可行的世界模型框架，包含环境表示、潜在状态与参数估计、动作规格、干预动力学和查询级响应等模块；通过自主编排器为每个查询动态选择和组合合适的组件；过渡模型可采用解析、仿真、学习或混合形式，但必须保持决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 通过控制基准实验（固定视觉场景、变化潜在物理参数）揭示现有模型失败案例，并展示所提方法能在现有系统无法正确回答的查询上给出正确结果。

Conclusion: 正确的抽象不是最细致的世界模型，而是保留与查询相关区分的最简模型。该分解使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计，并为规划、控制和验证提供设计原则。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [106] [Transforming and Encoding FTS for SAT Solving: What Helps, What Hurts (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30563)
*João Filipe,Álvaro Torralba,Gregor Behnke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将因子化任务编码为SAT问题，提出了多种编码策略，并分析了并行性和任务转换对SAT规划器性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化任务是一种比STRIPS或SAS+更紧凑的规划表示形式，但现有方法仅限于启发式搜索。研究者希望探索SAT方法在因子化任务上的应用。

Method: 提出了多种将因子化任务编码为SAT的翻译策略，重点是将因子化转移关系转换为命题逻辑的不同方式，并分析了不同层次的并行性和任务转换的影响。

Result: 提出了多种可行的SAT编码方案，并分析了并行性和任务转换对规划器性能的影响。

Conclusion: 因子化任务可以有效地编码为SAT，且不同的编码策略和并行性利用方式会影响SAT规划器的性能表现。

Abstract: Factored tasks are a classical planning representation that extends SAS+ with limited forms of disjunctive preconditions, conditional effects, and angelic nondeterminism. This allows for a more compact representation of tasks than traditional formalisms such as STRIPS or SAS+, and supports a wide range of task transformations. However, existing planning approaches for factored tasks have been limited to heuristic search methods.
  In this work, we investigate how to encode factored tasks in SAT. We propose several ways to encode the tasks, focusing on different strategies for translating the factored transition relation into propositional logic. We also analyze how to exploit parallelism at various levels in this setting and study the impact of common task transformations on the performance of SAT-based planners.

</details>


### [107] [Procedural Generation of First Person Shooter Maps using Map-Elites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30570)
*Simone de Donato,Pier Luca Lanzi,Daniele Loiacono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究利用MAP-Elites算法为第一人称射击（FPS）游戏设计关卡，提出了两种新的地图表示方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout），相比传统方法能生成更多样化且质量更高的地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPS地图生成方法在多样性和质量上存在局限，需要更好的地图表示方法来提升关卡设计的性能。

Method: 采用MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries（MESB）算法，引入Point-Line和Spatial-Layout两种新型地图表示方法，通过拓扑属性和涌现属性指标来引导优化过程。

Result: 新提出的地图表示方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout）相比传统All-Black和Grid-Graph方法，能够生成多样性更高、质量更好的FPS地图。

Conclusion: 新的地图表示方法能有效提升FPS关卡生成的多样性和质量，为游戏关卡自动设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We investigate the application of MAP-Elites (a well-known quality diversity algorithm) to design levels for First-Person Shooter (FPS) games. We consider two well-known map representations (All-Black and Grid-Graph) and introduce two novel representations (Point-Line and Spatial-Layout) that improve the characterization of FPS maps. We define a series of metrics to describe maps' topological properties (which solely depend on maps' layout), and emergent properties (which must be evaluated through actual gameplay). We perform an in-depth analysis to identify the most suitable features to guide MAP-Elites illumination process. We apply MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB) to evolve populations of FPS maps. Our results show that the new representations can generate maps with higher diversity and quality than the representations previously used for evolving FPS maps.

</details>


### [108] [Harness Updating Is Not Harness Benefit: Disentangling Evolution Capabilities in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30621)
*Minhua Lin,Juncheng Wu,Zijun Wang,Zhan Shi,Yisi Sang,Bing He,Zewen Liu,Tianxin Wei,Zongyu Wu,Zhiwei Zhang,Dakuo Wang,Xiang Zhang,Benoit Dumoulin,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou,Suhang Wang,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体的"框架自进化"能力，发现：框架更新能力在不同能力层级模型间趋于一致（弱模型也能产生有效更新），而框架受益能力则呈非单调性（中等模型受益最大，弱模型和强模型受益较小）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM智能体是否具备框架自进化能力——即模型能否通过执行经验更新外部框架（提示、技能、记忆、工具）来提升任务解决效果，以及这种能力与模型基础能力之间的关系。

Method: 将框架自进化分解为两种能力：(i) 框架更新能力（产生有用持久性框架更新的能力）；(ii) 框架受益能力（在任务解决中从更新后的框架中获益的能力）。通过不同能力层级的模型进行对比实验分析。

Result: 发现一：框架更新能力在不同能力层级模型间呈"平坦"趋势，Qwen3.5-9B的更新效果与Claude Opus~4.6相当。发现二：框架受益能力呈非单调性，弱模型受益极少，中等模型受益最大，强模型受益反而低于中等模型。

Conclusion: 建议将能力预算投入到任务解决智能体而不是进化器上；在智能体训练中重点加强框架调用和长程指令跟随能力。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed as systems built around editable external harnesses, including prompts, skills, memories and tools, that shape task execution without changing model parameters. Harness self-evolution adapts such agents by updating these harnesses from execution evidence. Yet it remains unclear whether a model's base capability in task-solving predicts its capabilities in harness self-evolution: which models produce useful harness updates, and which actually benefit from them? We analyze two harness self-evolution capabilities: (i) harness-updating, the capability to produce useful persistent harness updates from execution evidence; (ii) harness-benefit, the capability to benefit from updated harnesses during task solving. Our analysis reveals two findings. First, harness-updating is flat in base capability: models from different capability tiers produce harness updates that lead to surprisingly similar gains; even Qwen3.5-9B's updates yield gains comparable to those of Claude Opus~4.6. Second, harness-benefit is non-monotonic in base capability: weak-tier models benefit little from updated harnesses, mid-tier models benefit most, and strong-tier models benefit less than mid-tier. We trace low gains at the weak tier to two failure modes: weak-tier models may fail to activate relevant harness artifacts, or activate them but fail to follow them faithfully. These findings suggest investing capability budget in the task-solving agent rather than the evolver, and targeting harness invocation and long-horizon instruction following in agent training. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/A-EVO-Lab/a-evolve/tree/release/harness-evolution.

</details>


### [109] [EHRBench: An Automated and Reliable EHR-based Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30637)
*Yuzhang Xie,Keqi Han,Yunpeng Xiao,Hejie Cui,Guanchen Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Kai Shu,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EHRBench是一个基于电子健康记录的自动化临床决策基准，通过EHR-LLM-KB流水线构建了近百万个QA样本，用于评估LLM在诊断、治疗和预后三大临床决策任务上的可靠性，实验表明该基准可靠且揭示了现有LLM在临床推理中的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床决策评估缺乏一个兼具规模与质量、基于真实电子健康记录(EHR)的自动化可靠基准，以系统评估LLM在实际临床决策中的可靠性。

Method: 提出EHR-LLM-KB交互流水线：利用专用LLM将EHR轨迹自动转换为结构化模板，并确定性实例化为QA条目，同时通过知识库(KB)系统验证和过滤消除幻觉或模糊关系，构建了包含诊断、治疗、预后三类任务、近百万QA样本的基准。

Result: 在超过30个代表性LLM上进行了基准测试，结果显示不同设置下能力趋势一致，验证了EHRBench的可靠性，同时揭示了当前LLM在临床可靠性方面的可改进差距。

Conclusion: EHRBench为评估LLM在真实临床决策中的表现提供了可靠且可扩展的基准，有助于推动更可靠的临床LLM系统的开发。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [110] [Structure-Induced Information for Rerooting Levin Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30664)
*Jake Tuero,Michael Buro,Laurent Orseau,Levi H. S. Lelis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过学习"rerooter"隐式分解子任务，克服了显式子目标生成的开销和可扩展性问题，在复杂环境中实现了更高效的树搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于子目标的策略树搜索虽然有效，但显式子目标生成开销大且阻碍可扩展性，需要一种隐式分解子任务的方法来降低计算负担。

Method: 提出三种rerooter设计：(i)基于聚类的rerooter，利用全局状态空间结构；(ii)基于启发式的rerooter，利用学习到的代价估计；(iii)混合两者的rerooter。这些方法通过√LTS算法实现隐式子任务分解，避免显式重建子目标。

Result: 基于rerooting的方法在复杂环境中成功扩展，超越了基于子目标的策略树搜索，并在测试领域达到了最先进的在线训练效率。

Conclusion: 隐式rerooter设计能够显著降低计算开销，提升可扩展性，是一种有效的子目标搜索替代方案。

Abstract: Subgoal-based policy tree search, which uses a policy to guide search, is effective for complex single-agent deterministic problems but often relies on explicit subgoal generation that can incur substantial overhead and hinders scalability. In this paper, we overcome these limitations by using a learned ``rerooter'' through the recently-introduced $\sqrt{\text{LTS}}$ algorithm. A rerooter implicitly decomposes the problem into soft subtasks. While previous work focused on the formal guarantees for given or handcrafted rerooters, in this work we propose three rerooter designs: (i) a clustering-based rerooter that exploits global state-space structure, (ii) a heuristic-based rerooter that leverages learned cost-to-go estimates, and (iii) a hybrid that combines both signals. Our framework avoids having to explicitly reconstruct and reason over generated subgoals, thereby enabling scalable allocation of search effort with significantly lower computational overhead. Empirically, our rerooting-based methods scale to complex environments where subgoal-based policy tree search fails, and achieve state-of-the-art online training efficiency on the domains tested.

</details>


### [111] [Healthcare Mechanisms from Policy-as-Code Search under Strategic Provider Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30680)
*Zihan Wang,Xiang Xu,Hongyuan Zha,Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将医院机制设计重新定义为语言模型的程序合成，通过多智能体模拟器Medi-Sim评估策略性提供者响应，并利用LLM引导的进化代码搜索合成可检查的混合目标程序，消除向上编码、减少拒绝并保留大部分利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准固定了提供者响应，无法评估机制所产生的均衡，而医疗机制与策略性提供者响应密不可分。

Method: 将医院机制设计重新定义为语言模型的类型化、可检查规则程序合成；使用多智能体模拟器Medi-Sim（含编码、选择、延迟、努力、分流五个策略通道）执行和评分；通过激励扫描恢复经典卫生经济学发现；利用LLM引导的进化代码搜索合成混合目标程序。

Result: 激励扫描恢复了向上编码、利润压力下的低复杂度患者选择以及Goodhart漂移现象；关闭编码审计通道使低复杂度选择翻倍；合成的混合目标程序消除了向上编码，将拒绝率减半，并保留了大部分基线利润。

Conclusion: 通过程序合成和模拟驱动的方法，可以有效评估和优化医疗机制设计，应对策略性提供者响应带来的挑战。

Abstract: Healthcare mechanisms are inseparable from the strategic provider response they induce: existing healthcare AI benchmarks hold this response fixed and so cannot evaluate mechanisms by the equilibrium they produce. We recast hospital mechanism design as program synthesis for language models: typed, inspectable rule programs are executed and scored by Medi-Sim, a multi-agent simulator with five strategic provider channels (coding, selection, delay, effort, triage). An incentive sweep recovers classical health-economics findings as adjacent regimes -- up-coding and low-complexity-patient selection under profit pressure, and Goodhart-style drift where measured performance becomes anti-correlated with true outcomes -- and a single audit lever exposes pressure migration: closing the coding channel more than doubles low-complexity selection. LLM-guided evolutionary code search over the same rule-program space then synthesizes an inspectable mixed-objective program that eliminates up-coding, halves rejection, and retains most of the profit-oriented baseline's funds.

</details>


### [112] [Generating Graph-like Rules for Knowledge Graph Reasoning via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30747)
*Haoxiang Cheng,Yunfei Wang,Chao Chen,Kewei Cheng,Zhipeng Lin,Haoxuan Li,Changjun Fan,Shixuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRiD是一个将图状规则发现重构为离散生成过程的框架，通过监督预训练和强化学习微调的两阶段策略，有效挖掘知识图谱中的图状规则（如循环和分支），在六个基准数据集上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规则挖掘方法主要关注简单的链状规则，忽略了图状结构（如循环和分支）中蕴含的更丰富的语义信息，且搜索空间的组合爆炸使得图状规则挖掘极具挑战性。同时，扩散模型等生成方法因训练目标与规则质量不匹配、非可微的评估指标无法直接优化等问题，无法直接应用于规则挖掘。

Method: 提出GRiD框架，将图状规则发现重构为以目标关系为条件的离散生成过程。采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段进行监督预训练，从KG元图的子图中捕获结构先验；第二阶段通过强化学习策略梯度优化，利用非可微的规则质量指标直接指导模型微调。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，GRiD在知识图谱补全任务中取得竞争性表现。消融实验验证了方法的效率和鲁棒性，并表明图状规则与链状规则在知识图谱补全中具有互补性。

Conclusion: GRiD通过将图状规则挖掘转化为生成问题并采用两阶段训练策略，有效解决了现有方法在图状规则发现上的不足，实验证明图状规则能补充链状规则，提升知识图谱补全性能。

Abstract: Logical rules constitute a cornerstone of knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, valued for their interpretability and ability to model relational patterns. However, existing rule mining methods predominantly focus on simple chain-like rules and therefore neglect the richer relational information encoded in graph-like structures, such as cycles and branches. This limitation is further exacerbated by computational bottlenecks caused by the combinatorial explosion of the search space, which is especially challenging for graph-like rules. Meanwhile, generative approaches such as diffusion models, despite their success in other domains, can not be directly applied to rule mining because their training objectives are not aligned with the goal of learning high-quality rules, and non-differentiable KG rule quality metrics cannot directly guide model optimization. To address these limitations, we propose GRiD, a framework that reformulates graph-like rule discovery as a discrete generative process conditioned on the target relation. GRiD employs a two-phase training strategy. First, supervised pre-training enables GRiD to capture structural priors from subgraphs sampled from the KG meta-graph.
