Subsequently, reinforcement learning is applied to fine-tune GRiD through policy gradient optimization guided directly by non-differentiable rule-quality metrics. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that GRiD achieves competitive performance on KG completion tasks. Ablation studies confirm the efficiency and robustness of GRiD and further show that graph-like rules complement chain-like rules in KG completion. Our codes and datasets are available in https://github.com/Haoxiang-Cheng/GRiD

</details>


### [113] [Learning Agent-Compatible Context Management for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30785)
*Lu Yi,Runlin Lei,Liuyi Yao,Yuexiang Xie,Yuyang Li,Wenhao Zhang,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaCoM通过外部LLM和强化学习为冻结的智能体动态管理上下文，在长周期任务中显著提升性能，并发现保真度-可靠性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文任务中累积的上下文会导致性能退化，现有方法需要训练智能体本身进行上下文管理，不适用于闭源智能体，且忽略了不同智能体需要不同策略。

Method: 训练一个外部LLM作为上下文管理器，通过灵活的修改动作（如摘要、修剪）和端到端强化学习，为冻结的智能体管理上下文。

Result: 在网页搜索和深度研究基准上，AdaCoM显著提升了多种智能体的性能；揭示了保真度-可靠性权衡；迁移实验表明管理器可在能力相似的智能体间有效复用。

Conclusion: AdaCoM为闭源智能体提供了一种无需修改智能体本身的可复用上下文管理方案，且发现智能体能力越强越需要高保真度，能力越弱越需要激进压缩。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly face long-horizon tasks such as web search and deep research in real-world applications, where accumulated context can cause long-context degradation and reasoning failures. Prior work mitigates this through context management with agent-side context control or fixed strategies such as summarization, which require training the agent itself for adaptation - making it impractical for closed-source agents and ignoring that different agents may require different strategies. We introduce Adaptive Context Management (AdaCoM), which trains an external LLM to manage the context of a frozen agent through flexible modification actions and end-to-end reinforcement learning. Across diverse agents on web search and deep research benchmarks, AdaCoM substantially improves performance by preserving task constraints and progress while pruning stale content. The learned strategies reveal a Fidelity-Reliability Trade-off: agents with higher vanilla ReAct performance benefit from higher-fidelity context preservation, whereas lower-performing agents require more aggressive compression to stay within a reliable reasoning regime. Transfer experiments show that AdaCoM generalizes most effectively across agents with similar capability (measured by vanilla ReAct performance), suggesting a practical path toward reusable context managers for agent systems.

</details>


### [114] [PReMISE: Policy Rubrics as Measurement Specifications for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30803)
*Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Tharindu Kumarage,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Anna Rumshisky,Pradeep Natarajan,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PReMISE框架，用于发现和审计LLM评估中的评分标准（rubric），并证明不同评分标准对评估结果有显著影响，且高评分者一致性不代表低可被利用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评估者时，其评分高度依赖于所使用的评分标准（rubric），模糊的评分标准可能导致模型奖励捏造事实或违反用户意图的答案，但现有研究缺乏系统的方法来发现和审计评分标准。

Method: 提出PReMISE框架，包含：(i) 从成对人类偏好数据中发现策略级评分标准集；(ii) 从结构充分性、可靠性、偏好拟合、对抗鲁棒性四个维度审计评分标准。同时引入两种修复操作：偏好排序选择（提高评分准确率）和可靠性约束精炼（降低可利用性）。

Result: 没有一个原始评分标准源能同时满足可靠、偏好预测和对抗鲁棒；高评分者一致性不意味着低可被利用性；PReMISE是唯一在可应用性、特异性和有效维度上同时取得非平凡结果的评分标准源；偏好排序选择将评分准确率从65.0%提升至68.6%；可靠性约束精炼将可利用响应获高分的比率从46.4%降至36.0%。

Conclusion: 评分标准的选择对LLM评估结果至关重要，PReMISE框架能够有效发现和审计评分标准，并且所提出的修复操作可以在保证评估质量的同时降低系统被利用的风险。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to evaluate open-ended responses, but their scores depend strongly on the rubrics that condition them. A vague rubric asking for a response to be ``helpful and factual'' can reward polished answers that invent facts or violate user intent. We treat reusable rubrics as measurement specifications: changing the rubric changes the response quality measurement induced by a fixed judge. We introduce PReMISE, a framework that, given pairwise human-preference data, (i) discovers a policy-level rubric set, and (ii) audits any rubric set under LLM-judge use along four axes: structural adequacy, reliability, preference fit, and adversarial robustness. Across rubric sources no raw source is simultaneously reliable, preference-predictive, and adversarially robust; and high inter-rater agreement does not imply low exploitability. PReMISE is the only rubric source to score non-trivially on applicability, specificity, and effective dimensionality simultaneously. We contribute two audit-targeted repair operations: preference-rank selection raises judge accuracy on paired responses from $65.0\%$ to $68.6\%$, competitive with the strongest rubric-discovery baselines and leading on two of three judges in our cross-judge sweep; reliability-constrained refinement reduces the rate at which exploit responses receive high scores from $46.4\%$ to $36.0\%$ with little change in inter-judge agreement ($α{=}.531\to.519$).

</details>


### [115] [SLAT: Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming for Efficient CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30832)
*Jian Yao,Xiongcai Luo,Ran Cheng,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SLAT框架，通过段级自适应修剪方法，在保持推理准确率的同时将推理长度减少50%，有效解决了大推理模型中的过度思考问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大推理模型在生成链式推理时存在结构性冗余（过度思考），导致高计算开销却不提升准确率；而传统的长度惩罚方法过于粗糙，会连同有用推理一起抑制。

Method: 提出SLAT（Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming）框架，基于高概率段具有低边际效用的理论发现，在强化学习中引入段级最优性判定准则，选择性抑制冗余推理段。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，SLAT相比未压缩基线减少50%推理长度，同时保持有竞争力的准确率，建立了更优的准确率-效率帕累托边界。

Conclusion: 理论上 grounded 的段级感知修剪是大语言模型高效链式推理的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models have significantly improved chain-of-thought (CoT) capabilities via reinforcement learning (RL). However, generated reasoning chains frequently suffer from structural redundancy (i.e., \emph{overthinking}), incurring high computational overhead without improving answer correctness. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on token-uniform length penalties, which provide coarse, segment-agnostic pressure toward shorter outputs and can inadvertently suppress useful reasoning alongside redundancy. To address this, we demonstrate that inefficiency concentrates in high-probability segments with low marginal utility. We derive a theoretical characterization of segment suboptimality under the correctness-length trade-off objective and propose \textsc{SLAT} (Segment-Level Adaptive Trimming), an RL framework that selectively suppresses redundant segments based on this criterion. Empirical results on standard benchmarks indicate that \textsc{SLAT} establishes a superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier, reducing reasoning length by $50\%$ relative to uncompressed baselines while maintaining competitive accuracy. Overall, our results suggest that theoretically grounded, segment-aware trimming is a promising direction for efficient CoT reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [116] [Distilling LLM Feedback for Lean Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30861)
*Gaetan Narozniak,Gérard Biau,Rémi Munos,Ahmad Rammal,Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为反馈蒸馏（Feedback Distillation）的训练方法，通过在token级别匹配模型自身分布与语言模型提供的特权反馈，解决GRPO在推理模型后训练中存在的稀疏奖励、探索不足和模式坍缩问题。在Lean4定理证明任务中，该方法比GRPO保持更高的轨迹多样性，且两者互补，用反馈蒸馏初始化GRPO可获得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在推理模型后训练中存在稀疏奖励、探索受限和模式坍缩等问题，需要改进训练方法以提升复杂推理能力。

Method: 提出反馈蒸馏（Feedback Distillation），在token级别训练模型匹配自身分布与语言模型提供的特权反馈，提供token级监督并注入外部知识。

Result: 在Lean4定理证明中，反馈蒸馏比GRPO保持更高的生成轨迹多样性和策略熵，更好的pass@k扩展性；用反馈蒸馏初始化GRPO优于单独使用任一方法。

Conclusion: 反馈蒸馏与GRPO互补，为提升复杂推理模型的后训练提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Post-training for reasoning models typically combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, most commonly with GRPO. However, this algorithm suffers from sparse rewards, limited exploration, and mode collapse. Building upon recent works on self-distillation, we propose Feedback Distillation, a training method where the model is trained to match, at the token level, its own distribution conditioned on privileged feedback produced by a language model. Feedback Distillation offers token-level supervision and can inject external knowledge. Evaluating our method for Lean4 theorem-proving, we find that Feedback Distillation maintains greater diversity in generated trajectories than GRPO, yielding higher policy entropy and better pass@k scaling. The two methods are complementary: initializing GRPO from a Feedback Distillation checkpoint outperforms either method alone. All in all, our results suggest a promising avenue to improve post-training for complex reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang,Xingyu Wang,Mingze Kong,Zhubo Shi,Yuqian Hou,Hong Xu,Zhongxiang Dai,Minchen Yu,Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Unified Inference Scaling (UIS)框架，将模型路由与测试时扩展统一优化，并设计UniScale在线系统，利用上下文多臂老虎机和LinUCB算法实现细粒度、动态的推理质量-成本平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将模型路由和测试时扩展(TTS)作为两个独立维度处理，导致：路由只能提供粗粒度的离散性能变化，单模型TTS存在容量上限和收益递减问题，且两者分离限制了动态推理环境的适应性。

Method: 提出UIS统一优化空间，将模型路由和TTS联合建模；设计UniScale在线框架，将自适应UIS建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，采用LinUCB算法学习推理策略，并融入效率感知学习和成本建模以优化高维动作空间。

Result: 在多种动态推理场景下，UniScale有效利用UIS空间中的协同效应，实现了更细粒度且持续更优的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 通过统一模型路由与测试时扩展，UIS/UniScale克服了现有解耦设计的固有限制，为LLM部署提供了一种更灵活、高效的推理优化方案。

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [118] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang,Xiaogang Li,Ruochen Gao,Peiyao Xiao,Chengliang Xu,Zeyu Wang,Zichao Chen,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BilliardPhys-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在台球物理场景中的推理能力，发现模型存在"静止偏差"——当物理结果难以推断时，倾向于预测无交互发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在静态图像识别上表现良好，但在直观物理推理（如预测物体运动和交互）方面仍存在明显不足，缺乏系统性的评估基准来揭示这些缺陷。

Method: 构建BilliardPhys-Bench基准，使用程序化引擎生成包含摩擦和弹性碰撞的随机台球场景，测试模型在三种物理推理任务上的表现：预测球间碰撞、墙壁反弹以及运动停止后的最终球位。

Result: 评估了GPT、Claude、Gemini和Qwen系列的最新多模态大模型，发现随着模拟时间增加和场景几何复杂度提升，模型性能显著下降；并发现一种系统性失败模式——"静止偏差"。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型在视觉动力学推理上存在根本性缺陷，需要在多模态架构中引入更好的物理归纳偏置来改善这一能力。

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [119] [A Persona-Based Evaluation Framework for Pluralistic Alignment in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31021)
*Atahan Karagoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态空间约束的仿真框架，用结构化认知剖面替代单一评估函数，实现多元视角的AI评估，同时揭示了仿真评估者存在状态空间漂移和语义不一致的问题，论证了嵌入动态调节机制的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐评估依赖单一的基准测试框架，将多元的人类判断简化为聚合统计基线，忽略了文化、人口统计和情境的差异性。

Method: 提出状态空间约束仿真框架，构建表示不同人类视角的合成认知剖面流形，利用现代生成架构实例化并维持评估者角色，进行多元视角依赖的基准测试。

Result: 生成架构能以高一致性维持评估角色，但在序列推理和随机提示扰动下出现系统性的角色连贯性退化，表现为状态空间漂移和语义不一致。

Conclusion: 静态对齐约束不足以维持鲁棒的评估行为，需要在生成系统中嵌入动态的、可生存性驱动的调节机制，以保持连贯的认知仿真。

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability. However, we further analyze the stability of these simulated evaluators under sequential inference and stochastic prompt perturbations, revealing systematic degradation in persona coherence that manifests as state-space drift and semantic inconsistency. These findings suggest that static alignment constraints are insufficient for sustaining robust evaluative behavior over time. Instead, we argue for the necessity of embedding dynamic, viability-driven regulatory mechanisms within generative systems to preserve coherent cognitive emulation. By framing persona-based evaluation as a structured dynamical system over latent representation manifolds, this study provides a foundation for more adaptive, human-aligned, and context-sensitive approaches to AI evaluation.

</details>


### [120] [HADT: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Differential Transformer for Autonomous Earth Observation Satellite Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31023)
*Mohamad A. Hady,Muhammad Anwar Masum,Siyi Hu,Mahardhika Pratama,Jimmy Cao,Ryszard Kowalczyk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的强化学习架构，用于异构卫星集群自主执行对地观测任务中的资源管理，通过关系型观测-动作令牌化和差分注意力机制，实现了相比基线方法的显著性能提升，并展现出良好的适应性和迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于数学模型的优化调度方法在面对动态变化和不确定性环境时效果不佳，亟需一种无需模型、自适应的实时资源管理方案。

Method: 将资源管理问题重构为序列决策过程，采用模型无关的强化学习，提出基于Transformer的架构，包含关系型观测-动作令牌化和差分注意力机制。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提架构相比现有基线方法取得了显著的性能提升，并在不同卫星数量场景下展现出强适应性和迁移性。

Conclusion: 提出的Transformer强化学习架构能够有效解决异构卫星集群自主对地观测中的资源管理问题，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of autonomous resource management in heterogeneous satellite cluster conducting Earth Observation (EO) missions including optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites. In autonomous operation mode, satellites are equipped with intelligent capabilities enabling real-time decision-making based on the latest conditions, while requiring minimal interaction with ground operators. Traditional scheduling approaches typically rely on mathematical models to represent satellite mission and resource management. Then, this problem is solved by using optimization algorithms. However, such solutions become less effective when the underlying models are not available, over complex, and inaccurate due to dynamic changes and uncertainties inherent in the space mission environment. A promising alternative is to reformulate the problem as a sequential decision-making process and apply model-free reinforcement learning techniques to enable adaptive and real-time resource management. To this end, we propose a novel transformer-based architecture tailored for heterogeneous satellite cluster autonomous EO Mission with relational observations-actions tokenization and differential attention mechanism. Our experimental results demonstrate significant performance improvements compared to the available baselines. Moreover, the proposed architecture exhibits strong adaptability and transferability with respect to varying numbers of satellite clusters.

</details>


### [121] [GraphARC: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Graph-Based Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31031)
*Saku Peltonen,August Bøgh Rønberg,Andreas Plesner,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphARC，一个基于图数据的抽象推理基准，将ARC范式扩展到图结构，发现现有语言模型在图变换任务中存在理解与执行差距及扩展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象推理基准多局限于网格或文本格式，缺乏对图结构数据的系统评估，而图推理是智能的核心能力之一。

Method: GraphARC将ARC的小样本变换学习范式推广到图数据，涵盖局部、全局和层次化图变换，支持跨图族和规模的多样化生成。

Result: 现有语言模型能回答图属性问题但难以完成完整图变换任务，存在理解-执行鸿沟；在图规模增大时性能显著下降。

Conclusion: GraphARC结合了节点分类、链接预测和图生成，为未来图基础模型提供了有前景的测试平台。

Abstract: Relational reasoning lies at the heart of intelligence, but existing benchmarks are typically confined to formats such as grids or text. We introduce GraphARC, a benchmark for abstract reasoning on graph-structured data. GraphARC generalizes the few-shot transformation learning paradigm of the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC). Each task requires inferring a transformation rule from a few input-output pairs and applying it to a new test graph, covering local, global, and hierarchical graph transformations. Unlike grid-based ARC, GraphARC instances can be generated at scale across diverse graph families and sizes, enabling systematic evaluation of generalization abilities. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models on GraphARC and observe clear limitations. Models can answer questions about graph properties but often fail to solve the full graph transformation task, revealing a comprehension-execution gap. Performance further degrades on larger instances, exposing scaling barriers. More broadly, by combining aspects of node classification, link prediction, and graph generation within a single framework, GraphARC provides a promising testbed for future graph foundation models.

</details>


### [122] [Vector Linking via Cross-Model Local Isometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31100)
*Ziying Chen,Yang Cao,He Sun,Beining Yang,Tianjian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VecLinking方法，利用局部几何一致性，通过少量锚点种子集在哈希空间中迭代匹配，实现不同黑盒编码器生成的嵌入向量间的跨模型对象对应关系恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同黑盒对比编码器对部分重叠数据集生成的嵌入向量缺乏直接对应关系，现有方法难以在无监督条件下跨模型对齐嵌入空间。

Method: 基于局部几何一致性（短距离近似保持比例，长距离因模型特异性失真），提出迭代式参考几何嵌入哈希：用锚点距离表示向量，哈希空间匹配候选链接，通过Beta-Bernoulli后验聚合证据，引导出高置信链接作为新锚点。

Result: 在多个基准和嵌入模型对上，该方法在不同重叠率、锚点预算和域外锚点条件下均实现准确鲁棒的链接恢复，并成功应用于向量数据库集成和跨模型聚类。

Conclusion: 局部几何一致性是跨模型向量链接的关键性质，基于哈希的迭代锚点扩展策略能高效恢复跨编码器对象对应关系，具有良好实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: We study Vector Linking: given two embedding clouds produced by different black-box encoders over partially overlapping datasets, recover cross-model object correspondences using only vectors. Empirically and theoretically, we show that independently trained contrastive encoders exhibit local geometric consistency: short-range distances are approximately preserved up to a scale factor, while long-range distances are not due to model-specific distortion. Building on this, we propose an iterative, reference-based geometric embedding hashing that recovers vector links from a tiny seed set of paired anchors. It represents each vector by distances to sampled paired anchors, proposes candidate links via hash-space matching, and aggregates evidence across views in a Beta-Bernoulli posterior to bootstrap high-confidence links as new anchors. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and embedding model pairs demonstrate accurate and robust linking under varying overlap, seed budgets, and out-of-domain anchors, with applications to vector database integration and cross-model clustering. Code is available at https://github.com/DBgroup-Edinburgh/VecLinking.

</details>


### [123] [Formalizing and falsifying causal pathways of rare events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31254)
*Anahita Haghighat,Dominik Janzing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对结构方程模型中罕见事件的因果路径分析，提出了因果路径的形式化定义及其可检验含义，并引入了一种连接简单因果解释与详细因果建模的抽象方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的罕见事件根因分析形式化工作主要集中在异常点的识别，但缺乏对因果路径（即从原因到结果的中间链条）的正式定义和可检验性分析。作者希望通过形式化因果路径，弥合简单口头因果解释与详细因果模型之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于结构方程模型中罕见事件的根因分析形式化框架，提出因果路径的形式化定义，并推导其可检验的统计含义。进一步分析了在什么条件下这些含义仅依赖于基于罕见事件路径的因果抽象，而非完整的因果图。

Result: 识别出因果路径的可检验含义仅依赖于路径本身的因果抽象的条件，从而无需完整的因果图即可进行推理。引入了一种将因果结构抽象为罕见事件路径的方法，该方法能够桥接简单因果解释与详细因果建模。

Conclusion: 本文的工作为从罕见事件数据中提取可解释的因果路径提供了理论保障，使得在实际应用中可以在不掌握完整因果图的情况下，依然能够进行有意义的因果推断。

Abstract: Building on recent formalizations of root cause analysis for rare events (``outliers'') in structural equation models, we propose a formal definition of a causal pathway and discuss its testable implications. We identify conditions under which these implications depend only on a causal abstraction defined by the pathway of rare events, rather than on the full causal graph of the underlying system. Accordingly, we introduce an abstraction of causal structure to pathways of rare events that bridges simple verbal causal explanations and detailed causal modeling.

</details>


### [124] [COLLEAGUE.SKILL: Automated AI Skill Generation via Expert Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31264)
*Tianyi Zhou,Dongrui Liu,Leitao Yuan,Jing Shao,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COLLEAGUE.SKILL系统，能够从个人或角色的异构痕迹中自动蒸馏出可检查、可修正、可移植的AI技能包，包含能力轨道和行为轨道，支持自然语言反馈更新、版本回滚等全生命周期管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent系统缺乏从异构痕迹中端到端提取可检查、可修正的技能工作流，导致难以构建真正承载专家知识、判断和交互风格的人格化智能体。

Method: 提出COLLEAGUE.SKILL自动化痕迹到技能蒸馏系统，生成包含能力轨道（实践、心智模型、决策启发式）和行为轨道（沟通风格、交互规则、纠正历史）的技能包，支持检查、调用、自然语言更新、回滚、跨主机安装及受控分发。

Result: 公开仓库约18.5k GitHub星标；技能画廊列出215个技能（来自165位贡献者），技能卡累计超10万星标。

Conclusion: 人格化技能应表示为可移植、可修正的包，而非不透明的提示或隐式记忆。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected not only to complete isolated tasks, but also to carry bounded representations of human expertise, judgment, and interaction style. Building such person-grounded agents remains difficult because actionable knowledge associated with a person or role is usually embedded in heterogeneous traces rather than written as clean instructions. Existing memory and persona systems capture fragments of this evidence, while skill frameworks provide portable packaging formats; however, there is no end-to-end workflow for distilling these traces into inspectable, correctable, and agent-usable skills. We present an automated trace-to-skill distillation system for generating person-grounded AI skills via expert knowledge distillation. Given materials from a target person or role, COLLEAGUE.SKILL produces a versioned skill package with two coordinated tracks: a capability track for practices, mental models, and decision heuristics, and a bounded behavior track for communication style, interaction rules, and correction history. The package can be inspected, invoked, updated through natural-language feedback, rolled back, installed across agent hosts, and optionally prepared for controlled distribution. We describe the artifact contract, generation workflow, correction lifecycle, deployment surface, and domain presets implemented in the open-source system. At the time of writing, the public repository has approximately 18.5k GitHub stars; the gallery lists 215 skills from 165 contributors and more than 100k cumulative stars across listed skill cards. The system illustrates how person-grounded skills can be represented as portable, correctable packages rather than opaque prompts or hidden memories.

</details>


### [125] [TraceGraph: Shared Decision Landscapes for Diagnosing and Improving Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31308)
*Junjie Nian,Kang Chen,Ge Zhang,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceGraph是一个基于图的框架，通过将多模型智能体轨迹转化为共享决策景观，识别“陷阱”区域和“产出核心”，并利用陷阱感知恢复流程提升SWE-bench基准的解决率（最高从41.0%提升至44.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试虽记录丰富的交互轨迹，但评估方式仅简化为通过率或奖励分数，忽略了轨迹中蕴含的结构化决策信息，无法深入理解模型行为差异和失败模式。

Method: 1) 对每个任务，从多模型轨迹池中构建基于可观察动作-状态的有向图，屏蔽模型身份信息；2) 基于结果信息标注“产出核心”和“陷阱区域”；3) 用三个事件（Access、Trap exposure、Repair）总结每条轨迹；4) 利用陷阱区域设计运行时检测器，在命中历史陷阱状态时切换轻量级续写策略进行修复。

Result: 在五个基准分割上，TraceGraph揭示了聚合分数无法体现的导航差异，不同分割在“避免陷阱”与“从陷阱中恢复”上的侧重不同。在SWE-bench上，最佳单因素策略将官方解决率从40.4%提升至43.5%（每提供者触发子集）及从41.0%提升至44.8%（共触发实例）。

Conclusion: TraceGraph为分析智能体基准测试提供了过程级词汇，能够揭示模型在共享决策景观上的分歧点，并利用失败区域指导下游性能改进。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks increasingly record rich interaction trajectories, yet evaluation often reduces each rollout to a pass rate or reward score. We introduce TraceGraph, a graph-based framework that turns released multi-model agent trajectories into shared decision landscapes. For each task, TraceGraph builds a graph over observable action-observation states from pooled rollouts before model identity is introduced. It then overlays outcome-informed productive cores and trap regions, and summarizes each rollout with three events: Access, Trap exposure, and Repair. Across trajectories spanning five benchmark splits, TraceGraph profiles reveal navigation differences hidden by aggregate scores and show that splits differ in whether they reward avoiding traps or recovering from them. The same TraceGraph landscape also motivates a trap-aware recovery pipeline for SWE-bench: aruntime detector fires on states matching historical trap regions, then lightweight continuation policies are evaluated from the same prefix. On fired states, the best pooled single-factor policy raises official resolved rate from 40.4% to 43.5% on the per-provider fired subset and from 41.0% to 44.8% on common-fired instances, with provider-specific active components. Overall, TraceGraph provides a process vocabulary for asking what agent benchmarks test, where models diverge on a shared landscape, and how failure regions can guide downstream improvement.

</details>


### [126] [Diagnosing Failure Modes of Shared-State Collaboration in Resource-Constrained Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31354)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了模块化视觉推理系统中，弱模型（4B-8B）在共享工作记忆下的失败模式，发现共享工作区会放大幻觉而非解决它，主要失败模式为噪声强化和政策崩溃，并指出资源受限智能体的瓶颈在于通信保真度而非推理深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化视觉推理系统在多步协作中依赖共享工作记忆，但在低容量场景下中间状态演化的失败机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CoSee审计框架，形式化读-写-验证循环以追踪文档视觉问答中的信息流，并在多页、图表和网页基准上进行分析。

Result: 发现两种主要失败模式：噪声强化（无根据的笔记被重用为证据）和政策崩溃（上下文增加导致模型给出欠具体的短答案）。增加计算量可能与性能负相关。

Conclusion: 资源受限智能体的瓶颈在于通信保真度而非推理深度，研究为可靠模块化设计提供了踪迹级诊断和机制基线。

Abstract: Modular visual reasoning systems increasingly rely on shared working memory for multi-step collaboration, yet the failure dynamics of intermediate state evolution in low-capacity regimes remain underexplored. We study failure modes of collaborative reasoning with weak learners (4B--8B models) through the lens of noise accumulation. We introduce CoSee, an auditing framework that formalizes the read-write-verify loop to trace information flow in document visual question answering. Across multi-page, chart, and web-based benchmarks, we find a counter-intuitive degradation: naive shared workspaces often amplify hallucinations rather than resolve them. We identify two dominant failure modes: Noise Reinforcement, where ungrounded notes are reused as evidence, and Policy Collapse, where added context shifts the model toward under-specified, short-form answers. Using cost-accuracy Pareto frontiers, we show that increased compute can correlate negatively with performance without explicit verification. Our findings suggest that for resource-constrained agents, the bottleneck lies not in reasoning depth but in communication fidelity, providing trace-level diagnostics and a mechanistic baseline for reliable modular design.

</details>


### [127] [Learning to Adapt: Self-Improving Web Agent via Cognitive-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31365)
*Weile Chen,Bingchen Miao,Qifan Yu,Wendong Bu,Guoming Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Shengyu Zhang,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCALE框架通过Selector、Predictor、Judger三个对抗角色实现自主认知探索，结合SCALE-Hop图探索策略和SCALE-20k大规模数据集，显著提升了多模态大模型在复杂动态Web环境中的表现与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体依赖手工设计的执行流程或昂贵的专家轨迹，难以适应复杂动态环境，亟需一种自主发现局限、扩展认知边界的方法。

Method: 提出SCALE框架，包含三个对抗角色（Selector、Predictor、Judger）进行自主探索；提出SCALE-Hop图探索策略实现全局规划避免局部陷阱；构建SCALE-20k大规模真实网站数据集。

Result: 在多种Web环境中显著提升了多个MLLMs的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCALE框架为构建真正自主且自适应的Web智能体提供了可扩展、可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to promising progress in web agents. However, existing web agents often rely on handcrafted execution pipelines or expensive expert trajectories, limiting their adaptability to complex, dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose SCALE (Self-Cognitive-Aware Learning and Exploration), which leverages three adversarial roles, Selector, Predictor, and Judger to autonomously discover the agent's limitations and expand its cognitive boundaries through environmental exploration. Moreover, we propose SCALE-Hop, a graph exploration strategy that facilitates global planning and helps agents avoid local exploration traps. To further support learning, we construct SCALE-20k, a large-scale dataset collected from 19 real-world websites, containing diverse task types and structured demonstrations generated from SCALE's exploration traces. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the performance and generalization of multiple MLLMs in various web environments. Our framework offers a scalable and generalizable solution for building truly autonomous and adaptive web agents.

</details>


### [128] [HypoAgent: An Agentic Framework for Interactive Abductive Hypothesis Generation over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31370)
*Yisen Gao,Yixi Cai,Tianshi Zheng,Jiaxin Bai,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HypoAgent是一个基于多智能体框架的交互式知识图谱溯因推理系统，通过意图识别、假设生成和根因分析三个智能体协同工作，在多种设置下达到了最优语义相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱溯因推理方法在交互式场景中存在不足：难以在多轮对话中理解不断演变的自然语言意图，且当生成的假设失败时缺乏细粒度诊断能力。

Method: 提出HypoAgent框架，包含三个智能体：(1) 意图识别智能体，将用户话语和对话历史转化为可执行的KG条件；(2) 假设生成智能体，根据用户意图进行可控假设生成；(3) 根因分析智能体，诊断不可靠的假设片段并通过KG邻域探查询找支持的优化方案。

Result: 在常识和生物医学领域知识图谱上的实验表明，HypoAgent在单轮、多轮和无条件设置下均达到了最先进的语义相似度。

Conclusion: HypoAgent通过多智能体协作有效解决了交互式知识图谱溯因推理中的意图理解和假设诊断问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Abductive reasoning over knowledge graphs aims to generate logical hypotheses that explain observed entities or facts. Existing controllable hypothesis generation methods allow users to guide this process with explicit conditions, but they remain limited in interactive settings: they struggle to ground evolving natural-language intents across multi-turn dialogues and provide little fine-grained diagnosis when generated hypotheses fail. To address these limitations, we propose HypoAgent, an Agentic framework for interactive abductive Hypothesis Generation over knowledge graphs. HypoAgent integrates three agents: an Intent Recognition Agent that grounds user utterances and dialogue history into executable KG conditions, a Hypothesis Generation Agent that performs controllable hypothesis generation according to the extracted user intention, and a Root Cause Analysis Agent that diagnoses unreliable hypothesis fragments and leverages KG neighborhood probing to identify supported refinements. Experiments on commonsense and biomedical domain-specific knowledge graphs demonstrate that HypoAgent achieves state-of-the-art semantic similarity under single-turn, multi-turn, and unconditional settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/HypoAgent.

</details>


### [129] [FAM-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Condition-Aware Food-as-Medicine Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31410)
*Mingyang Mao,Bhargav Rishi Medisetti,Utkarsh Grover,Tanvir Ibrahim,Wenyan Li,Tingting Zhang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FAM-Bench基准，包含2500个营养专家验证的实例，覆盖13种饮食相关健康状况，用于评估AI模型在食品作为药物场景下的健康感知推理能力，包括菜肴适宜性判断和比较分析两个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品AI基准主要评估菜肴识别、食谱理解、营养估算等任务，缺乏对模型能否根据具体健康状况判断食物选择是否合适的测试。

Method: 构建了FAM-Bench多模态基准，包含2500个专家验证实例，涉及13种健康状况，设计了菜肴级适宜性评估（判断菜肴是否适合特定健康状况）和比较菜肴分析（对四个候选菜肴进行健康适宜性排序）两个任务。

Result: 创建了一个标准化的测试平台，要求模型整合食材证据、视觉烹饪线索和临床营养约束来进行健康感知推理。

Conclusion: FAM-Bench填补了食品AI在健康决策层面的评估空白，为语言模型和视觉语言模型提供了接地气的健康推理标准化测试。

Abstract: Food-as-Medicine requires models to reason beyond what a dish is or what nutrition it contains: they must decide whether a concrete food choice is appropriate for a specific health condition. Existing food AI benchmarks primarily evaluate dish recognition, recipe understanding, nutrient estimation, or general nutrition question answering, leaving this health-aware decision layer largely untested. We introduce FAM-Bench, a multi-modal Food-as-Medicine benchmark with 2500 nutrition-expert-verified instances across 13 diet-related health conditions. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: dish-level suitability assessment, where models judge whether a dish is suitable for a condition from its image and ingredient list, and comparative dish analysis, where models rank four candidate dishes by condition-specific suitability. Both tasks require integrating ingredient evidence, visual preparation cues, and clinical nutrition constraints, providing a standardized testbed for grounded health-aware reasoning in language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [130] [Answer-Set-Programming-based Abstractions for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31444)
*Rafael Bankosegger,Thomas Eiter,Johannes Oetsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用回答集编程（ASP）替代Prolog，在CARCASS框架中实现关系强化学习（RRL）的抽象，实验表明该方法在具有领域知识的场景下效果良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习面临巨大状态空间带来的学习与泛化挑战，关系和逻辑抽象是解决之道。CARCASS框架原用Prolog实现，但Prolog并非完全声明式语言，作者因此尝试使用更富表达力且完全声明式的ASP来替代。

Method: 基于ASP重新实现CARCASS框架中的逻辑抽象，在Blocks World和Minigrid两个领域进行案例研究，与原始Prolog实现进行对比评估。

Result: 基于ASP的CARCASS实现能够有效构建强化学习所需的抽象，在两个测试领域均展现出良好性能，特别是当领域知识可用时优势更为明显。

Conclusion: ASP为CARCASS框架提供了一种有前景的实现方式，有助于在强化学习中构建有效的抽象，尤其适用于具备领域知识的场景。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) enables autonomous agents to learn policies from experience, but realistic problems often involve enormous state spaces, making learning and generalisation challenging. Abstraction and approximation are therefore essential. Relational Reinforcement Learning (RRL) offers a way to reason about objects and their relations, and the CARCASS framework by Martijn van Otterlo demonstrates how logical representations can model Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) in first-order domains. Originally implemented in Prolog, CARCASS leverages domain knowledge to create powerful abstractions. We explore Answer-Set Programming (ASP), which is a rich and, contrary to Prolog, fully declarative modelling language, to realise CARCASS abstractions. We evaluate our ASP-based implementation in case studies of two domains, viz. Blocks World and Minigrid. Our results indicate that CARCASS with ASP provides a promising approach to constructing abstractions for RL, especially when domain knowledge is available.

</details>


### [131] [AutoSci: A Memory-Centric Agentic System for the Full Scientific Research Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31468)
*Weitong Qian,Beicheng Xu,Zhongao Xie,Bowen Fan,Guozheng Tang,Jiale Chen,Xinzhe Wu,Mingtian Yang,Chenyang Di,Jiajun Li,Lingching Tung,Peichao Lai,Yifei Xia,Ziyi Guo,Yanwei Xu,Yanzhao Qin,Shaoduo Gan,Xupeng Miao,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSci是一个面向完整科研生命周期的记忆中心型智能体系统，包含四个核心模块：SciMem（结构化科研记忆）、SciFlow（五阶段生命周期执行）、SciDAG（DAG形状多智能体算子）和SciEvolve（反馈驱动的自我进化），能够实现科研项目的执行、记忆和持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM科研智能体系统无法同时满足支持完整科研生命周期、维持结构化持久记忆以及随时间自我改进研究流程这三个关键需求，存在明显缺口。

Method: 提出AutoSci系统，采用记忆中心架构，围绕四个模块设计：(1) SciMem：分离长期知识记忆与活跃研究记忆的图式驱动记忆系统；(2) SciFlow：通过状态、上下文、验证、反馈和编排五种控制机制执行五阶段科研生命周期；(3) SciDAG：利用DAG形状的多智能体算子和可复用阶段模板增强复杂技能；(4) SciEvolve：将用户、实验、评审和外部环境的反馈信号转化为对前三个模块的版本化更新。

Result: 论文提出了完整的系统设计、开源代码仓库（https://github.com/skyllwt/AutoSci），但摘要未报告具体实验评估结果。

Conclusion: AutoSci作为持久化科研环境，能够跨科研项目执行、记忆并自我进化，填补了统一自动化科研系统的空白。

Abstract: Scientific research has traditionally been human-intensive, requiring researchers to coordinate literature, ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and review responses across long project cycles. The rise of LLM-based scientific agents creates an opportunity to automate this process. Such a system must support the full research lifecycle, maintain structured persistent memory across projects, and improve its own research procedures over time. However, existing systems either partially satisfy or fail to satisfy these requirements, leaving a gap for a unified automated scientific research system. As a result, we present AutoSci, a memory-centric agentic system for the full scientific research lifecycle. AutoSci is organized around four modules. SciMem provides schema-governed research memory, separating Long-Term Knowledge Memory for reusable scientific knowledge from Active Research Memory for project-level artifacts such as ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and reviews. SciFlow executes a five-stage lifecycle from literature understanding to rebuttal through a harness that controls state, context, verification, feedback, and orchestration. SciDAG augments difficult skills with DAG-shaped multi-agent operators and reusable stage-specific templates. SciEvolve converts feedback signals from users, experiments, reviews, and external environments into versioned updates to SciMem organization, SciFlow skills, and SciDAG templates. Together, these modules make AutoSci a persistent research environment that can execute, remember, and evolve across research projects. The code repository is available at https://github.com/skyllwt/AutoSci.

</details>


### [132] [LinTree: Improving LLM Reasoning with Explicitly Structured Search Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31492)
*Liwei Kang,Yee Whye Teh,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在推理过程中生成的中间轨迹可视为线性化搜索树，但仅凭原始搜索历史并不总能优于启发式搜索；通过添加显式的父指针来表示线性化树结构（LinTree），可以同时提升任务性能和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM推理轨迹中隐式表示的搜索树结构是否能够带来相对于传统启发式搜索的优势，以及如何更好地利用搜索历史信息。

Method: 在Blocks World、网格导航、Sokoban三个受控推理环境中，比较了基于轨迹条件的推理策略与基于LLM启发式的最佳优先搜索，并提出了显式添加父指针（LinTree）来表示线性化树结构的方法。

Result: 原始搜索历史不足以可靠地超越启发式搜索；而添加显式父指针表示树结构后，模型在任务性能和搜索效率上均优于隐式推理模型和LLM启发式引导搜索。

Conclusion: 搜索历史在树结构被显式化时最为有用，这启示了未来应设计更具结构感知能力的表示方法以提升LLM推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve reasoning problems by generating intermediate traces that explore and revise partial solutions. From a search perspective, these traces can be viewed as linearized search trees, where the model extends a partial solution, abandons it when it fails, and backtracks to try alternatives. Compared with traditional heuristic-guided search, such a policy has a potential advantage: it conditions on the whole search trace rather than only on the current local state. We first test whether LLMs utilize this advantage by comparing trace-conditioned reasoning policies against best-first search equipped with an LLM heuristic that only observes the current local state. Across three controlled reasoning environments, Blocks World, grid Navigation, and Sokoban, we find that raw access to search history alone is not enough to reliably outperform heuristic search. We then study one possible reason: in LLM reasoning traces, the underlying search tree is only implicitly represented, and when the model backtracks or switches branches, the trace does not explicitly identify which earlier search state is being revisited. We show that adding simple parent pointers to explicitly represent the linearized tree (LinTree) structure improves both task performance and search efficiency relative to implicit reasoning models and LLM-heuristic-guided search. These results suggest that search history becomes most useful when its tree structure is made explicit, motivating more structure-aware representations for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [133] [Choosing the Lens: Strategic Perspective Activation in Context-Dependent Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31581)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文依赖论证框架（CDAFs），扩展Dung的理论，通过一个击败函数在不同上下文中决定哪些攻击有效。引入基于相关集ρ和优先级π的视角标记特化方法，将相关集作为智能体的行动空间。通过小例子展示，智能体的目标论证在完全相关单射优先级下被拒绝，但在部分激活下被接受，且任何VAF受众无法复现。定义了ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION决策问题，并记录了基线复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化方法无法直接捕捉智能体通过影响外部体制（regime）来操纵论证结果的策略性杠杆，因此需要一种新的框架来建模不同上下文下的论证评估。

Method: 提出CDAFs框架，引入击败函数按上下文决定攻击有效性；通过视角标记特化方法，从相关集ρ和优先级π推导击败函数，将相关集作为智能体的行动空间。

Result: 在例子中，目标论证在所有完全相关单射优先级下被拒绝，但在部分激活下被接受，且这种部分激活效果无法被任何VAF受众复现。定义了ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION问题并给出基线复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 本研究为上下文敏感的论证评估提供了新框架，但紧密的复杂度界限和多智能体变体仍有待探索。

Abstract: The same arguments often need to be evaluated under different external regimes. An agent with influence over the regime has a strategic lever that standard formalisms do not directly capture. We introduce context-dependent argumentation frameworks (CDAFs), an extension of Dung's theory in which a defeat function determines, per context, which attacks succeed. A perspective-labeled specialisation derives the defeat function from a relevance set $ρ$ and a priority $π$. The relevance set is the agent's action space. In a small worked example, the agent's target argument is rejected under every full-relevance injective priority, yet accepted under partial activations, one of which no VAF audience can mirror. We define the corresponding decision problem, ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION, and record baseline complexity bounds. Tight bounds and multi-agent variants are left open.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong,Yifan Hou,Shaobo Cui,Antoine Bosselut,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了测试时计算策略在视觉-语言模型中的应用，发现特征启发法和多数投票法效果有限，根源在于预测多样性不足。为此提出基于熵的ETTC方法，通过预测熵选择最可信结果，在模型集成场景中显著优于多数投票法和最佳单模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTC策略在LLM中已被证明能提升推理能力，但在VLM中的应用和收益尚未得到充分探索，现有方法效果有限且缺乏理论分析。

Method: 提出ETTC（基于熵的测试时计算），通过计算预测熵来衡量模型置信度，选择熵最低（最确定）的预测结果。在单模型场景退化为多数投票，在模型集成场景利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型。

Result: ETTC在温和假设下理论上优于多数投票法，实验上一致超越投票法和最佳单模型。小模型可以与大型模型协同增强，实现标准策略无法达到的集成收益。

Conclusion: 特征启发式TTC方法无效，多数投票法因预测多样性不足仅带来有限提升；而基于熵的ETTC方法能有效利用模型间置信度差异，在模型集成场景下实现更优性能。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>


### [135] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个深度学习框架，利用下肢步态运动学直接预测髋部肌肉力和关节力矩，并在统一协议下比较了LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种序列模型。基于60名健康成人的步态数据，Transformer在健康人群中表现最佳（髋部肌肉力预测R²=0.819，关节力矩预测R²=0.862），并在零样本条件下对股骨头坏死患者保持中等预测能力（R²=0.537和0.569）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于肌肉骨骼仿真估算髋部肌肉力和关节力矩的方法虽然有效，但耗时长且难以在临床环境中应用，因此需要一种更高效、更易部署的替代方案。

Method: 收集60名健康成年人在三种节拍器引导步频下的步态数据，以10个双侧下肢关节角度为输入，OpenSim导出的髋部肌肉力和关节力矩为参考输出。在统一协议下训练LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种模型，并将最优模型直接零样本测试于9名股骨头坏死患者。

Result: Transformer在健康人群中表现最优：髋部肌肉力预测RMSE=1.33 N/kg、R²=0.819；髋关节力矩预测RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg、R²=0.862。零样本外部验证中，Transformer对ONFH患者保持中等预测能力（R²=0.537和0.569）。

Conclusion: 从步态运动学估算髋部动力学是可行的，Transformer可作为强基线模型，但需在更广泛的病理数据上验证并提升泛化能力后才能应用于临床。

Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

</details>


### [136] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对五种Transformer模型进行LoRA微调，发现合成不诚实行为可在早期层（1-3层）被线性探针以近完美AUC检测到，且不诚实表征在深层逐步固化。Gemma-2模型表现出高维表征稳定性，而Pythia/Llama/Qwen则出现表征坍缩。研究证实了线性表征假说，并表明通过少量监督微调即可快速植入鲁棒且领域不变的不诚实表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗性对齐（模型保持准确内部表征却故意输出错误结果）是AI安全的核心挑战。为在可控条件下研究习得性欺骗的表征基础，作者引入合成不诚实（通过对错误答案直接优化诱导）作为测试bed。

Method: 对Pythia-1.4B、Gemma-2-2B/9B、Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B五种模型使用LoRA微调得到诚实与不诚实变体。训练线性/MLP探针检测平均池化隐藏状态，进行噪声鲁棒性测试和机械分析（Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性等）。

Result: 线性探针在四类架构的1-3层达到AUC≥0.99（Pythia峰值仅0.705）。Logistic回归探针匹配或超越MLP探针。TruthfulQA训练的探针可零损失泛化到MMLU。Gemma-2在噪声下表现稳定。发现两种表征机制：Pythia/Llama/Qwen的表征坍缩 vs Gemma-2的高维保持。所有模型的不诚实方向在深层逐步固化。

Conclusion: 通过适度监督微调即可快速嵌入鲁棒、领域不变的不诚实表征，这对基于激活的监控机制具有重要启示。最优校准（ECE<0.01）可在1-4层实现。

Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

</details>


### [137] [A Novel Evaluation Metric for Unsupervised Learning in AIS-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection: MADQI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30388)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiyan,Raza Hasan,Shakeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对海事AIS数据异常检测的系统框架，并设计了一个无需标注数据的复合质量指标MADQI，用于评估无监督异常检测模型的性能。在AIS数据集上，该框架的MADQI得分为80.37%，其中ECE和ARC分别达到0.907和1.000，证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督学习算法（如Isolation Forest）在检测船舶异常行为时缺乏系统且有意义的评估指标，现有方法难以在没有标注数据的情况下衡量异常检测性能。

Method: 提出了MADQI复合指标，包含四个子指标：异常率一致性（ARC）、物理合理性评分（PPS）、分数分布分离度（SDS）和极端案例证据（ECE）；通过多块评估和自适应缩放进行自动归一化，并利用Haversine距离计算分析AIS数据的空间和行为特征。

Result: 在AIS数据集上，MADQI得分为80.37%；ECE得分为0.907，ARC得分为1.000，表明框架在检测极端异常和保持异常率一致性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架为海事AIS数据中的无监督异常检测提供了一种可靠且有意义的评估方法，实验结果表明其具有较好的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new systematic framework for detecting anomalies in maritime Automatic Identification System (AIS) datasets. These anomalies include abnormal vessel behaviours related to speed, position jumps, time gaps, and turn angles. Although unsupervised learning algorithms such as Isolation Forest are widely used for detecting anomalous vessel movements, they often lack systematic and meaningful evaluation measures. To address this limitation, we propose a novel quality metric called Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI). The prosed MADQI is a composite index designed to evaluate the anomaly detection performance of machine learning models without requiring labelled data. The proposed framework uses Haversine distance calculations to analyse AIS datasets and identify anomalies based on their spatial and behavioural characteristics. The proposed MADQI evaluation framework integrates four interconnected metrics: Anomaly Rate Consistency (ARC), Physical Plausibility Score (PPS), Score Distribution Separation (SDS), and Extreme Case Evidence (ECE). These metrics are combined through automatic normalisation using multi-chunk evaluation and adaptive scaling techniques. Experimental results on the AIS dataset show that the proposed framework achieved a MADQI score of 80.37%, demonstrating its effectiveness for unsupervised anomaly detection. In particular, the algorithm performed strongly in identifying abnormal vessel behaviour. Among the individual MADQI components, ECE and ARC achieved scores of 0.907 and 1.000, respectively, indicating excellent capability in detecting extreme anomalies and maintaining anomaly rate consistency. Overall, these results are encouraging and demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a reliable and meaningful approach for evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection in maritime AIS data.

</details>


### [138] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NumLeak框架，揭示顶级大模型在数值基准测试中存在严重的记忆泄露问题——模型对Fama-French市场超额收益等关键经济指标的回忆准确率高达r=0.97-0.99，但在真正的新数据上解析率骤降至21-57%，且发现日志概率排名比开放式生成更能检测记忆化。最后提出单行系统提示防御可阻挡99.8%的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数值基准可能已被模型在预训练中记忆，导致基于日期的评估无法区分模型是真的具备推理能力还是仅仅回忆了训练数据，因此需要一套系统化的框架来量化这一记忆泄露问题。

Method: 提出NumLeak测量框架，结合API边界探针（对生产模型进行黑盒探测）和白盒受控验证（在开源因果语言模型上进行）。通过多种子池化皮尔逊相关系数、解析率、日志概率排名等指标，系统评估模型对经济指标、失业率、通胀、温度等数值数据的记忆程度，并设计拒绝-回忆不对称分析和残差化回归来验证记忆通道假说。

Result: 顶级前沿LLM对Fama-French市场超额收益回忆r=0.97-0.99，五个兄弟因子误差在0.15个基点以内；对失业率、通胀、温度等也有类似高保真度。但在新发布数据上解析率崩塌至21-57%，回答的月份仍保持r≈0.99，符合记忆通道的拒绝-回忆不对称预测。白盒实验复现了剂量反应关系，日志概率排名能检测到开放生成遗漏的记忆化。Sonnet模型的"日期-市场情绪"回归与真实Mkt-RF的r=0.74，残差化后骤降至r=0.02。

Conclusion: 封闭API的黑盒探针严重低估了模型的记忆通道，而日志概率排名是更敏感的检测手段。提出的单行系统提示防御以极低效用成本即可阻挡99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击，表明存在简单有效的缓解措施。

Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

</details>


### [139] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LongDS基准，用于测试智能体在长序列、多轮数据分析中维持和演化分析状态的能力。68个任务、2225轮对话，覆盖6个领域。最佳模型准确率仅48.45%，后期性能下降近47个百分点，长程错误占52%-69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多评估孤立或短交互任务，缺乏对智能体在长序列数据分析中跟踪演化上下文能力的测试。

Method: 从真实Kaggle notebooks构建68个长序列任务，包含2225轮对话，覆盖6个领域。任务设计围绕状态演化模式（反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合），平均依赖跨度11.3轮。

Result: 最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%，后期相比早期性能下降近47个百分点；长程错误占失败的52%-69%；增加智能体步骤不一定提升性能。

Conclusion: 长程数据分析的关键瓶颈在于维持正确的分析状态而非增加交互次数，LongDS可用于推动可靠的长程智能体数据分析研究。

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

</details>


### [140] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次正式定义了概率化标签排序中的校准问题，构建了涵盖完整排序、子排序和Top-k排序的层次化校准框架，并通过实验发现主流模型在校准性上普遍不足，且校准度与基准准确率存在强相关但不完全一致的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准对于可靠决策至关重要，但以往研究仅关注分类和回归任务，而标签排序（如RLHF中的奖励模型）中的校准问题尚未被正式定义和研究。

Method: 形式化定义了标签排序的校准概念，构建了包含完整排序校准、子排序校准和Top-k校准的层次化框架，并证明了它们之间的蕴含关系及不可比性。

Result: 完整排序校准蕴含其他校准但反之不成立；子排序与Top-k校准不可比；主流标签排序模型校准性较差；RLHF奖励模型的校准度与基准准确率强相关但不完全一致。

Conclusion: 校准捕捉了超越Top-1准确率的有意义质量维度，未来需研究校准不良的下游影响及开发校正方法。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>


### [141] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出黑盒LLM蒸馏不应仅用输出相似度评估，而需引入有界行为不可区分性概念，并通过实验证明语义保真度不足以衡量蒸馏效果，需要对抗性与类别感知的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出匹配（语义相似性或任务一致性），但输出相似并不代表学生模型与教师模型在行为上不可区分。本文旨在提出更严格的评估标准，捕捉蒸馏过程中残留的行为差异。

Method: 形式化定义了(ε,q,t,A)-有界行为不可区分性，涵盖对抗优势、查询预算、计算约束和对抗器类别。在Qwen和Llama教师-学生对上，使用5000提示的行为探测套件，结合LoRA蒸馏、对抗鉴别器、配对教师识别和查询预算实验进行系统评估。

Result: LoRA蒸馏提升了语义相似度（Qwen从0.788到0.862，Llama从0.814到0.874）。但对抗评估显示鉴别器仍保有非零优势，行为差异集中在风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术提示上。Qwen的区分优势从0.158降至0.081。查询预算实验表明分歧引导采样未必优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义保真度有用但不充分；黑盒LLM蒸馏需要结合有界性、对抗性和类别感知的评估方法。

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $ε$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class.
  We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [142] [VeriGate: Verifier-Gated Step-Level Supervision for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30451)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriGate是一种改进的GRPO方法，通过引入验证器门控的步骤级奖励机制，解决原有方法在奖励稀疏时梯度消失的问题，在数学推理任务上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO仅使用结果级奖励（稀疏监督），当所有采样轨迹获得相同奖励时梯度消失，且缺乏步骤级信用分配，限制了探索和稳健推理的学习。

Method: 三个设计：1）验证器门控机制，仅在结果奖励退化时使用过程监督；2）将PRM步骤得分转化为未来累积奖励，实现延续感知的信用分配；3）将奖励转化为组归一化的token级优势，提供细粒度信用分配且不易被奖励攻击。

Result: 在MATH上训练，1.5B和7B Qwen2.5-Instruct模型在六个推理基准上平均准确率分别提升约20%和12%，显著减少零梯度失败和奖励攻击行为。

Conclusion: VeriGate通过验证器门控的步骤级GRPO有效解决了稀疏奖励问题，相比纯结果GRPO和PRM基线，提升了推理质量和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is an effective recipe for training reasoning models with verifier-based outcome rewards, but its supervision is sparse: when all sampled trajectories for a prompt receive the same verifier reward, the group-relative advantage collapses to zero and learning stalls. Outcome-only rewards also provide no step-level credit assignment, limiting exploration and making it harder to learn robust reasoning. We present VeriGate (Verifier-Gated Step-Level GRPO), a verifier-gated extension of GRPO that addresses these limitations with three design choices. First, VeriGate keeps the verifier in charge whenever verifier rewards induce a meaningful preference among sampled trajectories, and uses process supervision only when verifier rewards are degenerate. Second, instead of collapsing Process Reward Model (PRM) step scores into a single trajectory reward, VeriGate converts them into future-cumulated rewards to assign continuation-aware credit. Third, VeriGate transforms these rewards into group-normalized token-level advantages, restoring informative gradients and fine-grained credit assignment while remaining less susceptible to reward hacking than methods that optimize aggregated PRM scores. Empirically, training on MATH with 1.5B and 7B Qwen2.5-Instruct models and evaluating on six reasoning benchmarks, VeriGate improves average accuracy by about 20% and 12% for 1.5B and 7B models respectively, substantially reduces zero-gradient failures, decreases reward-hacking behavior, and improves reasoning quality relative to outcome-only GRPO and PRM-as-outcome baselines.

</details>


### [143] [A Unified Framework for Gradient Aggregation in Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30452)
*Zeou Hu,Kelvin Ho,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多目标优化中梯度聚合的统一框架，建立了收敛到Pareto驻点的最优收敛率，通过充分对齐条件统一了现有方法，并提出了新的capped MGDA算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标优化梯度聚合方法动机各异、逐案分析，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其收敛性质。

Method: 提出了充分对齐条件，证明凸包中非冲突方向是收敛的充分条件；通过对偶锥投影确保可行性；从原始优化视角统一现有算法；基于CVaR提出capped MGDA。

Result: 建立了梯度聚合方法的统一框架，推导了最优收敛率，capped MGDA在对抗联邦学习中表现出鲁棒性，并通过合成问题和实际基准验证了理论。

Conclusion: 非冲突方向是梯度聚合收敛的核心条件，所提出的统一框架为设计新算法提供了理论基础，capped MGDA是一种鲁棒有效的新方法。

Abstract: Many machine learning problems involve multiple inherent trade-offs that are best addressed by gradient-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) algorithms. Existing methods are often proposed with various motivations, analyzed case by case, and differ algorithmically in how the component gradients are aggregated at each step. In this work, we develop a unifying framework for gradient aggregation in MOO, establishing (optimal) rates of convergence to Pareto stationarity, the standard measure of performance in MOO. Central to our analysis is a sufficient alignment condition, from which we derive a theorem showing that non-conflicting directions, when chosen within the convex hull of gradients, form a fundamental sufficient condition for convergence. We further show that feasibility can be ensured through projection onto the dual cone, broadening the scope of methods that admit convergence guarantees. In parallel, we present a primal optimization perspective of gradient aggregation that encompasses established algorithms, clarifies their theoretical relationships, and enables the design of new variants. As an illustration, we introduce capped MGDA, derived from a CVaR-based formulation, and demonstrate its robustness in adversarial federated learning. Finally, we validate our theory through experiments on synthetic problems and practical benchmarks.

</details>


### [144] [Scalable Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via State Augmentation and Consensus for Separable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30461)
*Santiago Amaya-Corredor,Miguel Calvo-Fullana,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合状态增广策略学习与分布式对偶变量共识的约束多智能体强化学习方法，在保持可扩展性的同时实现全局约束协调，实验证明该方法可扩展到数千智能体而CTDE基线仅能处理数十个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立学习无法产生可行解，因为智能体无法确定各自对全局约束的贡献；CTDE方法复杂度随智能体数量呈二次增长，难以扩展到大规模系统。

Method: 每个智能体学习一个离线增广策略（以本地状态和编码约束反馈的对偶变量为条件）；执行时通过轻量级邻居间共识机制对拉格朗日乘子达成一致。

Result: 共识误差有界且随图连通性和共识轮次增加而减小；约束违反有界；方法在训练和执行阶段均线性扩展；在智能电网需求响应任务中扩展到数千智能体，而CTDE基线仅能处理数十个。

Conclusion: 共识协调对可行性是必要的：没有共识时智能体只能无限期推迟需求（退化非解）；有共识时智能体收敛到共享对偶变量并同时满足电网约束和需求满足。

Abstract: We present a distributed approach for constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) that combines state-augmented policy learning with distributed consensus over dual variables. Our method targets systems where agents have separable dynamics but must coordinate to satisfy global resource constraints, a setting in which, as we demonstrate empirically, independent learning fails to produce feasible solutions because agents cannot determine appropriate individual contributions toward collective constraint satisfaction. The key technical contribution is showing that lightweight neighbor-to-neighbor consensus over Lagrange multipliers suffices for globally coordinated constraint enforcement while preserving the scalability of independent training. Each agent learns a single augmented policy offline, conditioned on both its local state and a dual variable encoding constraint feedback. During execution, agents reach agreement on this dual variable through local communication alone. We prove that under mild connectivity assumptions, the consensus error among agents' multipliers is bounded, and show that this translates to a bounded constraint violation that decreases with graph connectivity and the number of consensus rounds. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches, whose complexity grows at least quadratically with agent count, our method scales linearly in both training and execution. Experiments on smart grid demand response demonstrate that consensus coordination is \emph{essential for feasibility}: without it, agents satisfy grid capacity constraints only by indefinitely postponing demand, a degenerate non-solution. With consensus, agents converge to a shared dual variable and satisfy both grid constraints and demand fulfillment, scaling to thousands of agents while CTDE baselines are limited to dozens.

</details>


### [145] [idSCD: Identifying Training Datasets through Semantic Correlation Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30462)
*Andrada Gobeaja,Ionut Hodoroaga,Elena Burceanu,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于语义相关性描述符（SCD）的白盒方法，通过模型内部学习到的语义相关结构来识别数据集是否属于训练混合，在多项实验中以高ROC-AUC超越现有黑盒与白盒基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集成员推断方法多依赖行为或分布证据（如置信度、损失、生成样本等），缺乏利用模型内部语义相关结构进行推断的研究。本文旨在证明数据集会在模型训练中留下特有的虚假相关痕迹，并据此实现数据集级成员推断。

Method: 提出语义相关性描述符（SCD），以白盒方式捕获模型学到的语义相关结构，使其在不同数据集混合间可比较。设计基于SCD的成员评分，仅需目标数据集及其独立SCD即可判断是否属于训练混合，无需留一法模型。

Result: 在自然语言推理、情感分类、医学文本分类三个场景中，SCD方法平均性能最高、标准差最低，超越RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA等黑盒基线及SIF白盒基线，最大相对增益超过60% ROC-AUC。

Conclusion: 数据集成员身份可通过内部语义相关结构追溯，SCD方法在数据集具有独特语义特征时效果尤为显著，为数据集级成员推断提供了新的白盒范式。

Abstract: Can a dataset be recognized from the spurious correlations it induces during training? We argue that datasets leave dataset-specific traces in a model's learned semantic correlation structure: incidental regularities that are predictive within a dataset, but not causal for the underlying task, can be internalized during training. We use this insight to study dataset-level membership inference, moving beyond existing methods that rely on behavioral or distributional evidence such as confidence scores, losses, margins, generated samples, or query responses. We introduce a white-box semantic fingerprinting approach based on semantic correlation descriptors (SCDs), which capture the semantic correlation structure learned by a model and make it comparable across dataset mixtures. In a controlled leave-one-dataset-out diagnostic, SCDs recover dataset-specific changes and perfectly separate matching from non-matching dataset pairs. We then propose a practical SCD-based membership score that tests whether a target dataset is part of a model's training mixture using only the model's SCD and the target dataset's standalone SCD, without requiring leave-one-dataset-out models. Across three diverse experimental settings, with dataset groups for natural language inference, emotion classification, and medical text classification, we test both the advantages and limitations of SCD-based membership inference with different degrees of semantic separation and keyword support between dataset splits. On average, the classifier based on this score achieves the highest performance and the lowest std, outperforming black-box baselines RMIA, Attack-P, and LiRA, as well as the white-box SIF baseline. These results show that dataset membership can be traced through internal semantic correlations, with the largest relative gain exceeding 60% in ROC-AUC when dataset groups expose distinct semantic particularities.

</details>


### [146] [Can Subgraph Explanations Be Weaponized to Steal Graph Neural Networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30470)
*Ojas Nimase,Jiate Li,Yue Zhao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对严格黑盒图分类模型的模型提取攻击方法XSTEAL，利用模型解释输出（二元解释掩码）来指导蒙特卡洛边敏感性估计，在仅观察到离散类标签和解释掩码的条件下高效窃取模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图机器学习即服务平台的解释性接口虽然满足了监管透明要求，但也为模型提取攻击创造了可利用的漏洞。现有攻击方法需要概率分数或梯度等辅助信息，无法适用于仅能观察到离散类标签和二元解释掩码的严格黑盒场景。

Method: (1)利用模型解释输出指导蒙特卡洛边敏感性估计向决策边界靠近，并采用Hoeffding浓度保证估计精度；(2)利用解释子图高效缩小边界搜索空间。

Result: 在多个基准图数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著优于现有基线攻击方法。

Conclusion: 模型解释性接口会创建可被利用的攻击面，该发现为可解释AI的防御机制和政策框架提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Graph Machine Learning as a Service (GMLaaS) platforms increasingly implement explainability interfaces to meet regulatory transparency requirements. However, this transparency creates exploitable vulnerabilities for model extraction attacks. We present the first model extraction attack specifically designed for graph classification under strict black-box constraints where the attacker observes only discrete class labels and binary explanation masks (no probability scores, gradients, or confidence values). Our method (1) uses model explanation outputs to guide Monte Carlo edge sensitivity estimation toward decision boundaries, with Hoeffding concentration guarantees on estimation accuracy and (2) exploits explanation subgraphs to efficiently narrow the boundary search space. Extensive experiments on benchmark graph datasets across multiple domains demonstrate our method's superiority over comparable baselines. These findings demonstrate that such explainability interfaces create exploitable attack surfaces, informing both defensive mechanisms and policy frameworks for explainable AI mandates. The implementation code is provided in https://github.com/LabRAI/XSTEAL/.

</details>


### [147] [Universal Multiclass Transductive Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30479)
*Steve Hanneke,Hongao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究通用直推式在线分类问题，允许标签空间无界。通过引入新组合结构LCLL树，完整刻画了可学习性，并证明可学习类的最优错误率只有有界或对数增长两种可能。结果还扩展到了不可知情形和已知实例序列随机过程的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直推式在线分类（实例序列已知但标签未知）场景下，现有理论多假设标签空间有界，本文旨在刻画标签空间可能无界时的可学习性边界和最优学习率。

Method: 1. 定义通用直推式在线分类问题，放松标签空间有界假设；2. 提出新的组合结构"Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree"；3. 结合无差异性质（indifference property）刻画可学习性；4. 推导可学习类的最优错误率。

Result: 1. 可学习类的最优错误率只有两种可能：有界增长或对数增长；2. LCLL树与无差异性质共同刻画了可学习性；3. 结果扩展到不可知情形和实例序列服从已知随机过程的情形。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了标签空间无界时直推式在线分类的可学习性，发现最优率仅两种可能，并通过新组合结构给出了充要条件，为理解更广泛的在线学习场景提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the problem of universal transductive online classification with a possibly unbounded label space. This setting considers online learning, with the sequence of instances (without labels) known to the learner in advance. We say a concept class $\mathcal{H}$ is learnable if there is a learning algorithm $\mathcal{A}$, such that for every realizable sequence, the number of mistakes made by $\mathcal{A}$ grows at most sublinearly with the number of predictions. We characterize the learnability of this setting and show that there are only two possible optimal rates for the learnable classes: either bounded or increasing logarithmically. We introduce a new combinatorial structure, called ``Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree'', which, along with the indifference property, characterizes the learnability. We also extend the learnability result to the agnostic case and the case where only the stochastic process that generates the instance sequence is known.

</details>


### [148] [Discovering a Zeta Map Algorithm on Dyck Paths via Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30482)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Blake Jackson,Kyu-Hwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个小型Transformer模型学习Dyck路径上的zeta映射，并利用机械可解释性工具揭示了模型内部基于层级的计算机制，进而提炼出一个新的组合算法——scaffolding map，证明了其与zeta映射的等价性，展示了AI辅助数学发现的一个可控范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在数学发现中日益常用，但数学问题往往需要输出可独立验证的显式构造而非预测。作者希望通过一个经典组合双射（zeta映射）作为研究案例，探索如何从模型习得的计算中提取出精确、可验证的数学算法。

Method: 训练一个极小的单层单头编码器-解码器Transformer学习Dyck路径上的zeta映射，然后运用机械可解释性工具（解码器交叉注意力分析、线性探针、因果干预）分析模型内部计算机制，将识别出的信号翻译为组合学语言，提炼出scaffolding map算法，并进行数学证明。

Result: 发现模型采用了一种基于层级（level）的机制：编码器表示使得路径层级信息线性可读，解码器以结构化方式选择和遍历输入位置。由此导出的scaffolding map算法与zeta映射一致（仅反转标记习惯不同）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个AI辅助数学发现的可控实例，表明机械可解释性可以将模型行为转化为精确、人类可验证的组合算法，为机器学习在数学发现中的应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly used in mathematical discovery, but in mathematics the desired output is often not a prediction itself, but an explicit construction that can be checked independently. We study this setting through the zeta map on Dyck paths, a classical bijection in the combinatorics of the q,t-Catalan numbers. We train a deliberately small one-layer, one-head encoder-decoder transformer on this map and analyze its learned computation using mechanistic interpretability tools, including decoder cross-attention analysis, linear probing, and causal intervention. The analysis reveals a level-based mechanism: encoder representations make path levels linearly accessible, while the decoder selects and traverses input positions in a structured way. Translating these signals into combinatorics leads to the scaffolding map, an explicit peak-centered traversal algorithm for Dyck paths. We prove that this algorithm agrees with the zeta map, modulo a reversal convention in the labeling. This gives a controlled example of AI-assisted mathematical discovery in which mechanistic interpretability turns model behavior into a precise, human-verifiable combinatorial algorithm.

</details>


### [149] [Graph-Conditioned Mixture of Graph Neural Network Experts for Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30486)
*Amirhossein Ghaffari,Saeid Sheikhi,Ekaterina Gilman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GC-MoE是一种图条件混合专家框架，通过为每个节点分配个性化的冻结预训练专家组合，仅训练约17K参数的路由模块，在四个交通预测基准上提升了MAE指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时空预测模型对所有节点使用统一的主干架构，忽略了图区域中不同节点（如道路段）在功能类别、结构和交通行为上的动态差异，因此需要一种节点级别的专家专业化方法。

Method: 提出GC-MoE框架，基于图拓扑和近期交通输入窗口为每个节点分配个性化的冻结预训练专家组合；包含一个输入感知、空间上下文化的路由器模块，并可选地引入有界图条件输出细化层。

Result: 在PEMS04、PEMS07、METR-LA和PEMS-BAY四个基准上，GC-MoE相比零参数集成基线提升了MAE，在RMSE和MAPE上也具有竞争力，仅训练约17K参数（冻结专家权重1.5M）。

Conclusion: 基于图条件的混合专家方法能够以极少的可训练参数实现高效的节点级专业化，有效提升时空预测性能。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting on sensor graphs is commonly tackled with a single backbone architecture applied uniformly across all nodes, although graph regions can exhibit different dynamics. Road segments differ in functional class, structure, and traffic behavior, suggesting that node-wise expert specialization can be useful. We propose GC-MoE, a graph-conditioned mixture of experts framework that assigns each node a personalized combination of frozen forecasting experts based on graph topology and the recent traffic input window. GC-MoE combines frozen pretrained spatio-temporal GNN experts with an input-aware, spatially contextualized router while training only a lightweight routing module. We also study a bounded graph-conditioned output refinement layer as an optional extension and include node-adaptive ST-LoRA adapters only as an ablation diagnostic. Across four standard benchmarks (PEMS04, PEMS07, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY), GC-MoE improves MAE over a zero-parameter ensemble baseline, with competitive RMSE and MAPE, while training only ~17K parameters on top of 1.5M frozen expert weights. The implementation is available at https://github.com/Ahghaffari/gc_moe.

</details>


### [150] [MAAT: Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30514)
*Suryash Yagnik,Shubham Gaur,Saksham Thakur,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有机器去忘评估中，因果性"Why"类问题占比极低（<0.06%–1.3%），导致方法在因果知识上的失败被掩盖。本文提出5WBENCH平衡基准（5类各1000样本）和MAAT框架，首次实现在Why类因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器去忘基准（如CounterFact、ZSRE、TOFU等）中"Why"类因果/关系型问题占比极低（<0.06%–1.3%），导致评估高度偏差——方法在因果知识上失败仍可获高分且无法被检测。需要平衡评估框架使因果去忘失败可量化。

Method: 1) 构建5WBENCH平衡基准（5类各1000样本）；2) 分析Why类困难源于多跳推理链（44% vs ≤2%）和长答案跨度（40.1 token）导致的梯度稀释；3) 提出MAAT框架：基于LoRA适配器权重的三阶段方案，含梯度投影上升、SVD秩维剪枝、任务向量否定、混合KL-隐状态保留修复。

Result: 现有基线方法无法同时在Why类上实现高遗忘与高保留。MAAT首次实现该目标，在遗忘-保留Pareto前沿上达到新操作点。

Conclusion: 机器去忘评估必须平衡各问题类型，尤其关注因果知识的评估。MAAT为处理因果知识去忘提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Machine unlearning evaluation is structurally skewed: Why-type questions, which probe causal and relational knowledge, comprise less than 0.06% of CounterFact, 0.6% of ZSRE, and less than 1.3% of TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP-Cyber.
