*Yuwei Zhang,Chengyu Dong,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Hejie Cui,Hongye Jin,Xinyang Zhang,Hamed Bonab,Colin Lockard,Jianshu Chen,Zhenyu Shi,Jingbo Shang,Xian Li,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMem提出了一种将记忆管理与主智能体工作流解耦的框架，通过k步偏移异步流水线实现记忆总结与智能体推理的并行执行，并采用奖励驱动的训练策略确保异步设置的鲁棒性，在SWE-Bench-Verified上实现了1.4倍延迟提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上下文管理通过迭代总结历史交互来解决长周期任务，但额外总结token的解码开销严重影响了端到端响应延迟，现有耦合架构在效率与效果之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出CoMem框架，将记忆管理与智能体工作流解耦；设计k步偏移异步流水线，使记忆模型的总结与智能体推理重叠执行；引入奖励驱动的训练策略，对齐记忆模型以捕获对智能体决策充分的统计信息。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified上，CoMem相比普通长上下文方案实现了1.4倍延迟提升，同时保持了大部分性能；延迟优势随系统吞吐量增加而扩展。

Conclusion: CoMem提供了一种更优的效率-效果权衡，为智能体推理与记忆压缩的独立优化开辟了模块化路径。

Abstract: Context management enables agentic models to solve long-horizon tasks through iterative summarization of previous interaction histories. However, this process typically incurs substantial decoding overhead for the extra summarization tokens, which significantly affect the end-to-end response latency at deployment. In this paper, we introduce CoMem, a novel framework that decouples memory management from the primary agent workflow, enabling these processes to execute in parallel. We propose a $k$-step-off asynchronous pipeline that overlaps the memory model's summarization with the agent's inference, effectively masking the latency of context processing. To ensure robustness under this asynchronous setting, we introduce a reward-driven training strategy that aligns the memory model to capture sufficient statistics for the agent's decision-making. Theoretical analysis confirms that CoMem offers a superior efficiency-effectiveness trade-off compared to coupled architectures. Our extensive experimental results on SWE-Bench-Verified show that CoMem provides 1.4x latency improvements upon vanilla long-context solutions while preserving most of the performance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these latency gains scale favorably with increased system throughput, offering a modular path forward for the independent optimization of agent reasoning and memory compression.

</details>


### [190] [A Lecture Note on Offline RL and IRL, Part II: Foundations of Inverse Reinforcement Learning and Dynamic Discrete Choice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30843)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了逆向强化学习（IRL）与结构计量经济学中动态离散选择（DDC）模型在概率框架上的等价性，系统梳理了从经典DDC方法到现代ML/IRL方法的发展脉络，并指出了各方法的局限性及识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决逆向强化学习问题：给定专家演示数据，恢复其背后的奖励函数。作者发现IRL和DDC两个领域实际在研究同一概率模型，但彼此鲜有交流，因此希望通过建立等价性来促进跨领域方法论融合。

Method: 首先从数学上证明DDC与熵正则化IRL的等价性；然后回顾经典DDC计算范式（嵌套不动点、条件选择概率、时间差分方法），并指出各方法的局限（维度灾难、转移核估计、致命三元组、投影不动点偏差）；接着梳理现代ML/IRL方法（对抗IRL、占用匹配、IQ-Learn、离线ML-IRL），明确各方法的目标函数及识别能力；最后介绍Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架。

Result: 建立了DDC与IRL两个领域之间严格的模型等价性；系统揭示了从Rust嵌套不动点算法到现代对抗IRL等方法各自的优缺点和识别限制；给出了一个统一视角下的梯度估计器用于离线IRL/DDC问题。

Conclusion: 逆向强化学习与动态离散选择模型本质上是同一问题的不同表述，通过跨领域的方法论梳理，为后续研究提供了清晰的理论基础和方法选择指南，特别是经验风险最小化框架为大规模离线IRL提供了可计算的方案。

Abstract: In the forward reinforcement-learning problem, the reward is fixed and known; the learner is asked to find a good policy or value function. Here we turn the question around. Given offline data generated by an expert, can we recover the reward the expert was optimizing? This is the inverse reinforcement learning problem, and remarkably, two communities, structural econometricians studying dynamic discrete choice (DDC) and machine learners studying entropy-regularized IRL, have been working on exactly the same probabilistic model under different names. We begin by proving their equivalence. We then develop the classical identification result of Magnac and Thesmar and the classical computational paradigms that grew out of it: Rust's nested fixed-point algorithm, the conditional-choice-probability approach of Hotz and Miller, and the two temporal-difference approaches of Adusumilli and Eckardt: linear semi-gradient TD and approximate value iteration. Each route has its limits: dimensionality, transition-kernel estimation, the deadly triad, or projected fixed-point bias. We then walk through the modern ML/IRL strand: adversarial IRL, occupancy matching, IQ-Learn, and offline ML-IRL, deriving each method's actual objective and stating precisely what it does and does not identify. We close with the empirical-risk-minimization framework of Kang et al., which yields a gradient-based estimator for offline IRL/DDC.

</details>


### [191] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu,Jiabao Pan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,See-Kiong Ng,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于数据归因奖励的LLM遗忘框架DareU，通过强化学习降低生成响应与遗忘数据的归因分数，有效平衡了遗忘质量与模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法依赖优化预测损失（如最大化遗忘集损失），常导致过度遗忘和模型效用下降，因此需要更优的遗忘框架。

Method: 将LLM遗忘的优化目标重新定义为归因归零，提出DareU框架，利用强化学习降低模型生成响应对遗忘数据所有者的归因分数，并使用LLM分类器高效近似归因计算。

Result: 实验结果表明DareU在实现有效遗忘的同时，优于现有基线方法，在遗忘质量与模型效用之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 基于归因奖励的强化学习遗忘范式能有效解决传统方法的过度遗忘问题，为LLM安全数据移除提供了新思路。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [192] [GlucoFM: A Dual-Stream Foundation Model for Continuous Glucose Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30865)
*Zechen Li,Keerthana Natarajan,Weizhi Zhang,Menglian Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Yuwei Zhang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Flora D. Salim,Mark Malhotra,Lindsey Sunden,Shwetak Patel,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM是一个轻量级的CGM基础模型，通过将血糖动态分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬时事件流，在7项临床预测任务中平均PR-AUC提升4.1分，优于现有CGM专用基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列和CGM基础模型通常将血糖轨迹编码为纠缠的单流序列，未能显式建模血糖动态中不同时间尺度的结构特征。

Method: 将不规则CGM记录对齐到24小时网格并保留观测掩码，将血糖动态分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬时事件流，采用掩码上下文潜在预测和时序动态预测两个互补目标进行预训练。

Result: 在4个队列7项任务中线性探测性能最优，平均PR-AUC提升4.1分；在糖尿病风险和β细胞功能障碍任务上全部最优，在4项胰岛素抵抗任务中3项最优；跨数据集迁移和小样本适应性能最佳。

Conclusion: 生理感知分解作为可迁移CGM表示学习的有效归纳偏置，为血糖监测基础模型设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides a dense view of daily metabolic physiology, yet existing generic time-series and CGM-specific foundation models often encode glucose traces as entangled single-stream sequences, leaving the distinct temporal structure of glycemic dynamics only implicitly modeled. We present GlucoFM, a lightweight CGM foundation model that aligns irregular recordings to a 24-hour chronological grid, preserves observation masks, and decomposes glucose dynamics into slow physiological state and transient event streams, capturing low-frequency glycemic baselines and short-term deviations that may reflect acute physiological responses or sensor artifacts. GlucoFM is pretrained on 109,066 hours of unlabeled CGM recordings from 477 subjects with two complementary objectives: masked contextual latent prediction over fused daily representations and temporal dynamics prediction over state and event streams. Across four diverse cohorts and seven clinical prediction tasks, GlucoFM achieves the strongest subject-disjoint linear-probing performance among evaluated baselines, improving average PR-AUC by 4.1 points over the best CGM-specific foundation model. Its gains are most pronounced on core metabolic outcomes, leading PR-AUC on all diabetes-risk and $β$-cell dysfunction tasks and on 3 of 4 insulin-resistance tasks. GlucoFM also achieves the best overall cross-dataset transfer performance and strong few-shot adaptation among evaluated methods, and consistent gains when aggregating multiple days for subject-level prediction, highlighting physiology-aware decomposition as an effective inductive bias for transferable CGM representation learning.

</details>


### [193] [DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007)
*Jyotirmoy Singh,Anushka Roy,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出蒸馏解释模型（DEM），通过三步玻璃箱框架将梯度提升专家模型的知识蒸馏到可解释决策树中，在生理传感器异常检测任务上达到高精度（AUC最高0.9964）且提供人类可读的if-then规则，推理速度比SHAP快1235倍，并首次提出蒸馏保真度指标量化解释可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理传感器异常检测方法要么是黑盒模型（高精度但不可解释），要么依赖SHAP/LIME等事后解释方法，缺乏既保持高预测性能又提供内在可解释性的方案。

Method: 提出三阶段玻璃箱框架：1）线性基线模型拟合，2）XGBoost专家模型学习残差中的非线性知识，3）将XGBoost蒸馏到可控制深度的可解释决策树中；同时提出蒸馏保真度指标衡量解释树对专家模型非线性贡献的忠实程度。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、WESAD、eICU和SmartNet四个数据集上，临床异常检测AUC达0.9964，可穿戴压力检测AUC达0.9047；推理速度0.17ms/千样本，比SHAP快1235倍；消融实验证实XGBoost蒸馏步骤优于朴素残差拟合。

Conclusion: DEM首次实现了高精度生理异常检测与内在可解释性的统一，通过蒸馏保真度指标提供了解释可信度的量化度量，并支持用户可控的准确率-可解释性权衡，适用于实时临床监测场景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in physiological sensor data from Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) can be caused by sensor faults, network disruptions, or missing data, leading to false alarms. Hence, it demands both high predictive accuracy and clinically interpretable explanations. Existing approaches rely either on black-box models that achieve strong performance but offer no transparency, or on post-prediction explanation methods such as SHAP and LIME. In this paper, we propose the Distilled Explanation Model (DEM), a three-stage glass-box framework that distills the non-linear knowledge of a gradient boosting expert into an interpretable decision tree operating on residuals relative to a linear baseline, so that the explanation is not an approximation but the prediction itself. DEM introduces a novel distillation fidelity metric that quantifies how faithfully the explanation tree captures the expert model's non-linear contribution, providing a principled measure of explanation trustworthiness absent from prior interpretable models. Evaluated across four physiological datasets, including MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, and an in-house SmartNet WBAN corpus, DEM achieves an AUC of 0.9964 on clinical contextual anomaly detection and 0.9047 on wearable stress detection while producing human-readable if-then rules at a controllable depth. Inference requires 0.17ms per 1000 samples, rendering DEM 1235x faster than SHAP-based post-hoc explanation and suitable for real-time physiological monitoring. Ablation studies confirm that the XGBoost distillation step provides measurable gains over naive residual fitting, and depth-sensitivity analysis demonstrates an explicit, user-controlled accuracy-interpretability trade-off unique to DEM among existing intrinsically interpretable models.

</details>


### [194] [Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在满足β-正则性（即存在大量近最优臂）的条件下，退火softmax贪心策略（一种不显式建模不确定性的更新规则）在贝叶斯多臂老虎机中可以实现接近最优的$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾界。这为RLVR/GRPO等基于经验奖励的强化学习方法为何有效提供了解释：当基策略有较高概率生成近最优动作时，即使缺乏不确定性估计，也能避免次优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR和GRPO等策略优化方法在更新时不显式追踪认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty），但实践中却表现良好。论文试图从理论上解释这种"不确定性无关更新"为何仍然有效。

Method: 使用贝叶斯伯努利多臂老虎机模型，分析退火softmax（Boltzmann）贪心策略的贝叶斯遗憾界。核心条件是先验分布满足β-正则性（β=1的线性上尾条件），即存在大量近最优臂。通过推导遗憾上界$\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$，得到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$的最优率。

Result: 1) 在β-正则性下，退火softmax贪心达到$\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$遗憾，当臂数$m=\Theta(\sqrt{T})$时达到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$的近最优率。2) 当臂数较少时，同一策略可能遭受线性遗憾。3) 该结果与RLVR形成结构类比：基策略产生正确完成的非零概率对应β-正则性。

Conclusion: 退火softmax贪心策略的有效性依赖于"大量近最优臂"的环境结构（β-正则性），这解释了RLVR/GRPO等忽略不确定性的方法在基策略足够好时能有效工作的原因。但不适用于臂数少的环境。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and group-based policy optimization methods such as GRPO update a stochastic policy by sampling multiple completions per prompt and increasing the policy's probability on those with higher reward, regularized by a KL penalty toward a reference policy. These updates do not include explicit mechanisms that track epistemic uncertainty. This paper studies a stylized explanation for why such uncertainty-agnostic updates can nevertheless be effective. We analyze an annealed softmax (Boltzmann) policy that selects actions according to a softmax of empirical mean rewards in a many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit. Under a linear upper-tail condition on the prior (the $β=1$ case of $β$-regularity), which implies an abundance of near-optimal arms, we prove that annealed softmax greedy achieves Bayes regret $\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$, and in particular $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ when the number of arms scales as $m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$. This is the near-optimal Bayes regret rate in this regime, attained also by empirical-mean greedy. Under $β$-regularity, many arms maintain empirical means close to the optimum throughout learning, so when softmax samples an arm other than the empirically best, that arm tends to be another near-optimal one rather than a clearly inferior one. By contrast, with a small number of arms, the same kind of softmax policy can suffer linear regret. The result also provides a structural analogy to RLVR, where a base policy with a non-negligible probability of producing a correct completion plays the role of $β$-regularity.

</details>


### [195] [Zero Collapse: A Failure Mode of Policy Gradient Methods in Discontinuous Reward Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30896)
*Nishant Kumar,Enrique Areyan Viqueira,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复拍卖竞价中强化学习的“零坍塌”失败模式，指出由于拍卖奖励景观的间断性和悬崖特性，策略梯度与actor-critic方法容易陷入零奖励区域难以恢复，并提出了针对性的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法（策略梯度、值函数方法）在处理拍卖环境时，因奖励函数具有间断、悬崖状特性（如先拍中出价低于阈值奖励为零，超过后奖励随出价递减），导致学习效果不佳，作者希望揭示其根本失败机制并提出解决方案。

Method: (1) 从机制上解释间断奖励如何导致信号消失和零坍塌；(2) 分析策略随机性与步长之间的交互作用；(3) 在REINFORCE和actor-critic变体上进行实证验证；(4) 提出初始化与架构选择的缓解策略；(5) 引入针对拍卖环境的正式RL框架。

Result: 发现actor-critic方法尤其脆弱，有偏的值估计会加速向不稳定区域移动；揭示了零坍塌现象的存在，即在平坦零奖励区域缺乏梯度信号导致恢复极其样本低效。

Conclusion: 拍卖环境的间断奖励结构会导致RL方法出现零坍塌，通过合理的初始化和架构设计可以提升稳定性，为拍卖场景下的RL应用提供了理论分析与实践指导。

Abstract: Bidding in repeated auctions is a central challenge for reinforcement learning (RL), combining continuous control with the strategic complexities of digital advertising. While policy gradient and value-based methods seem well-suited for these settings, they often struggle with the discontinuous, "cliff-like" nature of auction reward landscapes. In a first-price auction, for example, a bidder receives zero reward until they cross a specific threshold, after which the reward decreases as the bid increases. This creates a landscape of flat, zero-reward regions separated by sharp boundaries.
  We identify a fundamental failure mode in this setting termed "zero collapse." We show that stochastic exploration and gradient-based updates can cause policies to overshoot optimal high-reward regions and enter flat, zero-reward regimes. Once there, the lack of an informative gradient signal makes recovery extremely sample-inefficient, effectively trapping the agent. We find that actor-critic methods are particularly susceptible, as biased value estimates can accelerate this movement toward unstable regions.
  Our contributions include: (1) a mechanistic explanation of how discontinuous rewards lead to vanishing signals and zero collapse; (2) an analysis of the interaction between policy stochasticity and step size; and (3) an empirical demonstration of this phenomenon across REINFORCE and actor-critic variants. We propose practical mitigation strategies involving initialization and architectural choices to improve stability. Finally, we introduce a formal RL framework for auction environments highlighting their unique structural properties.

</details>


### [196] [Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049)
*Veronika Semmelrock,Benedetta Strizzolo,Francesco Zuccato,Gerhard Friedrich,Patrick Rodler,Konstantin Schekotihin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHECKMATE工具，通过代码进化自动生成算法，仅需形式化规范（what）而无需人工设计求解过程（how），在配置与调度问题上超越现有最优求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解决大规模组合优化问题需要专家精心设计形式化、求解器和启发式策略，即既要指定"解决方案是什么"，还要规定"如何推导"。这过程耗时且依赖专家经验，亟需一种自动化方法消除"如何推导"的负担。

Method: 提出CHECKMATE工具，利用代码进化（code evolution）实现算法自动生成。核心依赖形式化规范（formal specification）确保解的正确性并支持系统性能评估，同时结合自然语言描述引导进化过程。

Result: 在工业配置与调度领域的多个问题中，CHECKMATE自动进化出的算法在所有测试案例中均一致优于现有最优求解器（state-of-the-art solvers）。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了形式化方法在引导代码进化以自动解决复杂现实问题方面的巨大潜力，标志着算法生成范式的转变——从需要指定"如何做"到仅需指定"要什么"。

Abstract: Combinatorial and optimization problems are fundamental to many industrial AI applications. Solving large-scale real-world instances of such problems typically requires careful problem formalization, specialized solvers, and expert-designed heuristics. Thus, experts need to specify not only what solutions are, but also how they are derived. By introducing the tool CHECKMATE, we show that algorithm generation via code evolution represents a paradigm shift by eliminating the need to formulate the how. CHECKMATE solely relies on the what. Specifically, a formal specification ensures solutions' correctness and enables systematic performance evaluation of the generated programs, while a natural language description guides the evolutionary process. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on selected problems from two industrial domains: configuration and scheduling. In all cases, the evolved algorithms consistently outperform state-of-the-art solvers. This underscores the potential of formal methods in guiding code evolution for automatically solving complex real-world problems.

</details>


### [197] [PINNs Failure Modes are Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30910)
*Nigel T. Andersen,Takashi Matsubara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了PINNs的失效模式源于过拟合，并提出了基于正则化和扩展双反向传播的方法，在更少采样点下实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在简单方程上会出现失效模式（低损失但解不正确），此前虽有大量研究提出架构和优化方案，但根本原因尚不明确，需要深入理解失效机制并找到更简洁有效的解决方案。

Method: 1) 直接可视化残差，发现失效模式是过拟合导致（损失在配点上被优化但别处未优化）；2) 应用正则化消除失效模式；3) 将双反向传播扩展至全部残差集合。

Result: 在四个标准失效模式方程上，使用普通架构和最多23倍更少的配点，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: PINNs失效的根本原因是过拟合而非之前认为的复杂原因，正则化和扩展的双反向传播是有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a common class of machine learning-based partial differential equation (PDE) solvers which train a network to represent a solution by minimizing a residual loss that encodes the PDE. Despite their successes, they are known to fail on certain simple equations, converging to an incorrect solution despite low loss. These failure modes have garnered significant attention in the literature over the past several years, motivating both architectural and optimization based solutions. By directly visualizing the residual, we show that failure modes are the result of overfitting: the loss is minimized on the collocation points, but not elsewhere. Applying regularization causes the failure modes to vanish. Finally, we extend double backpropagation over the full set of residuals, and use it to achieve state-of-the-art performance on four standard failure mode equations with up to $23\times$ fewer collocation points and a vanilla architecture.

</details>


### [198] [Automating Formal Verification with Reinforcement Learning and Recursive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30914)
*Max Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用验证器环境增强大型语言模型生成经过验证的程序和证明的能力，主要通过从可验证奖励中强化学习（RLVR）和验证器引导的推理时搜索。在Dafny中训练的开源模型将验证通过率从2.2%提升至58.1%（后经过滤精炼至31.1%），在Lean中开发的验证器引导框架将修复通过率从46.2%提升至69.2%，并解决了42个先前未解决任务中的7个。同时引入了Dalek-Bench基准测试，但结果仍较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动化形式验证方面面临数据稀缺和精确性要求高的挑战，现有方法生成的可信代码和证明不足。

Method: 使用RLVR（基于GRPO等变体）在Dafny中训练模型，结合编译器和验证器结果评分；在Lean中开发验证器引导的推理框架，支持子目标分解、验证反馈和修复。

Result: Dafny实验验证通过率从2.2%提升至58.1%（过滤后从9.7%提升至31.1%）；Lean框架验证通过率从46.2%提升至69.2%，解决7个先前未解决任务；Dalek-Bench基准测试结果较弱。

Conclusion: 验证器环境能够显著提升LLM生成验证代码和证明的能力，但仍需进一步改进规范质量、任务分解策略和工具使用策略。

Abstract: Automated formal verification remains challenging for large language models because data for proof assistants and verification-aware languages is scarce, and correctness depends on satisfying precise machine-checkable specifications rather than producing plausible code. This thesis studies how verifier environments can improve LLM generation of verified programs and proofs through reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) and verifier-guided inference-time search. First, we train open-source models in Dafny with RLVR using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related variants, assembling generated candidates into complete programs and scoring them with compiler and verifier outcomes. Initial experiments on an APPS-derived Dafny dataset increased verified reward from 2.2% to 58.1%, but revealed specification hacking, where models exploit weak formal specifications instead of implementing the intended solutions. After filtering underspecified and vulnerable tasks, multi-turn RLVR on the refined benchmark improves the verified pass rate from 9.7% to 31.1%. Second, we develop a verifier-guided inference scaffold in Lean that treats proof generation as structured search over decomposed subgoals, verifier feedback, diagnostics, and repair. With a fixed base model, the full scaffold with proof reviser improves pass rate on an initial VeriCoding pilot set from 46.2% under direct repair to 69.2%. On the larger VERINA dataset, whole-task decomposition plus proof reviser solves 7 of 42 previously unsolved tasks. We also introduce Dalek-Bench, a repository-scale Lean benchmark derived from the Rust $\texttt{curve25519-dalek}$ verification project; preliminary results remain weak, indicating that stronger progress evaluation and task-specific tool-use policies are still needed.

</details>


### [199] [Unsupervised Diffusion Solver for Combinatorial Optimization via Combinatorial Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30920)
*Shengyu Feng,Tarun Suresh,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CAM的无监督训练框架，通过将扩散组合优化建模为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，并引入离散伴随动力学来传播优化信号，从而在不依赖最优解标注的情况下，取得了与有监督方法及传统求解器相竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散神经求解器在组合优化中表现出色，但它们通常需要大量接近最优解的标注数据进行有监督训练，这在实际应用中难以获得。本文旨在消除对有监督数据的依赖，实现无监督的扩散组合优化训练。

Method: 将扩散组合优化形式化为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，引入离散伴随动力学来传播优化信号，并提出组合伴随匹配（CAM）无监督训练框架，利用结构化的低方差轨迹级优化信号进行训练。

Result: CAM在多种组合优化问题上一致优于现有的无监督扩散基线方法，性能与强有监督扩散求解器乃至传统求解器相当。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CAM框架成功将伴随轨迹优化方法扩展到离散组合域，为无监督训练扩散组合优化求解器提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers have shown strong promise for combinatorial optimization (CO), but existing methods typically rely on supervised training with large collections of near-optimal solutions. In this work, we extend adjoint-based trajectory optimization methods to discrete combinatorial domains. We formulate diffusion-based CO as a stochastic control problem over Continuous-Time Markov Chains and introduce discrete adjoint dynamics for propagating optimization signals through discrete generative trajectories. Building on this formulation, we propose Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM), an unsupervised training framework for discrete diffusion solvers with structured and low-variance trajectory-level optimization signals. Empirically, CAM consistently outperforms existing unsupervised diffusion baselines and achieves performance competitive with strong supervised diffusion solvers and even traditional solvers across diverse combinatorial optimization problems. Our code is available at https://github.com/Shengyu-Feng/CAM.

</details>


### [200] [Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159)
*Daniil Plyusov,Alexey Gorbatovski,Alexey Malakhov,Nikita Balagansky,Boris Shaposhnikov,Daria Korotyshova,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB)预热方法，通过KL信任域将早期 rollout 策略替换为最接近教师的行为策略，并逐步退火至纯学生 rollout，解决策略蒸馏中前缀不匹配和早期 rollout 质量差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，早期学生 rollout 质量差，导致教师监督作用在弱或低质量前缀上，影响训练效果。

Method: 提出TRB预热方法：在学生中心的KL信任域内，将早期rollout策略替换为最接近教师的行为策略，保持per-prefix反向KL蒸馏损失不变，并将KL预算逐步退火至零，使训练在预热后回归纯学生rollout。

Result: 在两个数学推理蒸馏任务中，TRB方法取得了最强的平均性能。

Conclusion: TRB作为预热方法，能有效改善在线策略蒸馏中早期rollout质量差的问题，提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>


### [201] [Local linear convergence of gradient methods for overparameterized Gaussian mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30936)
*Jingxing Wang,Vasileios Charisopoulos,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化高斯混合模型的学习问题，提出了一种结合短梯度下降步和长Polyak步的交替方法，能够在线性速率下收敛到最优解，表明过参数化导致的慢收敛并非固有挑战，而是可以通过利用损失景观的有利结构来克服。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化在避免虚假局部最优和实现全局恢复方面是必要的，但它会显著拖慢局部收敛速度。本文旨在克服这一慢收敛问题。

Method: 提出一种交替方法：交替执行若干"短"梯度下降步（接近流形）和"长"Polyak步（缩小到最小化器的距离）。利用损失景观上存在一个缓慢增长的流形，在该流形上Polyak步长可几何级地降低损失。

Result: 在特定混合权重假设下，所提方法以局部线性速率收敛到最小化器；对于任意权重，方法收敛到接近最优的解（达到自然欠指定阈值）。

Conclusion: 过参数化导致的慢收敛并非固有挑战，可以通过利用损失景观的有利结构来克服。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning Gaussian mixture models under overparameterization. Prior work has shown that while overparameterization is essential for avoiding spurious local optima and enables global recovery of the ground-truth model using the gradient-EM (expectation-maximization) algorithm, it can dramatically slow down the local rate of convergence. Under certain assumptions on the mixture weights, we show that a standard divergence measure minimized by statistical learning procedures possesses a manifold of slow growth on which the well-known Polyak stepsize reduces the loss geometrically, and design a gradient-based method that converges to minimizers at a locally linear rate. Additionally, we show that our method converges to nearly optimal solutions -- up to a natural misspecification threshold -- for mixtures with arbitrary weights. At a high level, the method alternates between several "short" gradient descent steps that approach the manifold and "long" Polyak steps that contract the distance to minimizers. Our results suggest that slow convergence is not an intrinsic challenge of overparameterization, but can be overcome by exploiting the favorable structure of the loss landscape.

</details>


### [202] [Spectral Anatomy of Quantum Gaussian Process Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30952)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Guang Lin,Yuning Qiu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现量子高斯过程（QGP）中看似无关的“去量子化无指数加速”和“量子核后验病态”两个现象，均由同一诊断量——核格拉姆矩阵的归一化谱熵 $S(K)/\log n$ 控制。该诊断对多种量子与经典核族普遍适用，并在IBM Heron硬件上实现了高精度迁移（中位绝对误差3.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的两项独立研究分别指出：基于HHL的QGP回归在良态条件下不存在指数级加速，以及高表达力量子核会在贝叶斯优化中产生后验病态。本文试图揭示这两个看似无关的结果之间的内在联系。

Method: 推导了Nyström近似的Cauchy–Schwarz尾部界、基于Bach自由度的有限样本方差收缩恒等式，以及目标在核本征基下的本征维度表征的最优熵；通过大量实验验证诊断量在去量子化、ECE和方差收缩面板上的普适性，并在IBM Heron硬件上进行跨后端迁移实验。

Result: 在所有测试的核族（硬件高效、matchgate、IQP、RBF/Matérn/RFF/深度核）上，归一化谱熵曲线高度一致；NLL最优位于高熵（平滑目标）或低熵（带限量子数据目标）；从模拟器到Heron硬件的迁移中位绝对误差3.2%，均值5.2%，第二个后端均值误差2.7%，6量子比特扩展均值误差1.7%。

Conclusion: 归一化谱熵是统一理解QGP中加速潜力与后验行为的关键诊断量，且无需误差缓解即可在高噪声硬件上可靠估计。

Abstract: Two recent results have reshaped quantum Gaussian processes (QGPs). On the one hand, \citet{lowe2025assessing} rule out the exponential speedups claimed by HHL-based QGP regression in the typical, well-conditioned regime; on the other, an independent line of work shows that highly expressive quantum kernels suffer posterior pathologies that break Bayesian optimization. We show that these seemingly unrelated phenomena are governed by the same quantity: the normalized spectral entropy $S(K)/\log n$ of the kernel Gram matrix. We prove a Cauchy--Schwarz tail bound on Nyström approximation error, a finite-sample variance-contraction identity in terms of Bach's degrees of freedom $d_σ(K)$, and a characterization of the \emph{target-dependent} optimal entropy via the intrinsic dimension of the target in the kernel eigenbasis. Empirically, the diagnostic is kernel-agnostic: hardware-efficient, matchgate, IQP \emph{and} RBF/Matérn/RFF/deep-kernel families all collapse onto identical $S/\log n$ curves on dequantization, ECE, and variance-contraction panels. The NLL sweet spot lives at high entropy for smooth targets and at low entropy for band-limited quantum-data targets. The diagnostic transfers from simulator to IBM Heron hardware with median absolute error $3.2\%$ and mean $5.2\%$ in $S/\log n$ across $24$ configurations at $n_q = 4$, with matchgate and IQP within $5\%$ mean and a single HE configuration returning a $30\%$ outlier that drops to $0.5\%$ on rerun (attributed to calibration drift); the same diagnostic transfers to a second Heron backend (mean error $2.7\%$) and to a $n_q = 6$ scale-up on the original backend (mean error $1.7\%$). No error mitigation is applied throughout.

</details>


### [203] [Revisiting Zeroth-Order Hessian Approximation: A Single-Step Policy Optimization Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30960)
*Junbin Qiu,Zhaowei Hong,Renzhe Xu,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZoVH 框架，通过单步策略优化视角统一零阶 Hessian 估计，并设计了一套方差缩减的 Hessian、正则化逆及逆 Hessian-梯度积的估计器，理论证明无偏性与方差最优性，实验验证了其优越的估计精度与收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维场景下，零阶 Hessian 及其逆的估计存在方差大、样本效率低的问题，缺乏统一且低方差的估计器套件。

Method: 将零阶 Hessian 估计重新解释为单步策略优化的 Hessian，建立理论等价性；提出 ZoVH 套件，包括：1）理论最优基线最小化方差；2）重用历史查询的样本高效策略。

Result: 理论证明了 Hessian 估计器的无偏性、基线的方差最优性、ZoVH 套件的误差界以及曲率感知零阶算法的收敛保证；实验在多个实际任务中验证了优越性。

Conclusion: ZoVH 为零阶 Hessian 估计提供了统一的理论框架和实用的方差缩减方案，在无梯度优化的各类任务中具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate Zeroth-Order (ZO) Hessian estimation is a cornerstone of derivative-free methods, essential for tasks such as bilevel optimization, Bayesian inference, and uncertainty quantification. However, obtaining a complete suite of low-variance estimators for the Hessian and its inverse in high-dimensional settings remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose a unified framework that reinterprets ZO Hessian approximation through the lens of single-step Policy Optimization (PO). This perspective establishes a theoretical equivalence between general ZO Hessian estimators and the Hessian of a smoothed PO objective, unifying distinct classical randomized estimators as specific instances of baseline selection. Building on this foundation, we introduce ZoVH, a comprehensive suite of variance-reduced estimators for the full Hessian matrix, its regularized inverse, and the bias-corrected inverse Hessian-gradient product. ZoVH leverages two key techniques: (1) a unique optimal baseline derived to provably minimize variance, and (2) a query reuse strategy that incorporates historical function queries to enhance sample efficiency without inflating costs. Our rigorous theoretical analysis confirms the unbiasedness of the Hessian estimator, validates the variance optimality of our baseline, provides error bounds for the entire ZoVH suite, and establishes convergence guarantees for the resulting curvature-aware ZO algorithm. Extensive empirical results validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that ZoVH achieves superior estimation accuracy and convergence performance in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/Qjbtiger/ZoVH

</details>


### [204] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了约70篇TinyML设备上的在线学习（ODL）研究工作，从分布变化类型这一核心原则出发，分析了不同分布变化如何影响应用、硬件和解决方案结构，并指出了方法论基准与现实部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型部署到微控制器级设备（TinyML）后，面临部署后分布变化导致静态模型性能下降的根本挑战，而现有文献缺乏对分布变化类型及其差异化解决方案的系统性刻画。

Method: 对约70篇关于设备上学习（ODL）的研究工作进行综述，以分布变化类型为核心分析原则，考察不同分布变化对可寻址应用、硬件和解决方案结构的影响。

Result: 识别出方法论基准与现实世界部署场景之间存在持续性的差距。

Conclusion: 分布变化类型是影响TinyML设备上学习解决方案设计的关键因素，当前领域需要弥合基准测试与真实部署之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [205] [EchoRL: Reinforcement Learning via Rollout Echoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31228)
*Jinhe Bi,Aniri,Minglai Yang,Xingcheng Zhou,Wenke Huang,Sikuan Yan,Yujun Wang,Zixuan Cao,Michael Färber,Xun Xiao,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR训练中优势退化问题（所有自生成rollout均验证成功导致梯度消失），提出EchoRL方法，通过基于熵值的EchoClip提取辅助监督信号，持续提升模型训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练中随着进程推进，大量prompt的rollout出现优势退化现象：所有自生成rollout均验证成功，导致奖励标准差为零、优势为零、策略梯度消失，训练收益边际化且无效。

Method: 分析外部专家模型生成的金色轨迹的熵模式，提出EchoRL轻量模块：从验证成功的rollout中基于步骤级熵值识别EchoClip，将其作为辅助监督信号注入RL目标函数。

Result: 在10个基准、5个LLM骨干、4种流行RLVR后训练方法上，EchoRL以最小开销持续提升RLVR后训练性能。

Conclusion: EchoRL有效解决了优势退化导致的学习信号崩溃问题，通过挖掘被忽视的rollout中的有价值信号，进一步提升了RLVR训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards is an effective route for post-training to strengthen the reasoning capability of large language models. However, as training proceeds, the learning signal can collapse thus makes the training gain become marginal and ineffective. Specifically, a growing fraction of prompts' rollouts become advantage-degenerated: all the self-generated rollouts show verified-success, making the standard deviation over their rewards be zero; accordingly each rollout's advantage becomes degenerated (zero) as well. Given such rollouts' advantages, the policy-gradient for model optimization eventually vanishes, capping the training performance. We argue that some of these rollouts still contain valuable learning signals but unfortunately omitted with the existing RLVR methods. In this paper, inspired through analyzing the entropy pattern behind golden trajectories produced by external expert models, we propose EchoRL for better exploiting the advantage-degenerated rollouts to further improve the training performance. EchoRL is a lightweight module that first identifies an EchoClip from verified-success rollouts based on their step-level entropy values, and then feeds this clip back as an auxiliary supervision signal in the RL objective. Extensive experiments across 10 benchmarks, 5 LLM backbones, and 4 popular RLVR post-training methods demonstrate that EchoRL consistently improves RLVR post-training with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [206] [Learning Multi-Agent Coordination via Sheaf-ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31005)
*Jeffrey Seely,Bartłomiej Cupiał,Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微分的多智能体协调优化框架，通过细胞层（cellular sheaf）定义智能体间的共识约束，利用ADMM算法进行协调，并端到端训练整个系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法：局部视角无法统一全局共识。需要一种可微分的多智能体协调框架，既能处理异构共识，又能保持端到端可训练性。

Method: 1）输入分解为重叠局部视图，每个智能体处理由神经编码器参数化的凸子问题；2）通过ADMM协调智能体，约束由细胞层定义；3）通过展开优化进行反向传播联合训练。

Result: 在迷宫寻路、图像分类（MNIST）和数独任务上验证。MNIST上对分布偏移更鲁棒；数独上求解率显著高于参数匹配的MPNN基线。

Conclusion: ADMM结构暴露了原始、共识和对偶状态变量，使得协调动态可分析和干预，这是标准消息传递架构不具备的特性。

Abstract: We present a differentiable optimization framework for multi-agent coordination. An input is decomposed into overlapping local views, each processed by an agent that solves a convex subproblem parameterized by a neural encoder. Agents coordinate through the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) with inter-agent constraints specified by a cellular sheaf. The sheaf specifies which aspects of neighboring solutions must agree, allowing for heterogeneous notions of global consensus. Backpropagating through the unrolled optimization jointly trains all components of the multi-agent system. We evaluate on maze pathfinding, image classification, and Sudoku, where agents with individually insufficient local views learn to coordinate to produce correct global outputs. On MNIST, the local-view decomposition yields improved robustness to distribution shifts relative to a standard CNN. On Sudoku, the optimization-derived structure yields markedly higher solve rates than parameter-matched MPNN baselines. Finally, the ADMM structure exposes distinct primal, consensus, and dual state variables, opening the coordination dynamics to direct analysis and intervention -- a property unavailable in standard message-passing architectures.

</details>


### [207] [Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249)
*Bosong Huang,Panzhen Zhao,Zengxiang Li,Patricia Lee,Wei Jin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LVCG，一种在Frank向量心电图（VCG）空间中学习统一心脏电活动潜在表征的自监督框架，相比传统ECG空间方法能减少冗余、提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准十二导联ECG是同一心脏电活动在不同空间方向上的投影，在ECG空间进行表征学习会引入大量冗余，导致伪相关和过拟合风险增加。

Method: 受Frank VCG模型启发，提出LVCG框架，直接在物理上有物理意义的VCG潜在空间中进行自监督表征学习，学习视角不变（view-invariant）的潜在表征。

Result: LVCG在多个任务上全面优于基于ECG空间的基线方法，尤其在领域迁移场景中展现了更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在VCG潜在空间进行表征学习可有效减少冗余、避免伪相关，是比ECG空间更优的学习范式。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a cornerstone of cardiac assessment, making the learning of informative ECG representations fundamental to tasks ranging from disease diagnosis to clinical report generation. However, existing methods operate almost exclusively in the observable ECG signal space. In practice, the standard twelve-lead ECG represents multiple projections of the same underlying cardiac electrical activity from different spatial orientations. Therefore, representation learning in the ECG space inevitably introduces substantial redundancy, which may lead to spurious correlations and increased risk of overfitting. To address this and motivated by the Frank vectorcardiogram (VCG) model, we propose learning a unified latent representation of cardiac electrical activity directly in the VCG space. We introduce LVCG, the first general self-supervised representation learning framework designed to operate in this physically grounded latent space. By learning view-invariant latent VCG representations rather than lead-specific artifacts, VCG minimizes redundancy and improves generalization. LVCG generally outperforms ECG-space baselines across tasks, demonstrating enhanced robustness and generalization, especially in domain shift settings.

</details>


### [208] [An Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31016)
*Yulin Hu,Fuyan Ou,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效可扩展的图压缩方法SP-ESGC，通过解耦设计分离节点压缩和结构生成，实现高计算效率和跨GNN架构的良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法存在耦合优化导致计算效率低下，以及跨GNN架构泛化能力差的问题。

Method: 1) 基于谱图理论的热核特征传播生成节点表示；2) 混合聚类策略提取判别性类内质心；3) 预训练边预测器从原始图推断可迁移的结构模式。

Result: 在真实图数据集上，SP-ESGC实现了精确的图压缩，同时计算效率显著提高，并且在不同GNN架构上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SP-ESGC通过解耦设计有效解决了现有图压缩方法的效率与泛化瓶颈。

Abstract: Graph condensation (GC) is pivotal for enabling Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) deployment in resource-constrained scenarios by compressing large-scale graphs into compact synthetic counterparts. Existing GC methods commonly suffer from computational inefficiency due to coupled optimization as well as encountering poor generalization across GNN architectures. To address these challenges, this study proposes an Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving (SP-ESGC), which possesses a decoupled design that separates node condensation from graph structure generation. Specifically, it first employs heat kernel feature propagation to generate node representation via spectral graph theory-inspired diffusion. Further, a novel hybrid clustering strategy is designed to extracts discriminative intra-class centroids from the node representation. Finally, a pre-trained edge predictor infers transferable structural patterns from the original graph, ensuring accurate synthetic graph generation. Extensive experiments on real-world graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed SP-ESGC implementes a precise GC with significantly high computational efficiency. Moreover, SP-ESGC also generalizes well across diverse GNN architectures.

</details>


### [209] [The Terminal Representation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31289)
*Amir Esterhuysen,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的强化学习表示方法——终端表征（TR），它比 successor representation（SR）和 default representation（DR）更高效，无需特征分解即可用于下游任务，且理论完备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SR 和 DR 需要通过特征分解来支持下游任务（如选项发现、奖励塑造等），但特征分解计算开销大且隐含对称转移动力学的假设，限制了可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出终端表征（TR），一种基于奖励加权的轨迹编码方式，维度更低，可直接用于下游任务；给出了其理论推导、两种学习算法的收敛性证明、零样本组合性分析以及与不同奖励公式的等价性。

Result: 理论上证明了 TR 嵌入在 DR 的顶部特征向量中，无需特征分解即可捕获相同知识；实验表明 TR 在计算开销（学习、存储、使用）方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TR 是一种计算高效且理论坚实的替代方案，能够在不依赖特征分解的前提下完成与 DR 类似的下游任务，具有更好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Representation learning is a powerful tool for spatio-temporal abstraction within reinforcement learning (RL). Two well established approaches are through the successor representation (SR) and the default representation (DR). The SR encodes states by the future trajectories they induce, capturing information flow decoupled from reward. The DR builds on this by weighting trajectories with reward, integrating credit-assignment structure into the representation. Eigenvectors of both representations have been used to support a range of downstream tasks -- including option discovery, reward shaping, transfer learning, and exploration. We introduce a structurally distinct formulation: the terminal representation (TR). The TR encodes reward-weighted trajectories similarly to the DR, but can be learned as a lower-dimensionality object, and can be used directly for the mentioned applications without eigenvector computations. Eigendecomposition also imposes the assumption of symmetric transition dynamics, which the TR can bypass. In this work we develop the theoretical foundations of the TR: its derivation, convergence of two learning algorithms, its use for zero-shot compositionality, and equivalences between alternative reward formulations. We further show the TR is embedded in the top DR eigenvector, allowing it to capture the same underlying knowledge without eigendecomposition. Additionally, we provide empirical evidence of the TR as a viable alternative to existing representations in subsidiary applications, while requiring less computational overhead to learn, store, and use.

</details>


### [210] [Inconsistency-Aware Minimization: Improving Generalization with Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31324)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Hyeonseong Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部不一致性（local inconsistency）这一无需标签即可计算的泛化度量，并据此提出不一致感知最小化（IAM）优化方法，在监督、半监督和自监督学习中均能提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计泛化差距并开发改进泛化的优化方法对深度学习至关重要，利用无标签数据来实现这些目标在实际场景中具有显著优势。

Method: 从参数空间的信息几何视角推导出局部不一致性（local inconsistency），将其与Fisher信息矩阵和损失Hessian矩阵建立理论联系；并将其纳入训练目标，提出不一致感知最小化（IAM）。

Result: 局部不一致性与泛化差距相关；IAM在标准监督学习中达到与Sharpness-Aware Minimization等方法相当的性能，在半监督和自监督学习中也表现有效。

Conclusion: 局部不一致性是一种有效的、无需标签的泛化度量，IAM作为一种新的优化方法，能够在多种学习范式下提升模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Estimating the generalization gap and developing optimization methods that improve generalization are crucial for deep learning models, for both theoretical understanding and practical applications. Leveraging unlabeled data for these purposes offers significant advantages in real-world scenarios. This paper introduces a novel generalization measure, local inconsistency, derived from an information-geometric perspective on the parameter space of neural networks. A key feature of local inconsistency is that it can be computed without explicit labels. We establish theoretical underpinnings by connecting local inconsistency to the Fisher information matrix and the loss Hessian. Empirically, we demonstrate that local inconsistency correlates with the generalization gap. Based on these findings, we propose Inconsistency-Aware Minimization (IAM), which incorporates local inconsistency into the training objective. We demonstrate that in standard supervised learning settings, IAM enhances generalization, achieving performance comparable to that of existing methods such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization. Furthermore, IAM exhibits efficacy in semi- and self-supervised learning scenarios, where the local inconsistency is computed from unlabeled data.

</details>


### [211] [dashi: A Python library for Dataset Shift Characterization to Support Trustworthy AI Development and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31360)
*David Fernández-Narro,Pablo Ferri,Ángel Sánchez-García,Juan M. García-Gómez,Carlos Sáez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: dashi是一个开源Python库，用于数据漂移的探索、量化和特征分析，提供无监督和有监督两种方法，支持时间序列和多源数据，已在三个健康AI案例中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康AI中数据漂移会严重影响模型性能和患者安全，而现有缺乏易用且全面的软件工具来进行数据漂移分析。

Method: 提出dashi库，包含无监督方法（基于信息几何和非参数统计流形）和有监督方法（量化模型性能退化），支持用户自定义的时间/域批次。

Result: 在妊娠糖尿病、COVID-19和急救医疗调度三个案例中验证了dashi的有效性。

Conclusion: dashi通过交互式可视化分析和变异指标，支持AI生命周期各阶段的可信度，实现鲁棒安全的机器学习流程。

Abstract: The Artificial Intelligence (AI) life cycle requires a thorough understanding of the underlying data dynamics for robust, safe and cost-effective AI development and use. Dataset shifts are defined as changes between train and test data distributions. Whether occurring over time (temporal) or across different sites (multi-source), they can severely degrade model performance and compromise data quality. This is particularly important in health AI, where the safety and fundamental rights of patients can be severely affected by uncontrolled shifts both at training and operational stages. While the theoretical foundations of covariate, prior, and concept shifts are well established, there is a lack of accessible and comprehensive software tools to perform their analysis. We introduce dashi, an open-source Python library designed for the exploration, quantification, and characterization of dataset shifts. dashi provides a dual approach: an unsupervised approach that leverages information geometry and non-parametric statistical manifolds to data variability characterization and analysis (e.g., Information Geometric Temporal plots and Multi-Source Variability metrics like Global Probabilistic Deviation and Source Probabilistic Outlyingness), and a supervised approach that quantifies and characterizes model performance degradation. Both unsupervised and supervised approaches work across user-defined temporal and domain/source batches. We demonstrate the utility of dashi on three simulated and real-world health AI case studies on gestational diabetes mellitus, COVID-19 and emergency medical dispatch. By providing interactive visual analytics and variability metrics, dashi supports trustworthiness of AI life cycle stages enabling robust and safe machine learning pipelines through the assessment of data coherence and AI performance.

</details>


### [212] [UniRTL: Unifying Code and Graph for Robust RTL Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31040)
*Yi Liu,Hongji Zhang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniRTL是一个多模态预训练框架，通过联合利用RTL代码和CDFG（控制数据流图）学习统一的RTL表示，在性能预测和代码检索任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅依赖单一模态（RTL代码或图结构表示），难以充分表达RTL设计的语义和结构信息。作者认为融合代码和CDFG两种互补模态对全面理解RTL设计至关重要。

Method: 提出UniRTL框架，通过互掩码建模实现代码与图的细粒度对齐；采用分层训练策略，包含预训练的图感知分词器，以及功能摘要文本、代码和图的分阶段对齐。

Result: 在性能预测和代码检索两个下游任务上，UniRTL在不同设置下均一致优于现有方法，表现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniRTL作为一种多模态预训练框架，为硬件设计自动化提供了更强大和稳健的基础表示。

Abstract: Developing effective representations for register transfer level (RTL) designs is crucial for accelerating the hardware design workflow. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on a single data modality, either the RTL code or its associated graph-based representation, limiting the expressiveness and generalization ability of the learned representations. For RTL, the control data flow graph (CDFG) offers a comprehensive structural representation that preserves complete information, while the code modality explicitly encodes semantic and functional information. We argue that integrating these complementary modalities is essential for a thorough understanding of RTL designs. To this end, we propose UniRTL, a multimodal pretraining framework that learns unified RTL representations by jointly leveraging code and CDFG. UniRTL achieves fine-grained alignment between code and graph through mutual masked modeling and employs a hierarchical training strategy that incorporates a pretrained graph-aware tokenizer and staged alignment of text (i.e., functional summary) and code prior to graph integration. We evaluate UniRTL on two downstream tasks, performance prediction and code retrieval, under multiple settings. Experimental results show that UniRTL consistently outperforms prior methods, establishing it as a more robust and powerful foundation for advancing hardware design automation.

</details>


### [213] [Scaling Higher-Order Graph Learning with Maximal Clique Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31373)
*Antoine Vialle,Aref Einizade,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Jhony H. Giraldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出简化与分解的胞腔Weisfeiler-Lehman测试（sCWL和fCWL），在保持高阶表达力的同时提升计算效率；并引入最大团复形和CliqueWalk采样方法，实现可扩展的高阶图表示学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN仅限于建模成对交互，而基于胞腔复形的高阶模型虽然表达力更强，但可扩展性差，计算开销大。

Method: 1) 提出sCWL和fCWL测试，在保留CWL表达力的同时提升效率；2) 引入最大团复形，降低时间和内存复杂度；3) 提出CliqueWalk偏置随机游走，线性采样最大团，避免显式团枚举。

Result: 在保持强实证性能的同时，显著降低了时间与内存复杂度，且算法规模与图大小呈线性关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为高阶图表示学习提供了可扩展的拓扑解决方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited to modeling pairwise interactions, while higher-order models based on cell complexes achieve greater expressivity but often suffer from poor scalability. We introduce simplified and factored cellular Weisfeiler Leman tests (sCWL and fCWL), which preserve the expressivity of the CWL test while improving computational efficiency. We further introduce the maximal clique complex, enabling scalable CWNs with reduced time and memory complexity while retaining strong empirical performance. To avoid explicit clique enumeration, we propose CliqueWalk, a biased random walk that samples maximal cliques and scales linearly with graph size. These contributions yield a scalable topological learning framework for higher-order graph representation.

</details>


### [214] [PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463)
*Ruihang Lai,Hao Kang,Haozhan Tang,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Zichun Yu,Junru Shao,Todd C. Mowry,Chenyan Xiong,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent任务效率（ATE）这一新指标，并构建了面向AI编码智能体的MoE训练框架PithTrain，在保持与生产框架同等吞吐量的同时，显著提升了智能体操作框架的效率（Agent Turns减少62%，Active GPU Time减少64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE训练框架虽然吞吐量高，但针对新架构和系统优化的演进成本高昂，且传统评估仅关注吞吐量，忽略了AI编码智能体操作框架时隐藏的成本。

Method: 基于四项面向智能体的原生设计原则，构建了轻量级MoE训练框架PithTrain，并引入ATE-Bench基准测试，覆盖真实世界的训练框架任务，评估智能体任务效率。

Result: PithTrain在吞吐量上与生产框架持平；在ATE-Bench上，Agent Turns减少62%，Active GPU Time减少64%。

Conclusion: PithTrain证明了面向AI智能体设计的框架可以在不牺牲吞吐性能的前提下，大幅提升智能体的操作与扩展效率，ATE指标为未来训练框架评估提供了新维度。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for frontier language models. To meet this demand, production frameworks have built optimized MoE training stacks over years of engineering effort. Yet evolving these stacks for new architectures and system optimizations remains expensive. With the rise of AI coding agents, they could automate parts of training-framework development and accelerate this evolution. But applying them to these existing frameworks carries hidden costs, invisible to today's throughput-only evaluations. We name this missing dimension agent-task efficiency (ATE): the cost of using coding agents to understand, operate, and extend a framework. Grounded in four agent-native design principles, we build PithTrain, a compact, agent-native MoE training framework. We further introduce ATE-Bench, covering real-world training-framework tasks. Our evaluation shows PithTrain matches the throughput of production frameworks, and on ATE-Bench, PithTrain enables higher agent-task efficiency, with up to 62% fewer Agent Turns and 64% less Active GPU Time.

</details>


### [215] [Best-Arm Identification-Based Trust Region Selection for Bayesian Optimization on Multimodal Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31050)
*Nobuo Namura,Sho Takemori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹感知的贝叶斯优化框架，通过集成最佳臂识别与信任区域方法，在多模态优化中加速全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的贝叶斯优化在复杂多模态或高维问题上性能退化，信任区域方法虽有改进但区域选择效率有限，需要更高效的全局优化策略。

Method: 将最佳臂识别（BAI）与信任区域贝叶斯优化结合，通过外推多个局部优化器的轨迹来预测最终性能，并逐步淘汰次优候选区域。

Result: 理论上证明了在温和假设下所提方法比传统BO更快收敛到全局最优，在合成和真实基准实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: BAI引导的信任区域BO框架能有效提升多模态优化问题的求解效率与全局收敛性。

Abstract: Gaussian process-based Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular approach for expensive black-box optimization, but its performance often degrades on complex multimodal or high-dimensional problems. Trust region-based BO mitigates this issue by focusing on local regions, and recent studies suggest that selecting an effective region can be formulated as a multi-armed bandit problem. We propose a trajectory-aware framework that integrates best-arm identification (BAI) with trust region-based BO to efficiently solve multimodal optimization problems. Our method extrapolates the optimization trajectories of multiple locally initialized optimizers to predict their final performance and progressively eliminates suboptimal candidates via BAI. We theoretically show that the proposed BAI-guided BO converges faster to the global optimum than conventional BO under mild assumptions, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [216] [On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31500)
*Daria Fomina,Daniil Krasylnikov,Alexey Boykov,Andrey Dolgovyazov,Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy,Fedor Velikonivtsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件感知的GNN加速方法，将常见GNN层归为三种内核家族（SpMM卷积、归约聚合、注意力层），并针对每种家族开发了减少数据移动、改善局部性的GPU内核。实验表明，融合注意力内核在Graph Transformer上达到最高3.9倍加速（中位1.6倍），Tensor Core变体在局部密集图上达7.3倍；GATv2最高8.5倍加速（中位2.0倍），峰值内存减少最高76倍（中位6倍）；归约内核最高10倍加速（中位2.6倍）；SpMM层通过缓存cuSPARSE最高达8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN框架（如DGL和PyTorch Geometric）虽然支持通用消息传递，但复杂层常物化边级中间结果，导致内存流量增加，在大规模图上可扩展性受限。本文从I/O和算术强度角度出发，旨在解决GNN中稀疏、不规则内存访问带来的性能瓶颈。

Method: 将常见GNN层分类为三种内核家族：SpMM卷积、归约聚合和注意力层（GATv2/Graph Transformer）。针对每种家族设计减少数据移动、改善局部性的GPU内核，并研究了图重排序对不同内核映射的影响。

Result: 融合注意力内核在Graph Transformer上最高3.9倍加速（中位1.6倍），Tensor Core变体在局部密集图上达7.3倍；GATv2最高8.5倍加速（中位2.0倍），峰值内存减少最高76倍（中位6倍）；归约内核最高10倍加速（中位2.6倍）；SpMM层通过缓存cuSPARSE最高达8倍加速。

Conclusion: 本文开发的硬件感知GPU内核能显著加速GNN推理，减少内存占用，并作为即插即用替代方案发布，支持可复现的GNN加速。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.

</details>


### [217] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReuseRL提出了一种基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的强化学习方法，通过从成功轨迹中提取可复用的技能字典并添加分割代价惩罚，使语言模型智能体学习更结构化的行为模式，从而提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RL训练的语言模型智能体容易学习脆弱、任务特定的捷径行为，导致泛化能力差。作者假设如果成功轨迹在结构上是可压缩的（即可分解为一小组可复用的抽象模式），智能体的泛化能力会更好。

Method: ReuseRL将智能体RL建立在MDL原则基础上：1) 从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典；2) 在RL目标函数中增加分割代价，显式惩罚编码效率低的 idiosyncratic 行为；3) 提供了该压缩惩罚的PAC-Bayes泛化界证明。

Result: 在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个环境中，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外的成功率上均优于vanilla GRPO及强baseline方法。

Conclusion: 通过压缩惩罚鼓励智能体学习可复用的结构化行为模式，能够有效提升RL智能体的泛化能力，避免过拟合到任务特定的捷径上。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [218] [Learning to Bid in FCR Markets: A Best-of-Both-Worlds Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31070)
*Marius Potfer,Cheng Wan,Pierre Gruet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对欧洲频率恢复储备（FCR）市场的竞价问题，将多国FCR清算问题转化为重复的多单位统一价格拍卖，并采用Best-of-Both-Worlds组合半强盗算法，在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下实现O(√T)遗憾，实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCR市场中竞价者的竞争报价是隐藏的，只能观察到清算价格和成交数量等部分反馈，导致竞价决策困难。现有方法缺乏理论保证，需要一种既能适应市场不确定性又具有理论保障的竞价策略。

Method: 将多国FCR清算问题重新表述为重复的多单位统一价格拍卖，通过引入内生对手报价向量，转化为在线学习问题。采用Best-of-Both-Worlds组合半强盗算法，仅利用标准市场反馈（清算价格和成交数量）进行学习与竞价。

Result: 在随机环境中实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境中实现O(√T)遗憾。合成实验验证了理论预期的缩放行为，历史欧洲FCR数据回测显示该方法在稳定产品上表现优异，而EXP3类基线在强非平稳环境下更安全。

Conclusion: 基于学习的FCR市场竞价方法在理论上是有依据的，当学习规则与产品层面的市场稳定性匹配时，在实践中是有效的。

Abstract: Bidding in the European Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) market is challenging for flexibility providers because competing offers are hidden and bidders observe only partial feedback form the market, such as, clearing price and awarded quantity. For a participant active in a single country, we show that the multi-country FCR clearing problem can be recast as a repeated multi-unit uniform-price auction against an endogenous vector of opposing bids. This reformulation yields an online learning problem and allows us to adapt a Best-of-Both-Worlds combinatorial semi-bandit algorithm implementable from this standard market feedback. The resulting bidder achieves logarithmic pseudo-regret in stochastic environments and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret in adversarial ones. Synthetic experiments confirm the expected scaling, and backtests on historical European FCR data show competitive performance in practice: the method performs especially well on stable products, while EXP3-type baselines can be safer under stronger non-stationarity. Overall, the results show that learning-based bidding in FCR markets is theoretically grounded and practically useful when the learning rule matches product-level market stability.

</details>


### [219] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SD-JEPA将JEPA潜空间分解为正交的进展子空间（低维，余弦三元组损失）和内容子空间（高维，SIGReg），通过证明两种抗坍缩力作用在不相交维度上实现加法组合，在控制基准上超越LeWM基线，且1D角度进展坐标可作为场景感知罗盘，在惊喜检测和任务进度定位上优于标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA学习紧凑潜在世界模型，但潜空间中没有指定任一坐标来编码任务进展，缺乏对任务进度的显式表征。

Method: 将JEPA潜空间分解为正交的两个子空间：低维进展子空间（余弦间隔三元组损失塑造）和高维内容子空间（SIGReg正则化）；证明两种抗坍缩力作用在不相交维度上，实现加法组合而非竞争。

Result: SD-JEPA在大多数控制基准上超越LeWM基线；在Push-T上超越最强非LeWM JEPA基线；1D角度进展坐标在40个held-out立方体情节上，|Δθ_t|的AUROC比标准潜预测误差惊喜高0.18；跨4个环境的线性探针显示8维进展子空间（占潜空间4.2%）解释72-95%的任务进度方差。

Conclusion: 进展子空间可作为场景感知罗盘：随任务进展推进，回溯时倒退，受扰动时重定位到语义合适的任务阶段扇区，区分惊喜时刻与其语义含义，这是预测误差标量无法做到的。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|Δθ_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>


### [220] [Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31558)
*Felipe Urrutia,Juan José Alegría,Cinthia Sanchez Macias,Jorge Salas,Cristian B. Calderon,Cristobal Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究解码器-only Transformer（GPT-J）在多跳推理任务中注意力头的学习动态，发现成功学习伴随着纯注意力头（位置型或符号型）的出现，并揭示了两类机制在长序列外推能力上的定量差异——符号机制更可靠，位置机制面临严重限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型已广泛部署，理解其在结构化任务中的求解机制以及在新场景中的行为对于安全部署至关重要。

Method: 在两种结构等价的多跳推理任务（数字位置推理和字母符号推理）上训练GPT-J，利用最新度量方法将注意力头分类为位置型或符号型，并通过理论构造展示单层RoPE注意力如何实现这些功能，提出"差异度"概念来量化两种机制在长序列鲁棒性上的分离。

Result: 成功学习伴随着纯注意力头的涌现；数字任务需要位置型和符号型头，而字母任务只需要符号型头；符号机制在长序列外推中更可靠，位置机制面临更尖锐的限制。

Conclusion: 位置型和符号型注意力机制存在定量上的分离，符号机制具有更好的长序列外推能力，这对理解Transformer的泛化行为和安全部署具有指导意义。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widespread in today's society. As such, understanding the mechanisms by which they solve structured tasks and predicting how they may behave in novel scenarios is of great importance for safe deployment. We study the learning dynamics of attention heads in a controlled setting by training a decoder-only Transformer (GPT-J) on two structurally equivalent multi-hop reasoning tasks: a number task requiring positional reasoning and a letter task requiring symbolic reasoning. Using a recently introduced metric that classifies attention-head behavior as positional or symbolic for a given prompt, we show that successful learning is associated with the emergence of pure heads, i.e., heads that express themselves as either positional or symbolic. Despite the tasks' structural equivalence, they impose different mechanistic demands: the number task requires both positional and symbolic heads, whereas the letter task requires only symbolic heads. We then identify the computational roles of these heads, characterize the basic functions they implement, and give theoretical constructions showing how single-layer RoPE-based attention can realize these functions through geometrically interpretable query, key, and value operations. This analysis yields a quantitative separation between positional and symbolic mechanisms in their robustness to longer sequences, formalized through a novel notion of discrepancy. We empirically validate the resulting predictions in both controlled and real-world models, showing that symbolic mechanisms extrapolate more reliably to longer sequences while positional mechanisms face sharper limitations.

</details>


### [221] [Generalizing Multi-Scale Time-Series Modeling with a Single Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31129)
*Cheonwoo Lee,Dooho Lee,Doyun Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SiGMA（单一广义多尺度架构），通过可学习离散高斯（LDG）核实现距离感知缩放，克服了现有多尺度时序预测方法依赖固定离散缩放的局限性，在长期和短期预测任务上均取得最优结果，同时训练速度提升最高5.3倍，内存消耗减少最高3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中多尺度建模缺乏理论基础，现有方法依赖固定且离散的缩放方式，存在根本性局限。

Method: 将现有缩放方法统一为缩放算子族，提出SiGMA架构，基于尺度空间理论设计可学习离散高斯（LDG）核，实现距离感知缩放。

Result: 在长期预测16个设置中13个取得最佳；训练速度提升最高5.3倍，内存消耗减少最高3.8倍。

Conclusion: SiGMA通过可学习的距离感知缩放机制，在准确性和效率上均显著超越现有多尺度基线方法。

Abstract: Multi-scale modeling has emerged as an effective design principle for time-series forecasting by capturing temporal dynamics at multiple resolutions. As no principled foundation has been established in the literature, we unify existing scaling methods into a scaling operator family, revealing a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: reliance on fixed and discrete scaling. To address this limitation, we propose SiGMA (Single Generalized Multi-scale Architecture), which enables distance-aware scaling via the learnable discrete Gaussian (LDG) kernel grounded in scale-space theory. We evaluate SiGMA comprehensively on long- and short-term forecasting benchmarks against state-of-the-art multi-scale baselines. SiGMA outperforms all competitors on both tasks, especially achieving the best performance in 13 out of 16 long-term evaluation settings. Beyond accuracy, SiGMA significantly improves training speed by up to 5.3 times and reduces memory consumption by up to 3.8 times over the strongest competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/cheonwoolee/SiGMA.

</details>


### [222] [Learning Hyperspherical Time-Frequency Representations for Time-Series Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31155)
*Willian T. Lunardi,Samridha Shrestha,Martin Andreoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超球面嵌入的时域-频域联合表示学习方法，用于时间序列的分布外（OOD）检测，在UCR和UEA数据集上取得了优于对比学习和事后处理基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列的OOD检测在领域内研究不足，缺乏对监督时间序列表示如何用于可靠分布外检测的系统性理解。

Method: 采用von Mises-Fisher (vMF) 似然目标在单位超球面上学习类别条件结构，结合时域和频域编码器将输入信号映射到联合嵌入空间，使用k近邻和马氏距离进行OOD检测。

Result: 在完整的UCR和UEA时间序列档案上，该方法在k-NN和马氏距离评分下均一致优于强对比学习基线和事后处理方法。

Conclusion: 超球面嵌入结合时频域联合表示能有效提升时间序列OOD检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for time-series data remains comparatively underexplored compared to vision and language, with a limited principled understanding of how supervised time-series representations can be leveraged for reliable detection under distributional shifts. This work formulates time-series OOD detection as representation learning with hyperspherical embeddings, where class-conditional structure is induced by a von Mises-Fisher (vMF) likelihood-based objective on the unit sphere. The learned representation combines time- and frequency-domain views of the input signal via domain-specific encoders, integrating them into a joint embedding space for OOD detection. Detection uses distance-based scores over the learned embeddings, including k-nearest neighbors (k-NN) and Mahalanobis scores. We evaluate the approach at scale on the complete UCR and UEA time-series archives under a cross-dataset protocol. Empirical results show consistent improvements under both k-NN and Mahalanobis scoring over strong contrastive learning and post-hoc baselines in the same setting. Code is available at https://github.com/tiiuae/hypertf-time-series-ood.

</details>


### [223] [TabCausal: Pretraining Across Causal Environments for Tabular Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31156)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabCausal是一个数据驱动的因果发现基础模型，通过广泛的因果预训练（涵盖多种图先验、结构机制、噪声模型等）和动态任务构建策略，实现了从观测和混合干预数据中高效、可迁移的因果结构学习，在合成和语义因果环境基准上均取得了优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现基础模型（CDFMs）性能有限，常无法稳定匹配经典方法，关键在于因果预训练任务的构建方式存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出TabCausal，采用动态任务构建策略，在多样化图先验、结构机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预机制上进行广泛因果预训练。

Result: 在大型合成基准上，TabCausal的宏平均性能优于多种因果发现基线；同时引入语义因果环境基准，验证了其在干预证据下的鲁棒结构恢复能力。

Conclusion: 广泛的因果预训练是实现可迁移的摊销式因果发现的关键因素，尤其在干预数据场景下表现突出。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover directed causal relations from observational and interventional data, providing a basis for mechanistic understanding and reliable decision-making. Causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) seek to amortize this problem by mapping a dataset directly to a causal graph in a single forward pass, avoiding per-dataset testing, search, or optimization. However, existing CDFMs remain limited, often failing to consistently match strong classical methods, and we find that a key bottleneck is how causal pretraining tasks are constructed. Based on this observation, we propose TabCausal, a data-driven CDFM trained with broad causal pretraining over diverse graph priors, structural mechanisms, noise models, dimensions, sample sizes, and intervention regimes. A dynamic task construction strategy composes these causal environments into varied discovery tasks, enabling more transferable structural learning from observational and mixed-interventional data. On large-scale synthetic benchmarks, TabCausal achieves better macro-averaged performance than a diverse set of causal discovery baselines. To further bridge abstract synthetic generators and realistic causal reasoning scenarios, we introduce a protocol-guided and LLM-audited semantic causal environment benchmark, where domain-grounded SCMs generate interpretable observational and interventional datasets for out-of-distribution analysis. Across both synthetic and semantic environments, TabCausal demonstrates robust structure recovery, especially under interventional evidence, highlighting broad causal pretraining as a key ingredient for transferable amortized causal discovery.

</details>


### [224] [Convergence of Two-Timescale Markovian Stochastic Approximations with Applications in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31172)
*Vagul Mahadevan,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了两时间尺度随机逼近（SA）在马尔可夫噪声下的稳定性与收敛性，无需投影算子或噪声紧致性假设，并首次证明了带资格迹的TDC算法在离线策略学习下的几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前两时间尺度SA的稳定性与收敛性仅在独立同分布噪声下被证明，而强化学习中实际更常见的是马尔可夫噪声，因此需要建立更 realistic 的理论保证。

Method: 关键创新在于用慢时间尺度参数的运行最大值来控制快时间尺度参数，而非像以往工作那样使用当前慢时间尺度参数。

Result: 成功建立了马尔可夫噪声下两时间尺度SA的稳定性与收敛性，无需投影算子且噪声无需定义在紧致空间上；首次证明了带资格迹的TDC算法在离线策略线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为强化学习中更实际的噪声模型提供了理论保证，拓展了两时间尺度SA的应用范围。

Abstract: This work studies the convergence of two-timescale stochastic approximations (SA), a class of iterative algorithms that update two sets of parameters in fast and slow timescales respectively. Notable examples of two-timescale SA in reinforcement learning (RL) include temporal difference learning with gradient correction (TDC) and actor-critic methods. Previously, the stability (i.e., boundedness) and convergence of two-timescale SA were only established under i.i.d. noise. This work instead establishes the stability and convergence of two-timescale SA under Markovian noise, a setup that is more realistic in RL. Notably, we do not need to use any projection operator and the noise does not need to live in a compact space. Our key technical novelty is to control the fast timescale parameter with the running max of the slow timescale parameter, instead of with the current slow timescale parameter, as most prior works do. As a key application, we establish the first almost sure convergence of TDC with eligibility traces under off-policy learning with linear function approximation.

</details>


### [225] [Retriever Portfolios: A Principled Approach to Adaptive RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31176)
*Miltiadis Stouras,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Silvio Lattanzi,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自动选择少量多样检索器组合（投资组合）的方法，以覆盖不同查询分布区域，在多个QA基准上优于单一检索器和朴素多检索器基线，且延迟和成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常使用单一检索器和固定超参数，但实际查询高度异构（从简单事实到复杂多跳推理），单一检索器难以覆盖所有查询类型。

Method: 从候选池中自动选择少量多样检索器构成投资组合，通过期望best-of-k目标形式化问题，并设计具有近最优保证的高效构建算法。

Result: 在多个QA基准上，学习到的投资组合和路由管线在检索指标和答案质量上均优于单一检索器和朴素多检索器基线；与推理时超参调优相比，固定投资组合在相当或更好的准确率下，延迟和token成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 固定检索器投资组合能有效覆盖异构查询分布，兼顾准确率与效率，是一种实用的RAG优化策略。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rely on a single retriever and a single set of hyperparameters, despite facing highly heterogeneous queries that range from simple factoid questions to complex multi-hop reasoning. We propose a method that automatically selects a small, diverse subset of retrievers (a portfolio) from a large pool of candidates, to cover different regions of the target query distribution. We formalize this setting via an expected best-of-$k$ objective over the query distribution and show that it admits an efficient portfolio construction algorithm with near-optimal guarantees. Across multiple QA benchmarks, our learned portfolios and router pipeline consistently outperform single-retriever and naive multi-retriever baselines on both retrieval metrics and answer quality. In addition, compared to inference-time hyperparameter tuning approaches, fixed portfolios enable parallel retrieval and LLM calls, achieving comparable (and sometimes better) accuracy with substantially lower latency and token cost.

</details>


### [226] [How well does Classification Accuracy capture Concept Drift Detection Quality? An overview of Concept Drift Detection evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31186)
*Joanna Komorniczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了合成非平稳数据流中漂移检测质量指标与分类性能之间的关系，通过七种数据生成工具和八种漂移检测质量指标，旨在建立统一的漂移检测评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前学术界缺乏统一的漂移检测评估框架，现有研究常依赖分类质量指标，但这些指标受多种因素影响，不能可靠反映漂移检测的真实质量。

Method: 研究在七种合成非平稳数据流生成工具上，分析八种漂移检测质量指标与分类器性能的关联，并将漂移动态作为影响因素纳入考量。

Result: 识别出最具信息量的漂移检测质量指标集，并深入理解了评估方法的选择机制。

Conclusion: 提出了一个更可靠、更全面的漂移检测评估框架，为社区统一评估标准提供了方向。

Abstract: Data streams are nowadays among the most frequently analyzed data structures, with the concept drift posing a major challenge encountered by processing systems. Despite the proposition of numerous solutions to counteract the accuracy degeneration due to concept drift, the scientific community has not yet established a unified framework for evaluating the concept drift detection task. Existing research often relies on classification quality metrics, but these can be affected by multiple factors and may not reliably reflect drift detection quality. In this work, we present an in-depth overview of the relationship between metrics for quantifying drift detection quality and classification performance in synthetic nonstationary data streams. The proposed research studies eight drift detection quality metrics in relation to the classifier's performance across seven synthetic data stream generation tools, additionally considering drift dynamics as a factor. The studies aim to identify the most informative set of drift detection quality metrics and provide a deep understanding of the method's evaluation.

</details>


### [227] [FlagGAM: Rule-Based Generalized Additive Modeling for Explainable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31189)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlagGAM是一种基于规则定义基函数的框架，通过分离特征级规则构建与预测，在保持可解释性的同时提升表格数据的预测准确性，并且对缺失和噪声输入具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域的表格预测需要模型兼具准确性、透明性和对不完美输入的鲁棒性，而现有方法难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出FlagGAM框架，包含Flag核心模块（将数值/类别变量转换为稀疏、可读的单变量基函数，如阈值标志、类别标志、尾偏离基和类别阶梯函数）和默认加法头（以受限GAM风格组合这些基函数），保留稀疏规则基矩阵而非压缩为计数摘要。

Result: 默认FlagGAM在透明加法模式下接近EBM性能，在混合类型回归上显著优于岭回归；在缺失和噪声扰动下AUROC下降幅度小于常见基线。灵活预测头进一步提升了准确性，接近强树基线。

Conclusion: FlagGAM为需要竞争性准确性、可沟通规则和对不完美输入鲁棒性的表格数据场景提供了实用的中间方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction in high-stakes domains requires models that are accurate, transparent, and robust to imperfect inputs. We propose FlagGAM, a rule-defined basis framework that separates feature-level rule construction from prediction. A Flag Core Module converts numerical and categorical variables into sparse, human-readable univariate bases, including threshold flags, category-level flags, tail-deviation bases, and categorical step functions; a default additive head then combines these bases as a restricted GAM-style predictor. Rather than reducing triggered rules to compact count summaries, FlagGAM retains a sparse rule-basis matrix that supports mixed-type classification and regression, feature-specific weighting, and optional flexible prediction heads. Across tabular benchmarks, default FlagGAM remains close to EBM in transparent additive mode, improves substantially over ridge regression on mixed-type regression, and shows smaller AUROC degradation than common baselines under missing and noisy perturbations. Flexible heads further improve accuracy and approach strong tree-based baselines, with the caveat that the resulting model should be interpreted as a rule-basis representation followed by a nonlinear predictor rather than as a fully additive GAM. Overall, FlagGAM provides a practical middle ground for tabular settings that require competitive accuracy, communicable rules, and robustness to imperfect inputs.

</details>
